# I Chronicles

דברי הימים א

Link: [https://torahapp.org/share/book/I%20Chronicles](https://torahapp.org/share/book/I%20Chronicles)

חזרה על אירועים שכבר סופרו בתורה ובנביאים ראשונים, בדגש על קורותיו של דוד המלך.

Recounts of events in the Torah and early Prophets, focusing on King David.

Sections: Chapter-Verse

Location: Tanakh/Writings

Originally from [Sefaria](https://www.sefaria.org) via [TorahLM.org](https://torahlm.org) — see [licensing](https://torahlm.org/sefaria_license).

Version (He): Miqra according to the Masorah (Segments: 943, License: CC-BY-SA)

Versions (En):
  - The Koren Jerusalem Bible (Segments: 943, License: CC-BY-NC)
  - The Rashi Ketuvim by Rabbi Shraga Silverstein (Segments: 942, License: CC-BY)


## Chapter 1

###### I Chronicles 1:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/I%20Chronicles/r/1:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/I%20Chronicles/r/1:1)


אָדָ֥ם שֵׁ֖ת אֱנֽוֹשׁ׃


**The Koren Jerusalem Bible:**
Adam, Shet, Enosh,


**The Rashi Ketuvim by Rabbi Shraga Silverstein:**
[The son of] Adam: Sheth, [and the son of Sheth:] Enosh.


###### I Chronicles 1:2

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/I%20Chronicles/r/1:2](https://torahapp.org/share/book/I%20Chronicles/r/1:2)


קֵינָ֥ן מַהֲלַלְאֵ֖ל יָֽרֶד׃


**The Koren Jerusalem Bible:**
Qenan, Mahalal᾽el, Yered,


**The Rashi Ketuvim by Rabbi Shraga Silverstein:**
[And the son of Enosh:] Kenan, [and the son of Kenan:] Mahalalel, [and the son of Mahalalel:] Yared.


###### I Chronicles 1:3

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/I%20Chronicles/r/1:3](https://torahapp.org/share/book/I%20Chronicles/r/1:3)


חֲנ֥וֹךְ מְתוּשֶׁ֖לַח לָֽמֶךְ׃


**The Koren Jerusalem Bible:**
Ḥanokh, Metushelaĥ, Lemekh,


**The Rashi Ketuvim by Rabbi Shraga Silverstein:**
[And the son of Yared:] Chanoch, [and the son of Chanoch:] Methuselah, [and the son of Methuselah:] Lamech.


###### I Chronicles 1:4

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/I%20Chronicles/r/1:4](https://torahapp.org/share/book/I%20Chronicles/r/1:4)


נֹ֥חַ שֵׁ֖ם חָ֥ם וָיָֽפֶת׃{ס}


**The Koren Jerusalem Bible:**
Noaĥ, Shem, Ḥam, and Yefet.


**The Rashi Ketuvim by Rabbi Shraga Silverstein:**
[And the son of Lamech:] Noach, [and the sons of Noach:] Shem, Cham, and Yafeth.


###### I Chronicles 1:5

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/I%20Chronicles/r/1:5](https://torahapp.org/share/book/I%20Chronicles/r/1:5)


בְּנֵ֣י יֶ֔פֶת גֹּ֣מֶר וּמָג֔וֹג וּמָדַ֖י וְיָוָ֣ן וְתֻבָ֑ל וּמֶ֖שֶׁךְ וְתִירָֽס׃{ס}


**The Koren Jerusalem Bible:**
The sons of Yefet; Gomer, and Magog, and Maday, and Yavan, and Tuval, and Meshekh, and Tiras.


**The Rashi Ketuvim by Rabbi Shraga Silverstein:**
[And the sons of Yefeth:] Gomer, Magog, Maddai, Yavan, Tuval, Meshech, and Tiras.


###### I Chronicles 1:6

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/I%20Chronicles/r/1:6](https://torahapp.org/share/book/I%20Chronicles/r/1:6)


וּבְנֵ֖י גֹּ֑מֶר אַשְׁכְּנַ֥ז וְדִיפַ֖ת וְתוֹגַרְמָֽה׃{ס}


**The Koren Jerusalem Bible:**
And the sons of Gommer; Ashkenaz, and Difat, and Togarma.


**The Rashi Ketuvim by Rabbi Shraga Silverstein:**
And the sons of Gomer: Ashkenaz, Difath, and Togarmah.

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/I%20Chronicles/r/1:7](https://torahapp.org/share/book/I%20Chronicles/r/1:7)


וּבְנֵ֥י יָוָ֖ן אֱלִישָׁ֣ה וְתַרְשִׁ֑ישָׁה כִּתִּ֖ים וְרוֹדָנִֽים׃{ס}


**The Koren Jerusalem Bible:**
And the sons of Yavan; Elisha, and Tarshisha, Kittim, and Rodanim.


**The Rashi Ketuvim by Rabbi Shraga Silverstein:**
And the sons of Yavan: Elishah, Tarshishah, Kittim, and Rodanim.


###### I Chronicles 1:8

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/I%20Chronicles/r/1:8](https://torahapp.org/share/book/I%20Chronicles/r/1:8)


בְּנֵ֖י חָ֑ם כּ֥וּשׁ וּמִצְרַ֖יִם פּ֥וּט וּכְנָֽעַן׃


**The Koren Jerusalem Bible:**
The sons of Ḥam; Kush, and Miżrayim, Put, and Kena῾an.


**The Rashi Ketuvim by Rabbi Shraga Silverstein:**
The sons of Cham: Kush, Mitzrayim, Put, and Canaan.


###### I Chronicles 1:9

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/I%20Chronicles/r/1:9](https://torahapp.org/share/book/I%20Chronicles/r/1:9)


וּבְנֵ֣י כ֔וּשׁ סְבָא֙ וַֽחֲוִילָ֔ה וְסַבְתָּ֥א וְרַעְמָ֖א וְסַבְתְּכָ֑א וּבְנֵ֥י רַעְמָ֖א שְׁבָ֥א וּדְדָֽן׃{ס}


**The Koren Jerusalem Bible:**
And the sons of Kush; Seva, and Ḥavila, and Savta, and Ra῾ma, and Savtekha. And the sons of Ra῾ma; Sheva, and Dedan.


**The Rashi Ketuvim by Rabbi Shraga Silverstein:**
And the sons of Kush: Seva, Chavilah, Savtah, Ra'amah, and Savtecha. And the sons of Ra'amah: Sheva and Dedan.


###### I Chronicles 1:10

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/I%20Chronicles/r/1:10](https://torahapp.org/share/book/I%20Chronicles/r/1:10)


וְכ֖וּשׁ יָלַ֣ד אֶת־נִמְר֑וֹד ה֣וּא הֵחֵ֔ל לִֽהְי֥וֹת גִּבּ֖וֹר בָּאָֽרֶץ׃{ס}


**The Koren Jerusalem Bible:**
And Kush begot Nimrod: he began to be mighty upon the earth.


**The Rashi Ketuvim by Rabbi Shraga Silverstein:**
And Kush begot Nimrod. He began to be a mighty one in the earth [to cause all to rebel (nimrod = "Let us rebel") against the Holy One Blessed be He (in the counsel of the Tower of Babel)].


###### I Chronicles 1:11

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/I%20Chronicles/r/1:11](https://torahapp.org/share/book/I%20Chronicles/r/1:11)


וּמִצְרַ֡יִם יָלַ֞ד אֶת־[לוּדִ֧ים] (לודיים) וְאֶת־עֲנָמִ֛ים וְאֶת־לְהָבִ֖ים וְאֶת־נַפְתֻּחִֽים׃


**The Koren Jerusalem Bible:**
And Miżrayim begot Ludim, and ῾Anamim, and Lehavim, and Naftuĥim,


**The Rashi Ketuvim by Rabbi Shraga Silverstein:**
And Mitzrayim begot Ludim and Anamim and Lehavim and Naftuchim.


###### I Chronicles 1:12

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/I%20Chronicles/r/1:12](https://torahapp.org/share/book/I%20Chronicles/r/1:12)


וְֽאֶת־פַּתְרֻסִ֞ים וְאֶת־כַּסְלֻחִ֗ים אֲשֶׁ֨ר יָצְא֥וּ מִשָּׁ֛ם פְּלִשְׁתִּ֖ים וְאֶת־כַּפְתֹּרִֽים׃{ס}


**The Koren Jerusalem Bible:**
and Patrusim, and Kasluĥim, (of whom came to the Pelishtim,) and Kaftorim.


**The Rashi Ketuvim by Rabbi Shraga Silverstein:**
And Pathrusim and Kasluchim, whence came forth Plishtim and Kaftorim [(Pathrusim and Kasluchim "exchanging" their wives, and "between both" begetting Plishtim and Kaftorim)].


###### I Chronicles 1:13

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/I%20Chronicles/r/1:13](https://torahapp.org/share/book/I%20Chronicles/r/1:13)


וּכְנַ֗עַן יָלַ֛ד אֶת־צִיד֥וֹן בְּכֹר֖וֹ וְאֶת־חֵֽת׃


**The Koren Jerusalem Bible:**
And Kena῾an begot Żidon his firstborn, and Ḥet,


**The Rashi Ketuvim by Rabbi Shraga Silverstein:**
And Canaan begot Tziddon, his first-born, and Cheth.

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/I%20Chronicles/r/1:14](https://torahapp.org/share/book/I%20Chronicles/r/1:14)


וְאֶת־הַיְבוּסִי֙ וְאֶת־הָ֣אֱמֹרִ֔י וְאֵ֖ת הַגִּרְגָּשִֽׁי׃


**The Koren Jerusalem Bible:**
and the Yevusi, and the Emori, and the Girgashi,


**The Rashi Ketuvim by Rabbi Shraga Silverstein:**
And the Yevussi and the Emori and the Girgashi.

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/I%20Chronicles/r/1:15](https://torahapp.org/share/book/I%20Chronicles/r/1:15)


וְאֶת־הַחִוִּ֥י וְאֶת־הַעַרְקִ֖י וְאֶת־הַסִּינִֽי׃


**The Koren Jerusalem Bible:**
and the Ḥivvi and the Arqi, and the Sini,


**The Rashi Ketuvim by Rabbi Shraga Silverstein:**
And the Chivi and the Arki and the Sini.

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/I%20Chronicles/r/1:16](https://torahapp.org/share/book/I%20Chronicles/r/1:16)


וְאֶת־הָאַרְוָדִ֥י וְאֶת־הַצְּמָרִ֖י וְאֶת־הַחֲמָתִֽי׃{ס}


**The Koren Jerusalem Bible:**
and the Arvadi, and the Żemari, and the Ḥamati.


**The Rashi Ketuvim by Rabbi Shraga Silverstein:**
And the Arvadi and the Tzemari and the Chamathi.


###### I Chronicles 1:17

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/I%20Chronicles/r/1:17](https://torahapp.org/share/book/I%20Chronicles/r/1:17)


בְּנֵ֣י שֵׁ֔ם עֵילָ֣ם וְאַשּׁ֔וּר וְאַרְפַּכְשַׁ֖ד וְל֣וּד וַאֲרָ֑ם וְע֥וּץ וְח֖וּל וְגֶ֥תֶר וָמֶֽשֶׁךְ׃{ס}


**The Koren Jerusalem Bible:**
The sons of Shem; ῾Elam, and Ashshur, and Arpakhshad, and Lud, and Aram, and ῾Uż, and Ḥul, and Geter, and Meshekh.


**The Rashi Ketuvim by Rabbi Shraga Silverstein:**
The sons of Shem: Eilam, Ashur, Arpachshad, Lud, and Aram. [And the sons of Aram:] Utz, Chul, Gether, and Meshech.


###### I Chronicles 1:18

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/I%20Chronicles/r/1:18](https://torahapp.org/share/book/I%20Chronicles/r/1:18)


וְאַרְפַּכְשַׁ֖ד יָלַ֣ד אֶת־שָׁ֑לַח וְשֶׁ֖לַח יָלַ֥ד אֶת־עֵֽבֶר׃


**The Koren Jerusalem Bible:**
And Arpakhshad begot Shelaĥ, and Shelaĥ begot ῾Ever.


**The Rashi Ketuvim by Rabbi Shraga Silverstein:**
And Arpachshad begot Shalach, and Shelach begot Ever.


###### I Chronicles 1:19

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/I%20Chronicles/r/1:19](https://torahapp.org/share/book/I%20Chronicles/r/1:19)


וּלְעֵ֥בֶר יֻלַּ֖ד שְׁנֵ֣י בָנִ֑ים שֵׁ֣ם הָאֶחָ֞ד פֶּ֗לֶג כִּ֤י בְיָמָיו֙ נִפְלְגָ֣ה הָאָ֔רֶץ וְשֵׁ֥ם אָחִ֖יו יׇקְטָֽן׃


**The Koren Jerusalem Bible:**
And to ῾Ever were born two sons: the name of the one was Peleg; because in his days the earth was divided: and his brother’s name was Yoqtan.


**The Rashi Ketuvim by Rabbi Shraga Silverstein:**
And to Ever was born two sons; the name of the first Peleg, for in his days the earth was divided (niflegah) [Languages were confounded and men "divided themselves" throughout the earth], and the name of his brother was Yaktan. [(Because he "humbled" (hiktin) himself, he merited the many families (which follow).]


###### I Chronicles 1:20

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/I%20Chronicles/r/1:20](https://torahapp.org/share/book/I%20Chronicles/r/1:20)


וְיׇקְטָ֣ן יָלַ֔ד אֶת־אַלְמוֹדָ֖ד וְאֶת־שָׁ֑לֶף וְאֶת־חֲצַרְמָ֖וֶת וְאֶת־יָֽרַח׃

**The Rashi Ketuvim by Rabbi Shraga Silverstein:**

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/I%20Chronicles/r/1:21](https://torahapp.org/share/book/I%20Chronicles/r/1:21)


וְאֶת־הֲדוֹרָ֥ם וְאֶת־אוּזָ֖ל וְאֶת־דִּקְלָֽה׃

and Hadoram, and Uzal, and Diqla,


**The Rashi Ketuvim by Rabbi Shraga Silverstein:**

###### I Chronicles 1:22

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/I%20Chronicles/r/1:22](https://torahapp.org/share/book/I%20Chronicles/r/1:22)


וְאֶת־עֵיבָ֥ל וְאֶת־אֲבִֽימָאֵ֖ל וְאֶת־שְׁבָֽא׃


**The Koren Jerusalem Bible:**
and ῾Eval, and Avima᾽el, and Sheva,


**The Rashi Ketuvim by Rabbi Shraga Silverstein:**
And Eival and Avimael and Sheva.


###### I Chronicles 1:23

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/I%20Chronicles/r/1:23](https://torahapp.org/share/book/I%20Chronicles/r/1:23)


וְאֶת־אוֹפִ֥יר וְאֶת־חֲוִילָ֖ה וְאֶת־יוֹבָ֑ב כׇּל־אֵ֖לֶּה בְּנֵ֥י יׇקְטָֽן׃{ס}

and Ofir, and Ḥavila, and Yovav. All these were the sons of Yoqtan.


**The Rashi Ketuvim by Rabbi Shraga Silverstein:**

**The Koren Jerusalem Bible:**
Shem, Arpakhshad, Shelaĥ,


**The Rashi Ketuvim by Rabbi Shraga Silverstein:**
Shem, Arpachshad, Shalach,

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/I%20Chronicles/r/1:25](https://torahapp.org/share/book/I%20Chronicles/r/1:25)


עֵ֥בֶר פֶּ֖לֶג רְעֽוּ׃

**The Rashi Ketuvim by Rabbi Shraga Silverstein:**
Ever, Peleg, Re'u,

Serug, Naĥor, Teraĥ,


**The Rashi Ketuvim by Rabbi Shraga Silverstein:**
Serug, Nachor, Tarach,


###### I Chronicles 1:27

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/I%20Chronicles/r/1:27](https://torahapp.org/share/book/I%20Chronicles/r/1:27)


אַבְרָ֖ם ה֥וּא אַבְרָהָֽם׃{ס}


**The Koren Jerusalem Bible:**
Avram; the same is Avraham.


**The Rashi Ketuvim by Rabbi Shraga Silverstein:**
Avram — this is Abraham.


###### I Chronicles 1:28

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/I%20Chronicles/r/1:28](https://torahapp.org/share/book/I%20Chronicles/r/1:28)


בְּנֵי֙ אַבְרָהָ֔ם יִצְחָ֖ק וְיִשְׁמָעֵֽאל׃{ס}


**The Koren Jerusalem Bible:**
The sons of Avraham; Yiżĥaq, and Yishma᾽el.


**The Rashi Ketuvim by Rabbi Shraga Silverstein:**
The sons of Abraham: Isaac and Ishmael.


###### I Chronicles 1:29

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/I%20Chronicles/r/1:29](https://torahapp.org/share/book/I%20Chronicles/r/1:29)


אֵ֖לֶּה תֹּלְדוֹתָ֑ם בְּכ֤וֹר יִשְׁמָעֵאל֙ נְבָי֔וֹת וְקֵדָ֥ר וְאַדְבְּאֵ֖ל וּמִבְשָֽׂם׃


**The Koren Jerusalem Bible:**
These are their generations: The firstborn of Yishma᾽el, Nevayot; then Qedar, and Adbe᾽el, and Mivsam,


**The Rashi Ketuvim by Rabbi Shraga Silverstein:**
These are their progeny: the first-born of Ishmael: Nevayoth — and Kedar and Adbe'el and Mivsam,


###### I Chronicles 1:30

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/I%20Chronicles/r/1:30](https://torahapp.org/share/book/I%20Chronicles/r/1:30)


מִשְׁמָ֣ע וְדוּמָ֔ה מַשָּׂ֖א חֲדַ֥ד וְתֵימָֽא׃


**The Koren Jerusalem Bible:**
Mishma, and Duma, Massa, Ḥadad, and Tema,


**The Rashi Ketuvim by Rabbi Shraga Silverstein:**
Mishma and Duma and Massa, Chadad and Tema,


###### I Chronicles 1:31

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/I%20Chronicles/r/1:31](https://torahapp.org/share/book/I%20Chronicles/r/1:31)


יְט֥וּר נָפִ֖ישׁ וָקֵ֑דְמָה אֵ֥לֶּה הֵ֖ם בְּנֵ֥י יִשְׁמָעֵֽאל׃{ס}


**The Koren Jerusalem Bible:**
Yetur, Nafish, and Qedema. These are the sons of Yishma᾽el.


**The Rashi Ketuvim by Rabbi Shraga Silverstein:**
Yetur, Nafish and Kedman — these are the sons of Ishmael.


###### I Chronicles 1:32

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/I%20Chronicles/r/1:32](https://torahapp.org/share/book/I%20Chronicles/r/1:32)


וּבְנֵ֨י קְטוּרָ֜ה פִּילֶ֣גֶשׁ אַבְרָהָ֗ם יָלְדָ֞ה אֶת־זִמְרָ֧ן וְיׇקְשָׁ֛ן וּמְדָ֥ן וּמִדְיָ֖ן וְיִשְׁבָּ֣ק וְשׁ֑וּחַ וּבְנֵ֥י יׇקְשָׁ֖ן שְׁבָ֥א וּדְדָֽן׃{ס}


**The Koren Jerusalem Bible:**
Now the sons of Qetura, Avraham’s concubine: she bore Zimran, and Yoqshan, and Medan, and Midyan, and Yishbaq, and Shuaĥ. And the sons of Yoqshan; Sheva, and Dedan.


**The Rashi Ketuvim by Rabbi Shraga Silverstein:**
And the sons of Keturah, the concubine of Abraham: She bore Zimran and Yakshan and Medan and Midyan and Yishbak and Shuach. And the sons of Yakshan were Sheva and Dedan.


###### I Chronicles 1:33

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/I%20Chronicles/r/1:33](https://torahapp.org/share/book/I%20Chronicles/r/1:33)


וּבְנֵ֣י מִדְיָ֗ן עֵיפָ֤ה וָעֵ֙פֶר֙ וַחֲנ֔וֹךְ וַאֲבִידָ֖ע וְאֶלְדָּעָ֑ה כׇּל־אֵ֖לֶּה בְּנֵ֥י קְטוּרָֽה׃{ס}


**The Koren Jerusalem Bible:**
And the sons of Midyan; ῾Efa, and ῾Efer, and Ḥanokh, and Avida, and Elda῾a. All these are the sons of Qetura.


**The Rashi Ketuvim by Rabbi Shraga Silverstein:**

## Chapter 2

###### I Chronicles 2:1

**The Rashi Ketuvim by Rabbi Shraga Silverstein:**

###### I Chronicles 2:2

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/I%20Chronicles/r/2:2](https://torahapp.org/share/book/I%20Chronicles/r/2:2)

Dan, Yosef, and Binyamin, Naftali, Gad and Asher.


**The Rashi Ketuvim by Rabbi Shraga Silverstein:**

###### I Chronicles 2:3

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/I%20Chronicles/r/2:3](https://torahapp.org/share/book/I%20Chronicles/r/2:3)


בְּנֵ֣י יְהוּדָ֗ה עֵ֤ר וְאוֹנָן֙ וְשֵׁלָ֔ה שְׁלוֹשָׁה֙ נ֣וֹלַד ל֔וֹ מִבַּת־שׁ֖וּעַ הַֽכְּנַעֲנִ֑ית וַיְהִ֞י עֵ֣ר**׀** בְּכ֣וֹר יְהוּדָ֗ה רַ֛ע בְּעֵינֵ֥י יְהֹוָ֖ה וַיְמִיתֵֽהוּ׃{ס}


**The Koren Jerusalem Bible:**
The sons of Yehuda; ῾Er, and Onan, and Shela: which three were born to him of the daughter of Shua the Kena῾anite woman. And ῾Er, the firstborn of Yehuda, was evil in the sight of the Lord; and he slew him.


**The Rashi Ketuvim by Rabbi Shraga Silverstein:**
The sons of Judah: Er and Onan and Shelah. Three were born to him of the daughter of Shua, the Canaanite. And Er, the first-born of Judah was evil in the eyes of the L-rd [viz. Rashi on Genesis 38:7], and He killed him.


###### I Chronicles 2:4

שִׁשָּׁה֙ נוֹלַד־ל֣וֹ בְחֶבְר֔וֹן וַיִּ֨מְלׇךְ־שָׁ֔ם שֶׁ֥בַע שָׁנִ֖ים וְשִׁשָּׁ֣ה חֳדָשִׁ֑ים וּשְׁלֹשִׁ֤ים וְשָׁלוֹשׁ֙ שָׁנָ֔ה מָלַ֖ךְ בִּירוּשָׁלָֽ͏ִם׃{ס}

## Chapter 4

###### I Chronicles 4:1

**The Rashi Ketuvim by Rabbi Shraga Silverstein:**

**The Koren Jerusalem Bible:**

**The Rashi Ketuvim by Rabbi Shraga Silverstein:**

וּפְנוּאֵל֙ אֲבִ֣י גְדֹ֔ר וְעֵ֖זֶר אֲבִ֣י חוּשָׁ֑ה אֵ֤לֶּה בְנֵי־חוּר֙ בְּכ֣וֹר אֶפְרָ֔תָה אֲבִ֖י בֵּ֥ית לָֽחֶם׃


**The Koren Jerusalem Bible:**
and Penu᾽el the father of Gedor, and ῾Eżer the father of Ḥusha. These are the sons of Ḥur, the firstborn of Efrata, the father of Bet-leĥem.


**The Rashi Ketuvim by Rabbi Shraga Silverstein:**

###### I Chronicles 4:5

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/I%20Chronicles/r/4:5](https://torahapp.org/share/book/I%20Chronicles/r/4:5)

**The Rashi Ketuvim by Rabbi Shraga Silverstein:**

**The Rashi Ketuvim by Rabbi Shraga Silverstein:**

**The Rashi Ketuvim by Rabbi Shraga Silverstein:**

**The Koren Jerusalem Bible:**
And Qoż begot ῾Anuv, and Żoveva, and the families of Aĥarĥel the son of Harum.


**The Rashi Ketuvim by Rabbi Shraga Silverstein:**

###### I Chronicles 4:9

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/I%20Chronicles/r/4:9](https://torahapp.org/share/book/I%20Chronicles/r/4:9)


וַיְהִ֣י יַעְבֵּ֔ץ נִכְבָּ֖ד מֵאֶחָ֑יו וְאִמּ֗וֹ קָרְאָ֨ה שְׁמ֤וֹ יַעְבֵּץ֙ לֵאמֹ֔ר כִּ֥י יָלַ֖דְתִּי בְּעֹֽצֶב׃


**The Koren Jerusalem Bible:**

**The Rashi Ketuvim by Rabbi Shraga Silverstein:**

###### I Chronicles 4:10

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/I%20Chronicles/r/4:10](https://torahapp.org/share/book/I%20Chronicles/r/4:10)


וַיִּקְרָ֣א יַ֠עְבֵּ֠ץ לֵאלֹהֵ֨י יִשְׂרָאֵ֜ל לֵאמֹ֗ר אִם־בָּרֵ֨ךְ תְּבָרְכֵ֜נִי וְהִרְבִּ֤יתָ אֶת־גְּבוּלִי֙ וְהָיְתָ֤ה יָדְךָ֙ עִמִּ֔י וְעָשִׂ֥יתָ מֵּרָעָ֖ה לְבִלְתִּ֣י עׇצְבִּ֑י וַיָּבֵ֥א אֱלֹהִ֖ים אֵ֥ת אֲשֶׁר־שָׁאָֽל׃{ס}


**The Koren Jerusalem Bible:**
And Ya῾beż called on the God of Yisra᾽el, saying, Oh that Thou wouldst bless me indeed, and enlarge my border, and that Thy hand might be with me, and that Thou wouldst keep me from harm, that it may not hurt me! And God granted him that which he requested.


**The Rashi Ketuvim by Rabbi Shraga Silverstein:**
And Yabetz called to the G-d of Israel, saying: If You bless me, and expand my border, and Your hand be with me and save (me) from evil, not to sadden me, [(then I will offer, etc.)]. And G-d brought to pass what he had asked.

וּכְל֥וּב אֲחִי־שׁוּחָ֖ה הוֹלִ֣יד אֶת־מְחִ֑יר ה֖וּא אֲבִ֥י אֶשְׁתּֽוֹן׃


**The Koren Jerusalem Bible:**

**The Rashi Ketuvim by Rabbi Shraga Silverstein:**

**The Rashi Ketuvim by Rabbi Shraga Silverstein:**

**The Rashi Ketuvim by Rabbi Shraga Silverstein:**

## Chapter 9

###### I Chronicles 9:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/I%20Chronicles/r/9:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/I%20Chronicles/r/9:1)


וְכׇל־יִשְׂרָאֵל֙ הִתְיַחְשׂ֔וּ וְהִנָּ֣ם כְּתוּבִ֔ים עַל־סֵ֖פֶר מַלְכֵ֣י יִשְׂרָאֵ֑ל וִיהוּדָ֛ה הׇגְל֥וּ לְבָבֶ֖ל בְּמַעֲלָֽם׃{ס}


**The Koren Jerusalem Bible:**
So all Yisra᾽el were reckoned by genealogies; and, behold, they were written in the book of the kings of Yisra᾽el; and Yehuda was carried away to Bavel for their transgression.


**The Rashi Ketuvim by Rabbi Shraga Silverstein:**
And all of Israel [(as opposed to Judah)] traced themselves [(to their fathers' houses)] and they are recorded in the book of the kings of Israel [(which is found with them in exile)], and Judah was exiled to Bavel for its faithlessness, [(their genealogical records with them, from which I ([Ezra, one of the exiles]) have copied what is recorded herein.)]


###### I Chronicles 9:2

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/I%20Chronicles/r/9:2](https://torahapp.org/share/book/I%20Chronicles/r/9:2)


וְהַיּֽוֹשְׁבִים֙ הָרִ֣אשֹׁנִ֔ים אֲשֶׁ֥ר בַּאֲחֻזָּתָ֖ם בְּעָרֵיהֶ֑ם יִשְׂרָאֵל֙ הַכֹּ֣הֲנִ֔ים הַלְוִיִּ֖ם וְהַנְּתִינִֽים׃


**The Koren Jerusalem Bible:**
Now the first inhabitants who dwelt in their possessions in their cities were, the Yisra᾽elites, the priests, Levites, and the temple servants.


**The Rashi Ketuvim by Rabbi Shraga Silverstein:**
And the first settlers [(to return from exile with Zerubavel, settling)] in their holdings in their cities, were the Israelites [i.e., those from all the tribes of Israel who had mixed with Judah)], the priests, the Levites, and the Nethinites [i.e., the Giveonites (viz. Joshua 9:27)].


###### I Chronicles 9:3

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/I%20Chronicles/r/9:3](https://torahapp.org/share/book/I%20Chronicles/r/9:3)

###### I Chronicles 9:27

**The Rashi Ketuvim by Rabbi Shraga Silverstein:**
And roundabout the house of G-d did they reside, the watch being theirs. And they were (appointed) over the opening (of the gates) each morning.

## Chapter 10

###### I Chronicles 10:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/I%20Chronicles/r/10:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/I%20Chronicles/r/10:1)


וּפְלִשְׁתִּ֖ים נִלְחֲמ֣וּ בְיִשְׂרָאֵ֑ל וַיָּ֤נׇס אִֽישׁ־יִשְׂרָאֵל֙ מִפְּנֵ֣י פְלִשְׁתִּ֔ים וַיִּפְּל֥וּ חֲלָלִ֖ים בְּהַ֥ר גִּלְבֹּֽעַ׃


**The Koren Jerusalem Bible:**
Now the Pelishtim fought against Yisra᾽el; and the men of Yisra᾽el fled from before the Pelishtim and fell down slain on mount Gilboa.


**The Rashi Ketuvim by Rabbi Shraga Silverstein:**
[viz. I Samuel 31] And the Philistines warred with Israel, and the men of Israel fled before the Philistines, and they fell slain on Mount Gilboa.


###### I Chronicles 10:2

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/I%20Chronicles/r/10:2](https://torahapp.org/share/book/I%20Chronicles/r/10:2)


וַיַּדְבְּק֣וּ פְלִשְׁתִּ֔ים אַחֲרֵ֥י שָׁא֖וּל וְאַחֲרֵ֣י בָנָ֑יו וַיַּכּ֣וּ פְלִשְׁתִּ֗ים אֶת־יוֹנָתָ֧ן וְאֶת־אֲבִינָדָ֛ב וְאֶת־מַלְכִּישׁ֖וּעַ בְּנֵ֥י שָׁאֽוּל׃


**The Koren Jerusalem Bible:**
And the Pelishtim followed hard after Sha᾽ul, and after his sons; and the Pelishtim slew Yonatan, and Avinadav, and Malki-shua, the sons of Sha᾽ul.


**The Rashi Ketuvim by Rabbi Shraga Silverstein:**
And the Philistines overtook Saul and his sons, and the Philistines smote Jonathan and Avinadav and Malkishua, the sons of Saul.


###### I Chronicles 10:3

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/I%20Chronicles/r/10:3](https://torahapp.org/share/book/I%20Chronicles/r/10:3)


וַתִּכְבַּ֤ד הַמִּלְחָמָה֙ עַל־שָׁא֔וּל וַיִּמְצָאֻ֖הוּ הַמּוֹרִ֣ים בַּקָּ֑שֶׁת וַיָּ֖חֶל מִן־הַיּוֹרִֽים׃


**The Koren Jerusalem Bible:**
And the battle went hard against Sha᾽ul, and the archers hit him, and he was in dread because of the archers.


**The Rashi Ketuvim by Rabbi Shraga Silverstein:**
And the battle pressed hard on Saul, and he was found by archers, and he was sorely wounded by the archers.


###### I Chronicles 10:4

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/I%20Chronicles/r/10:4](https://torahapp.org/share/book/I%20Chronicles/r/10:4)


וַיֹּ֣אמֶר שָׁאוּל֩ אֶל־נֹשֵׂ֨א כֵלָ֜יו שְׁלֹ֥ף חַרְבְּךָ֣**׀** וְדׇקְרֵ֣נִי בָ֗הּ פֶּן־יָבֹ֜אוּ הָעֲרֵלִ֤ים הָאֵ֙לֶּה֙ וְהִתְעַלְּלוּ־בִ֔י וְלֹ֤א אָבָה֙ נֹשֵׂ֣א כֵלָ֔יו כִּ֥י יָרֵ֖א מְאֹ֑ד{ס}וַיִּקַּ֤ח שָׁאוּל֙ אֶת־הַחֶ֔רֶב וַיִּפֹּ֖ל עָלֶֽיהָ׃


**The Koren Jerusalem Bible:**
Then Sha᾽ul said to his armourbearer, Draw thy sword, and pierce me with it; lest these uncircumcised ones come and abuse me. But his armourbearer would not; for he was much afraid. So Sha᾽ul took a sword, and fell upon it.


**The Rashi Ketuvim by Rabbi Shraga Silverstein:**
And Saul said to his weapon bearer: Draw your sword and run me through with it, lest these uncircumcised ones come and run me through and abuse me. But his weapon bearer would not, for he feared greatly. And Saul took the sword and he fell upon it.


###### I Chronicles 10:5

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/I%20Chronicles/r/10:5](https://torahapp.org/share/book/I%20Chronicles/r/10:5)


וַיַּ֥רְא נֹשֵֽׂא־כֵלָ֖יו כִּ֣י מֵ֣ת שָׁא֑וּל וַיִּפֹּ֥ל גַּם־ה֛וּא עַל־הַחֶ֖רֶב וַיָּמֹֽת׃{ס}


**The Koren Jerusalem Bible:**

**The Rashi Ketuvim by Rabbi Shraga Silverstein:**
And when his weapon bearer saw that Saul had died, he, too, fell on his sword and died.


###### I Chronicles 10:6

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/I%20Chronicles/r/10:6](https://torahapp.org/share/book/I%20Chronicles/r/10:6)

**The Rashi Ketuvim by Rabbi Shraga Silverstein:**

###### I Chronicles 10:7

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/I%20Chronicles/r/10:7](https://torahapp.org/share/book/I%20Chronicles/r/10:7)


וַ֠יִּרְא֠וּ כׇּל־אִ֨ישׁ יִשְׂרָאֵ֤ל אֲשֶׁר־בָּעֵ֙מֶק֙ כִּ֣י נָ֔סוּ וְכִי־מֵ֖תוּ שָׁא֣וּל וּבָנָ֑יו וַיַּעַזְב֤וּ עָֽרֵיהֶם֙ וַיָּנֻ֔סוּ וַיָּבֹ֣אוּ פְלִשְׁתִּ֔ים וַיֵּשְׁב֖וּ בָּהֶֽם׃{ס}


**The Koren Jerusalem Bible:**
And when all the men of Yisra᾽el who were in the valley saw that they fled, and that Sha᾽ul and his sons were dead, then they forsook their cities, and fled: and the Pelishtim came and dwelt in them.


**The Rashi Ketuvim by Rabbi Shraga Silverstein:**
And all the men of Israel in the valley saw that they had fled and that Saul and his sons had died, and they left their cities and fled, and the Philistines came and dwelt in them.


###### I Chronicles 10:8

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/I%20Chronicles/r/10:8](https://torahapp.org/share/book/I%20Chronicles/r/10:8)


וַֽיְהִי֙ מִֽמׇּחֳרָ֔ת וַיָּבֹ֣אוּ פְלִשְׁתִּ֔ים לְפַשֵּׁ֖ט אֶת־הַחֲלָלִ֑ים וַֽיִּמְצְא֤וּ אֶת־שָׁאוּל֙ וְאֶת־בָּנָ֔יו נֹפְלִ֖ים בְּהַ֥ר גִּלְבֹּֽעַ׃


**The Koren Jerusalem Bible:**
And it came to pass on the morrow, when the Pelishtim came to strip the slain, that they found Sha᾽ul and his sons fallen on mount Gilboa.


**The Rashi Ketuvim by Rabbi Shraga Silverstein:**
And it was on the morrow that the Philistines came to strip the slain, and they found Saul and his sons fallen on Mount Gilboa.


###### I Chronicles 10:9

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/I%20Chronicles/r/10:9](https://torahapp.org/share/book/I%20Chronicles/r/10:9)


וַיַּ֨פְשִׁיטֻ֔הוּ וַיִּשְׂא֥וּ אֶת־רֹאשׁ֖וֹ וְאֶת־כֵּלָ֑יו וַיְשַׁלְּח֨וּ בְאֶרֶץ־פְּלִשְׁתִּ֜ים סָבִ֗יב לְבַשֵּׂ֛ר אֶת־עֲצַבֵּיהֶ֖ם וְאֶת־הָעָֽם׃


**The Koren Jerusalem Bible:**
And when they had stripped him, they took his head, and his armour, and sent into the land of the Pelishtim round about, to carry tidings to their idols, and to the people.


**The Rashi Ketuvim by Rabbi Shraga Silverstein:**
And they stripped him, and they took up his head and his armor, and they sent [them by messenger] in the land of the Philistines roundabout to apprise [the men of] their [houses of] idolatry and the people.


###### I Chronicles 10:10

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/I%20Chronicles/r/10:10](https://torahapp.org/share/book/I%20Chronicles/r/10:10)


וַיָּשִׂ֙ימוּ֙ אֶת־כֵּלָ֔יו בֵּ֖ית אֱלֹהֵיהֶ֑ם וְאֶת־גֻּלְגׇּלְתּ֥וֹ תָקְע֖וּ בֵּ֥ית דָּגֽוֹן׃{ס}


**The Koren Jerusalem Bible:**
And they put his armour in the house of their gods, and fastened his head in the temple of Dagon.


**The Rashi Ketuvim by Rabbi Shraga Silverstein:**
And they put his armor in the house of their god, and they stuck his skull in the house of Dagon.


###### I Chronicles 10:11

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/I%20Chronicles/r/10:11](https://torahapp.org/share/book/I%20Chronicles/r/10:11)


וַֽיִּשְׁמְע֔וּ כֹּ֖ל יָבֵ֣ישׁ גִּלְעָ֑ד אֵ֛ת כׇּל־אֲשֶׁר־עָשׂ֥וּ פְלִשְׁתִּ֖ים לְשָׁאֽוּל׃


**The Koren Jerusalem Bible:**
And when all Yavesh-gil῾ad heard all that the Pelishtim had done to Sha᾽ul,


**The Rashi Ketuvim by Rabbi Shraga Silverstein:**
And all of Yavesh-gilead heard of all that the Philistines had done to Saul.


###### I Chronicles 10:12

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/I%20Chronicles/r/10:12](https://torahapp.org/share/book/I%20Chronicles/r/10:12)


וַיָּק֘וּמוּ֮ כׇּל־אִ֣ישׁ חַ֒יִל֒ וַיִּשְׂא֞וּ אֶת־גּוּפַ֣ת שָׁא֗וּל וְאֵת֙ גּוּפֹ֣ת בָּנָ֔יו וַיְבִיא֖וּם יָבֵ֑ישָׁה וַיִּקְבְּר֨וּ אֶת־עַצְמוֹתֵיהֶ֜ם תַּ֤חַת הָֽאֵלָה֙ בְּיָבֵ֔שׁ וַיָּצ֖וּמוּ שִׁבְעַ֥ת יָמִֽים׃


**The Koren Jerusalem Bible:**
they arose, all the men at arms, and took away the body of Sha᾽ul, and the bodies of his sons, and brought them to Yavesh, and buried their bones under the terebinth in Yavesh, and fasted seven days.


**The Rashi Ketuvim by Rabbi Shraga Silverstein:**
And all the men of valor arose and took up Saul's body and the bodies of his sons and brought them to Yavesh, and they buried their bones under the oak in Yavesh, and they fasted seven days.


###### I Chronicles 10:13

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/I%20Chronicles/r/10:13](https://torahapp.org/share/book/I%20Chronicles/r/10:13)


וַיָּ֣מׇת שָׁא֗וּל בְּמַֽעֲלוֹ֙ אֲשֶׁ֣ר מָעַ֣ל בַּיהֹוָ֔ה עַל־דְּבַ֥ר יְהֹוָ֖ה אֲשֶׁ֣ר לֹֽא־שָׁמָ֑ר וְגַם־לִשְׁא֥וֹל בָּא֖וֹב לִדְרֽוֹשׁ׃


**The Koren Jerusalem Bible:**
So Sha᾽ul died for his transgression which he committed against the Lord, against the word of the Lord, which he did not keep, and also for asking counsel of a medium, to make inquiry;


**The Rashi Ketuvim by Rabbi Shraga Silverstein:**
And Saul died because of his faithlessness, wherein he was faithless to the L-rd, not having kept the word of the L-rd, and, also, having inquired of the ov [viz. I Samuel 28:7].


###### I Chronicles 10:14

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/I%20Chronicles/r/10:14](https://torahapp.org/share/book/I%20Chronicles/r/10:14)


וְלֹא־דָרַ֥שׁ בַּיהֹוָ֖ה וַיְמִיתֵ֑הוּ וַיַּסֵּב֙ אֶת־הַמְּלוּכָ֔ה לְדָוִ֖יד בֶּן־יִשָֽׁי׃{פ}


**The Koren Jerusalem Bible:**
and he inquired not of the Lord: therefore he slew him, and turned the kingdom over to David the son of Yishay.


**The Rashi Ketuvim by Rabbi Shraga Silverstein:**
And he did not inquire of the L-rd, and He slew him, and He transferred the reign to David the son of Yishai.



## Chapter 11

###### I Chronicles 11:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/I%20Chronicles/r/11:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/I%20Chronicles/r/11:1)


וַיִּקָּבְצ֧וּ כׇֽל־יִשְׂרָאֵ֛ל אֶל־דָּוִ֖יד חֶבְר֣וֹנָה לֵאמֹ֑ר הִנֵּ֛ה עַצְמְךָ֥ וּֽבְשָׂרְךָ֖ אֲנָֽחְנוּ׃


**The Koren Jerusalem Bible:**
Then all Yisra᾽el gathered themselves to David to Ḥevron, saying, Behold, we are thy bone and thy flesh.


**The Rashi Ketuvim by Rabbi Shraga Silverstein:**
And all of Israel gathered together to David, to Chevron, saying: Behold, we are your bone and flesh.


###### I Chronicles 11:2

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/I%20Chronicles/r/11:2](https://torahapp.org/share/book/I%20Chronicles/r/11:2)


גַּם־תְּמ֣וֹל גַּם־שִׁלְשׁ֗וֹם גַּ֚ם בִּֽהְי֣וֹת שָׁא֣וּל מֶ֔לֶךְ אַתָּ֛ה הַמּוֹצִ֥יא וְהַמֵּבִ֖יא אֶת־יִשְׂרָאֵ֑ל וַיֹּ֩אמֶר֩ יְהֹוָ֨ה אֱלֹהֶ֜יךָ לְךָ֗ אַתָּ֨ה תִרְעֶ֤ה אֶת־עַמִּי֙ אֶת־יִשְׂרָאֵ֔ל וְאַתָּה֙ תִּֽהְיֶ֣ה נָגִ֔יד עַ֖ל עַמִּ֥י יִשְׂרָאֵֽל׃


**The Koren Jerusalem Bible:**
And moreover in time past, even when Sha᾽ul was king, thou wast he who didst lead out Yisra᾽el and bring them in: and the Lord thy God said to thee, Thou shalt be shepherd over my people Yisra᾽el, and thou shalt be ruler over my people Yisra᾽el.


**The Rashi Ketuvim by Rabbi Shraga Silverstein:**
Also of yore, also when Saul was king, it was you who led Israel out and brought them in. And the L-rd said to you: You shall graze My people Israel, and you shall be a leader over Israel.


###### I Chronicles 11:3

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/I%20Chronicles/r/11:3](https://torahapp.org/share/book/I%20Chronicles/r/11:3)


וַ֠יָּבֹ֠אוּ כׇּל־זִקְנֵ֨י יִשְׂרָאֵ֤ל אֶל־הַמֶּ֙לֶךְ֙ חֶבְר֔וֹנָה וַיִּכְרֹת֩ לָהֶ֨ם דָּוִ֥יד בְּרִ֛ית בְּחֶבְר֖וֹן לִפְנֵ֣י יְהֹוָ֑ה וַיִּמְשְׁח֨וּ אֶת־דָּוִ֤יד לְמֶ֙לֶךְ֙ עַל־יִשְׂרָאֵ֔ל כִּדְבַ֥ר יְהֹוָ֖ה בְּיַד־שְׁמוּאֵֽל׃{ס}


**The Koren Jerusalem Bible:**
Therefore all the elders of Yisra᾽el came to the king to Ḥevron; and David made a covenant with them in Ḥevron before the Lord; and they anointed David king over Yisra᾽el, according to the word of the Lord by Shemu᾽el.


**The Rashi Ketuvim by Rabbi Shraga Silverstein:**
And all the elders of Israel came to the king in Chevron and David made a covenant with them in Chevron before the L-rd. And they anointed David as king over Israel, according to the word of the L-rd by Samuel.


###### I Chronicles 11:4

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/I%20Chronicles/r/11:4](https://torahapp.org/share/book/I%20Chronicles/r/11:4)


וַיֵּ֨לֶךְ דָּוִ֧יד וְכׇל־יִשְׂרָאֵ֛ל יְרוּשָׁלַ֖͏ִם הִ֣יא יְב֑וּס וְשָׁם֙ הַיְבוּסִ֔י יֹשְׁבֵ֖י הָאָֽרֶץ׃


**The Koren Jerusalem Bible:**
And David and all Yisra᾽el went to Yerushalayim, which is Yevus; where the Yevusi were, the inhabitants of the land.


**The Rashi Ketuvim by Rabbi Shraga Silverstein:**
And David and all of Israel went to Jerusalem — Yevus — where the Yevusites were, the inhabitants of the land.


###### I Chronicles 11:5

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/I%20Chronicles/r/11:5](https://torahapp.org/share/book/I%20Chronicles/r/11:5)


וַיֹּ֨אמְר֜וּ יֹשְׁבֵ֤י יְבוּס֙ לְדָוִ֔יד לֹ֥א תָב֖וֹא הֵ֑נָּה וַיִּלְכֹּ֤ד דָּוִיד֙ אֶת־מְצֻדַ֣ת צִיּ֔וֹן הִ֖יא עִ֥יר דָּוִֽיד׃


**The Koren Jerusalem Bible:**
And the inhabitants of Yevus said to David, Thou shalt not come here. Nevertheless David took the stronghold of Żiyyon, which is the city of David.


**The Rashi Ketuvim by Rabbi Shraga Silverstein:**
And the inhabitants of Yevus said to David: You will not come here. And David took [(Yevus)] Metzudath Tziyon [("the stronghold of Zion")], which is the city of David.


###### I Chronicles 11:6

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/I%20Chronicles/r/11:6](https://torahapp.org/share/book/I%20Chronicles/r/11:6)


וַיֹּ֣אמֶר דָּוִ֔יד כׇּל־מַכֵּ֤ה יְבוּסִי֙ בָּרִ֣אשׁוֹנָ֔ה יִהְיֶ֥ה לְרֹ֖אשׁ וּלְשָׂ֑ר וַיַּ֧עַל בָּרִאשׁוֹנָ֛ה יוֹאָ֥ב בֶּן־צְרוּיָ֖ה וַיְהִ֥י לְרֹֽאשׁ׃


**The Koren Jerusalem Bible:**
And David said, Whoever smites the Yevusi first shall be chief and captain. So Yo᾽av the son of Żeruya went first up, and became the chief.


**The Rashi Ketuvim by Rabbi Shraga Silverstein:**
And David said: Whoever smites the Yevusites, [going up] first, will be chief and head. And Yoav the son of Tzeruyah went up first, and he became the head.


###### I Chronicles 11:7

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/I%20Chronicles/r/11:7](https://torahapp.org/share/book/I%20Chronicles/r/11:7)


וַיֵּ֥שֶׁב דָּוִ֖יד בַּמְצָ֑ד עַל־כֵּ֥ן קָרְאוּ־ל֖וֹ עִ֥יר דָּוִֽיד׃


**The Koren Jerusalem Bible:**
And David dwelt in the stronghold; therefore they called it the city of David.


**The Rashi Ketuvim by Rabbi Shraga Silverstein:**
And David dwelt in the stronghold, wherefore they called it "the city of David."


###### I Chronicles 11:8

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/I%20Chronicles/r/11:8](https://torahapp.org/share/book/I%20Chronicles/r/11:8)


וַיִּ֤בֶן הָעִיר֙ מִסָּבִ֔יב מִן־הַמִּלּ֖וֹא וְעַד־הַסָּבִ֑יב וְיוֹאָ֕ב יְחַיֶּ֖ה אֶת־שְׁאָ֥ר הָעִֽיר׃


**The Koren Jerusalem Bible:**
And he built the city round about, even from the Millo round about: and Yo᾽av repaired the rest of the city.


**The Rashi Ketuvim by Rabbi Shraga Silverstein:**
And he built the city roundabout the milo [(a low-walled enclosure encircling the stronghold)] and within it. And Yoav restored the rest of the city.


###### I Chronicles 11:9

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/I%20Chronicles/r/11:9](https://torahapp.org/share/book/I%20Chronicles/r/11:9)


וַיֵּ֥לֶךְ דָּוִ֖יד הָל֣וֹךְ וְגָד֑וֹל וַיהֹוָ֥ה צְבָא֖וֹת עִמּֽוֹ׃{פ}


**The Koren Jerusalem Bible:**
So David grew greater and greater: for the Lord of hosts was with him.


**The Rashi Ketuvim by Rabbi Shraga Silverstein:**
And David grew ever greater, and the L-rd of hosts was with him.


###### I Chronicles 11:10

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/I%20Chronicles/r/11:10](https://torahapp.org/share/book/I%20Chronicles/r/11:10)


וְאֵ֨לֶּה רָאשֵׁ֤י הַגִּבֹּרִים֙ אֲשֶׁ֣ר לְדָוִ֔יד הַמִּתְחַזְּקִ֨ים עִמּ֧וֹ בְמַלְכוּת֛וֹ עִם־כׇּל־יִשְׂרָאֵ֖ל לְהַמְלִיכ֑וֹ כִּדְבַ֥ר יְהֹוָ֖ה עַל־יִשְׂרָאֵֽל׃{ס}


**The Koren Jerusalem Bible:**
These also are the chiefs of the mighty men whom David had, who held fast to him in his kingdom, together with all Yisra᾽el, to make him king, according to the word of the Lord concerning Yisra᾽el.


**The Rashi Ketuvim by Rabbi Shraga Silverstein:**
And these are the heads of David's stalwarts, who championed his kingdom, to make him king, according to the word of the L-rd concerning Israel.


###### I Chronicles 11:11

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/I%20Chronicles/r/11:11](https://torahapp.org/share/book/I%20Chronicles/r/11:11)


וְאֵ֛לֶּה מִסְפַּ֥ר הַגִּבֹּרִ֖ים אֲשֶׁ֣ר לְדָוִ֑יד יָֽשׇׁבְעָ֣ם בֶּן־חַכְמוֹנִ֗י רֹ֚אשׁ (השלושים) [הַשָּׁ֣לִישִׁ֔ים] הֽוּא־עוֹרֵ֧ר אֶת־חֲנִית֛וֹ עַל־שְׁלֹשׁ־מֵא֥וֹת חָלָ֖ל בְּפַ֥עַם אֶחָֽת׃


**The Koren Jerusalem Bible:**
And this is the number of the mighty men whom David had; Yoshov῾am, the son of Ḥakhmoni, the chief of the captains: he swung his spear over three hundred slain at one time.


**The Rashi Ketuvim by Rabbi Shraga Silverstein:**
And these are the number of David's stalwarts [viz. II Samuel 23:8-39]: Yashavam the son of Chachmoni, head of the three. He would brandish his spear [(to fight further)] over three hundred that he had slain at one time.


###### I Chronicles 11:12

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/I%20Chronicles/r/11:12](https://torahapp.org/share/book/I%20Chronicles/r/11:12)


וְאַחֲרָ֛יו אֶלְעָזָ֥ר בֶּן־דּוֹד֖וֹ הָאֲחוֹחִ֑י ה֖וּא בִּשְׁלוֹשָׁ֥ה הַגִּבֹּרִֽים׃


**The Koren Jerusalem Bible:**
And after him was El῾azar the son of Dodo, the Aĥoĥite, who was one of the three mighty men.


**The Rashi Ketuvim by Rabbi Shraga Silverstein:**
And after him, Elazar the son of Dodo the Achohite, among the three [choicest] of the mighty.


###### I Chronicles 11:13

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/I%20Chronicles/r/11:13](https://torahapp.org/share/book/I%20Chronicles/r/11:13)


הֽוּא־הָיָ֨ה עִם־דָּוִ֜יד בַּפַּ֣ס דַּמִּ֗ים וְהַפְּלִשְׁתִּים֙ נֶאֶסְפוּ־שָׁ֣ם לַמִּלְחָמָ֔ה וַתְּהִ֛י חֶלְקַ֥ת הַשָּׂדֶ֖ה מְלֵאָ֣ה שְׂעוֹרִ֑ים וְהָעָ֥ם נָ֖סוּ מִפְּנֵ֥י פְלִשְׁתִּֽים׃


**The Koren Jerusalem Bible:**
He was with David at Pas-dammim, and there the Pelishtim were gathered together to battle, where there was a plot of ground full of barley; and the people fled from before the Pelishtim.


**The Rashi Ketuvim by Rabbi Shraga Silverstein:**
He was with David in Pas-damim when the Philistines were gathered there for war. And there was a field-plot (there) full of barley. (And the people had fled from the Philistines).


###### I Chronicles 11:14

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/I%20Chronicles/r/11:14](https://torahapp.org/share/book/I%20Chronicles/r/11:14)


וַיִּֽתְיַצְּב֤וּ בְתוֹךְ־הַֽחֶלְקָה֙ וַיַּצִּיל֔וּהָ וַיַּכּ֖וּ אֶת־פְּלִשְׁתִּ֑ים וַיּ֥וֹשַׁע יְהֹוָ֖ה תְּשׁוּעָ֥ה גְדוֹלָֽה׃


**The Koren Jerusalem Bible:**
And they set themselves in the midst of that plot, and delivered it, and slew the Pelishtim; and the Lord saved them by a great deliverance.


**The Rashi Ketuvim by Rabbi Shraga Silverstein:**
And they [(he and his men)] stood in the midst of the plot and they saved it and they smote the Philistines. And the L-rd wrought a great salvation.


###### I Chronicles 11:15

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/I%20Chronicles/r/11:15](https://torahapp.org/share/book/I%20Chronicles/r/11:15)


וַיֵּרְד֡וּ שְֽׁלוֹשָׁה֩ מִן־הַשְּׁלוֹשִׁ֨ים רֹ֤אשׁ עַל־הַצֻּר֙ אֶל־דָּוִ֔יד אֶל־מְעָרַ֖ת עֲדֻלָּ֑ם וּמַחֲנֵ֣ה פְלִשְׁתִּ֔ים חֹנָ֖ה בְּעֵ֥מֶק רְפָאִֽים׃


**The Koren Jerusalem Bible:**
Now three of the thirty captains went down to the rock to David, into the cave of ῾Adullam; and the host of the Pelishtim encamped in the valley of Refa᾽im.


**The Rashi Ketuvim by Rabbi Shraga Silverstein:**
And there went down three of the chief officers upon the rock to David, to the cave of Adulam. And a troop of Philistines were encamped in the valley of Refaim.


###### I Chronicles 11:16

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/I%20Chronicles/r/11:16](https://torahapp.org/share/book/I%20Chronicles/r/11:16)


וְדָוִ֖יד אָ֣ז בַּמְּצוּדָ֑ה וּנְצִ֣יב פְּלִשְׁתִּ֔ים אָ֖ז בְּבֵ֥ית לָֽחֶם׃


**The Koren Jerusalem Bible:**
And David was then in the stronghold, and the garrison of the Pelishtim was then at Bet-leĥem.


**The Rashi Ketuvim by Rabbi Shraga Silverstein:**

###### I Chronicles 11:17

**The Koren Jerusalem Bible:**

**The Rashi Ketuvim by Rabbi Shraga Silverstein:**

###### I Chronicles 11:18

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/I%20Chronicles/r/11:18](https://torahapp.org/share/book/I%20Chronicles/r/11:18)


וַיִּבְקְע֨וּ הַשְּׁלֹשָׁ֜ה בְּמַחֲנֵ֣ה פְלִשְׁתִּ֗ים וַיִּֽשְׁאֲבוּ־מַ֙יִם֙ מִבּ֤וֹר בֵּֽית־לֶ֙חֶם֙ אֲשֶׁ֣ר בַּשַּׁ֔עַר וַיִּשְׂא֖וּ וַיָּבִ֣אוּ אֶל־דָּוִ֑יד וְלֹא־אָבָ֤ה דָוִיד֙ לִשְׁתּוֹתָ֔ם וַיְנַסֵּ֥ךְ אֹתָ֖ם לַיהֹוָֽה׃


**The Koren Jerusalem Bible:**
And those three broke through the host of the Pelishtim, and drew water out of the well of Bet-leĥem, that was by the gate, and took it, and brought it to David: but David would not drink of it, but poured it out to the Lord,


**The Rashi Ketuvim by Rabbi Shraga Silverstein:**
And the three broke through the camp of the Philistines and drew water from the well of Beth-lechem near the gate. And they carried it and brought it to David. And David did not want to drink it, and he poured it out before the L-rd.

וַיֹּ֡אמֶר חָלִ֩ילָה֩ לִּ֨י מֵאֱלֹהַ֜י מֵעֲשׂ֣וֹת זֹ֗את הֲדַ֣ם הָאֲנָשִׁים֩ הָאֵ֨לֶּה אֶשְׁתֶּ֤ה בְנַפְשׁוֹתָם֙ כִּ֣י בְנַפְשׁוֹתָ֣ם הֱבִיא֔וּם וְלֹ֥א אָבָ֖ה לִשְׁתּוֹתָ֑ם אֵ֣לֶּה עָשׂ֔וּ שְׁלֹ֖שֶׁת הַגִּבּוֹרִֽים׃


**The Koren Jerusalem Bible:**
and he said, far be it from me before my God, that I should do this thing: shall I drink the blood of these men that have put their lives in jeopardy? for with the jeopardy of their lives have they brought it. Therefore he would not drink it. These things did these three mightiest.


**The Rashi Ketuvim by Rabbi Shraga Silverstein:**
And he said: G d forbid that I should do this. Shall I drink [the blood] of these men with their lives? For at [the risk of] their lives did they bring it! And he did not want to drink it. These (things) were done by the three warriors.

and Yishma῾ya the Giv῾onite, a mighty man among the thirty, and over the thirty;

וּמִן־הַגָּדִ֡י נִבְדְּל֣וּ אֶל־דָּוִיד֩ לַמְצַ֨ד מִדְבָּ֜רָה גִּבֹּרֵ֣י הַחַ֗יִל אַנְשֵׁ֤י צָבָא֙ לַמִּלְחָמָ֔ה עֹרְכֵ֥י צִנָּ֖ה וָרֹ֑מַח וּפְנֵ֤י אַרְיֵה֙ פְּנֵיהֶ֔ם וְכִצְבָאיִ֥ם עַל־הֶהָרִ֖ים לְמַהֵֽר׃

אֵ֣לֶּה הֵ֗ם אֲשֶׁ֨ר עָבְר֤וּ אֶת־הַיַּרְדֵּן֙ בַּחֹ֣דֶשׁ הָרִאשׁ֔וֹן וְה֥וּא מְמַלֵּ֖א עַל־כׇּל־[גְּדוֹתָ֑יו] (גדיתיו) וַיַּבְרִ֙יחוּ֙ אֶת־כׇּל־הָ֣עֲמָקִ֔ים לַמִּזְרָ֖ח וְלַֽמַּעֲרָֽב׃{פ}

###### I Chronicles 12:17

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/I%20Chronicles/r/12:17](https://torahapp.org/share/book/I%20Chronicles/r/12:17)

And there came of the children of Binyamin and Yehuda to the stronghold, to David.


**The Rashi Ketuvim by Rabbi Shraga Silverstein:**

###### I Chronicles 12:18

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/I%20Chronicles/r/12:18](https://torahapp.org/share/book/I%20Chronicles/r/12:18)


וַיֵּצֵ֣א דָוִיד֮ לִפְנֵיהֶם֒ וַיַּ֙עַן֙ וַיֹּ֣אמֶר לָהֶ֔ם אִם־לְשָׁל֞וֹם בָּאתֶ֤ם אֵלַי֙ לְעׇזְרֵ֔נִי יִֽהְיֶה־לִּ֧י עֲלֵיכֶ֛ם לֵבָ֖ב לְיָ֑חַד וְאִֽם־לְרַמּוֹתַ֣נִי לְצָרַ֗י בְּלֹ֤א חָמָס֙ בְּכַפַּ֔י יֵ֛רֶא אֱלֹהֵ֥י אֲבוֹתֵ֖ינוּ וְיוֹכַֽח׃{ס}


**The Koren Jerusalem Bible:**
And David went out to meet them, and answered and said to them, If you are come peaceably to me to help me, my heart shall be knit to you: but if you are come to betray me to my enemies, seeing there is no wrong in my hands, then let the God of our fathers see, and rebuke you.


**The Rashi Ketuvim by Rabbi Shraga Silverstein:**
And David went out before them and said to them: If for peace you have come to me, to aid me, may my heart be upon you as one (heart); but if to deceive me (and hand me over) to my foes, with no wrong in my hands, may the G-d of our fathers see (it) and exact payment.


###### I Chronicles 12:19

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/I%20Chronicles/r/12:19](https://torahapp.org/share/book/I%20Chronicles/r/12:19)


וְר֣וּחַ לָֽבְשָׁ֗ה אֶת־עֲמָשַׂי֮ רֹ֣אשׁ (השלושים) [הַשָּׁלִישִׁים֒] לְךָ֤ דָוִיד֙ וְעִמְּךָ֣ בֶן־יִשַׁ֔י שָׁל֨וֹם׀שָׁל֜וֹם לְךָ֗ וְשָׁלוֹם֙ לְעֹ֣זְרֶ֔ךָ כִּ֥י עֲזָֽרְךָ֖ אֱלֹהֶ֑יךָ וַיְקַבְּלֵ֣ם דָּוִ֔יד וַֽיִּתְּנֵ֖ם בְּרָאשֵׁ֥י הַגְּדֽוּד׃{פ}

Then the spirit came upon ῾Amasay, who was chief of the captains, and he said, Thine are we, David, and on thy side, thou son of Yishay: peace, peace be to thee, and peace be to thy helpers; for thy God helps thee. Then David received them, and made them captains of the band.


**The Rashi Ketuvim by Rabbi Shraga Silverstein:**
And a spirit invested Amassai, the chief of the officers (and he said:) [We are] for you, David, and with you, son of Yishai: Peace, peace unto you and peace to your helpers, for the L-rd has helped you! And David received them and placed them at the heads of the battalions.


###### I Chronicles 12:20

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/I%20Chronicles/r/12:20](https://torahapp.org/share/book/I%20Chronicles/r/12:20)


וּמִֽמְּנַשֶּׁ֞ה נָפְל֣וּ עַל־דָּוִ֗יד בְּבֹא֨וֹ עִם־פְּלִשְׁתִּ֧ים עַל־שָׁא֛וּל לַמִּלְחָמָ֖ה וְלֹ֣א עֲזָרֻ֑ם כִּ֣י בְעֵצָ֗ה שִׁלְּחֻ֜הוּ סַרְנֵ֤י פְלִשְׁתִּים֙ לֵאמֹ֔ר בְּרָאשֵׁ֕ינוּ יִפּ֖וֹל אֶל־אֲדֹנָ֥יו שָׁאֽוּל׃


**The Koren Jerusalem Bible:**
And some of Menashshe deserted to David, when he came with the Pelishtim against Sha᾽ul to battle: but they did not help them: for the lords of the Pelishtim took counsel and sent him away, saying, He will desert to his master Sha᾽ul to the jeopardy of our heads.


**The Rashi Ketuvim by Rabbi Shraga Silverstein:**
And from Menasheh there went over (men) to David, when he came with the Philistines against Saul for war (But he did not help them [the Philistines], for in counsel the chiefs of the Philistines sent him away, saying: "With our heads [as a bribe], he will go over to his master, Saul" [who will thereby pardon him]).


###### I Chronicles 12:21

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/I%20Chronicles/r/12:21](https://torahapp.org/share/book/I%20Chronicles/r/12:21)


בְּלֶכְתּ֣וֹ אֶל־צִֽיקְלַ֗ג נָפְל֣וּ עָלָ֣יו׀מִֽמְּנַשֶּׁ֡ה עַ֠דְנַ֠ח וְיוֹזָבָ֤ד וִידִֽיעֲאֵל֙ וּמִיכָאֵ֣ל וְיוֹזָבָ֔ד וֶאֱלִיה֖וּא וְצִלְּתָ֑י רָאשֵׁ֥י הָאֲלָפִ֖ים אֲשֶׁ֥ר לִמְנַשֶּֽׁה׃


**The Koren Jerusalem Bible:**
As he went to Żiqlag, there deserted to him of Menashshe, ῾Adnah, and Yozavad, and Yedi῾a᾽el, and Mikha᾽el, and Yozavad, and Elihu, and Żilletay, captains of the thousands that were of Menashshe.


**The Rashi Ketuvim by Rabbi Shraga Silverstein:**
When he went (back) to Tziklag, there went over to him of Menasheh: Adnach and Yozavad and Yedia'el and Michael and Yozavad and Elihu and Tzilathai, chiefs of the thousands of Menasheh.

## Chapter 13

###### I Chronicles 13:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/I%20Chronicles/r/13:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/I%20Chronicles/r/13:1)


וַיִּוָּעַ֣ץ דָּוִ֗יד עִם־שָׂרֵ֧י הָאֲלָפִ֛ים וְהַמֵּא֖וֹת לְכׇל־נָגִֽיד׃


**The Koren Jerusalem Bible:**
And David consulted with the captains of thousands and hundreds, and with every leader.


**The Rashi Ketuvim by Rabbi Shraga Silverstein:**
And David took counsel with the officers of the thousands and the hundreds, by all the leaders.


###### I Chronicles 13:2

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/I%20Chronicles/r/13:2](https://torahapp.org/share/book/I%20Chronicles/r/13:2)


וַיֹּ֨אמֶר דָּוִ֜יד לְכֹ֣ל**׀** קְהַ֣ל יִשְׂרָאֵ֗ל אִם־עֲלֵיכֶ֨ם ט֜וֹב וּמִן־יְהֹוָ֣ה אֱלֹהֵ֗ינוּ נִפְרְצָה֙ נִשְׁלְחָ֞ה עַל־אַחֵ֣ינוּ הַנִּשְׁאָרִ֗ים בְּכֹל֙ אַרְצ֣וֹת יִשְׂרָאֵ֔ל וְעִמָּהֶ֛ם הַכֹּהֲנִ֥ים וְהַלְוִיִּ֖ם בְּעָרֵ֣י מִגְרְשֵׁיהֶ֑ם וְיִקָּבְצ֖וּ אֵלֵֽינוּ׃


**The Koren Jerusalem Bible:**
And David said to all the congregation of Yisra᾽el, If it seem good to you, and that it be of the Lord our God, let us send abroad to our brethren everywhere, who are left in all the land of Yisra᾽el, and with them also to the priests and Levites who are in their cities that have pasture lands that they may gather themselves to us:


**The Rashi Ketuvim by Rabbi Shraga Silverstein:**
And David said to the entire congregation of Israel: If it is acceptable to you, and it has gone forth from the L-rd our G-d, let us send to our brothers remaining in all the lands of Israel, and with them, the Cohanim and the Levites in the cities of their open lands, and let them gather themselves unto us.


###### I Chronicles 13:3

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/I%20Chronicles/r/13:3](https://torahapp.org/share/book/I%20Chronicles/r/13:3)


וְנָסֵ֛בָּה אֶת־אֲר֥וֹן אֱלֹהֵ֖ינוּ אֵלֵ֑ינוּ כִּֽי־לֹ֥א דְרַשְׁנֻ֖הוּ בִּימֵ֥י שָׁאֽוּל׃


**The Koren Jerusalem Bible:**
and let us bring back the ark of our God to us: for we did not inquire at it in the days of Sha᾽ul.


**The Rashi Ketuvim by Rabbi Shraga Silverstein:**
And let us return the ark of our G-d to us, for we did not solicit Him in the days of Saul.


###### I Chronicles 13:4

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/I%20Chronicles/r/13:4](https://torahapp.org/share/book/I%20Chronicles/r/13:4)


וַיֹּאמְר֥וּ כׇֽל־הַקָּהָ֖ל לַעֲשׂ֣וֹת כֵּ֑ן כִּֽי־יָשַׁ֥ר הַדָּבָ֖ר בְּעֵינֵ֥י כׇל־הָעָֽם׃


**The Koren Jerusalem Bible:**
And all the congregation said that they would do so: for the thing seemed right in the eyes of all the people.


**The Rashi Ketuvim by Rabbi Shraga Silverstein:**
And the entire congregation said to do so, for the thing was right in the eyes of all the people.

וַיַּקְהֵ֤ל דָּוִיד֙ אֶת־כׇּל־יִשְׂרָאֵ֔ל מִן־שִׁיח֥וֹר מִצְרַ֖יִם וְעַד־לְב֣וֹא חֲמָ֑ת לְהָבִיא֙ אֶת־אֲר֣וֹן הָאֱלֹהִ֔ים מִקִּרְיַ֖ת יְעָרִֽים׃


**The Koren Jerusalem Bible:**

**The Rashi Ketuvim by Rabbi Shraga Silverstein:**
And David gathered all of Israel, from Shichor of Egypt until Levo-chamath, to bring the ark of G-d from Kiryath-yearim.

**The Koren Jerusalem Bible:**
And David went up, and all Yisra᾽el to Ba῾ala, that is, to Qiryat-ye῾arim, which belonged to Yehuda, to bring up from there the ark of God the Lord, who dwells above the keruvim, by whose name it is called.


**The Rashi Ketuvim by Rabbi Shraga Silverstein:**
And David went up, and all of Israel, to Ba'alah, to Kiryath-yearim of Judah, to bring up from there the ark of G-d, the L-rd, who dwells upon the cherubs, which was called by the Name.

**The Rashi Ketuvim by Rabbi Shraga Silverstein:**

**The Rashi Ketuvim by Rabbi Shraga Silverstein:**

**The Rashi Ketuvim by Rabbi Shraga Silverstein:**

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/I%20Chronicles/r/14:2](https://torahapp.org/share/book/I%20Chronicles/r/14:2)

**The Rashi Ketuvim by Rabbi Shraga Silverstein:**

and Yivĥar, and Elishua, and Elpelet,

and Noga, and Nefeg, and Yafia,


**The Rashi Ketuvim by Rabbi Shraga Silverstein:**
And Nogah and Nefeg and Yafia.

**The Koren Jerusalem Bible:**
and Elishama, and Be῾elyada, and Elifelet.


**The Rashi Ketuvim by Rabbi Shraga Silverstein:**
And Elishama and Belyada and Elifalet.


###### I Chronicles 14:8

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/I%20Chronicles/r/14:8](https://torahapp.org/share/book/I%20Chronicles/r/14:8)


וַיִּשְׁמְע֣וּ פְלִשְׁתִּ֗ים כִּי־נִמְשַׁ֨ח דָּוִ֤יד לְמֶ֙לֶךְ֙ עַל־כׇּל־יִשְׂרָאֵ֔ל וַיַּעֲל֥וּ כׇל־פְּלִשְׁתִּ֖ים לְבַקֵּ֣שׁ אֶת־דָּוִ֑יד וַיִּשְׁמַ֣ע דָּוִ֔יד וַיֵּצֵ֖א לִפְנֵיהֶֽם׃


**The Koren Jerusalem Bible:**
And when the Pelishtim heard that David was anointed king over all Yisra᾽el, all the Pelishtim went up to seek David. And David heard of it, and went out against them.


**The Rashi Ketuvim by Rabbi Shraga Silverstein:**
And the Philistines heard that they had anointed David as a king over all of Israel, and all the Philistines went up to seek out David. And David heard and he went out before them.

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/I%20Chronicles/r/14:9](https://torahapp.org/share/book/I%20Chronicles/r/14:9)


וּפְלִשְׁתִּ֖ים בָּ֑אוּ וַֽיִּפְשְׁט֖וּ בְּעֵ֥מֶק רְפָאִֽים׃


**The Koren Jerusalem Bible:**
And the Pelishtim came and made a raid in the valley of Refa᾽im.


**The Rashi Ketuvim by Rabbi Shraga Silverstein:**
And the Philistines came and spread out in the valley of Rephaim.


###### I Chronicles 14:10

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/I%20Chronicles/r/14:10](https://torahapp.org/share/book/I%20Chronicles/r/14:10)


וַיִּשְׁאַ֨ל דָּוִ֤יד בֵּאלֹהִים֙ לֵאמֹ֔ר הַאֶֽעֱלֶה֙ עַל־[פְּלִשְׁתִּ֔ים] (פלשתיים) וּנְתַתָּ֖ם בְּיָדִ֑י וַיֹּ֨אמֶר ל֤וֹ יְהֹוָה֙ עֲלֵ֔ה וּנְתַתִּ֖ים בְּיָדֶֽךָ׃


**The Koren Jerusalem Bible:**
And David inquired of God, saying, Shall I go up against the Pelishtim? and wilt Thou deliver them into my hand? And the Lord said to him, Go up; for I will deliver them into thy hand.


**The Rashi Ketuvim by Rabbi Shraga Silverstein:**
And David inquired of G-d, saying: Shall I go up against the Philistines? Will You deliver them into my hand? And the L-rd said to him: Go up, and I will deliver them into your hand.


###### I Chronicles 14:11

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/I%20Chronicles/r/14:11](https://torahapp.org/share/book/I%20Chronicles/r/14:11)


וַיַּעֲל֥וּ בְּבַֽעַל־פְּרָצִים֮ וַיַּכֵּ֣ם שָׁ֣ם דָּוִיד֒ וַיֹּ֣אמֶר דָּוִ֔יד פָּרַ֨ץ הָאֱלֹהִ֧ים אֶת־אוֹיְבַ֛י בְּיָדִ֖י כְּפֶ֣רֶץ מָ֑יִם עַל־כֵּ֗ן קָ֥רְא֛וּ שֵֽׁם־הַמָּק֥וֹם הַה֖וּא בַּ֥עַל פְּרָצִֽים׃


**The Koren Jerusalem Bible:**
So they came up to Ba῾al-perażim; and David smote them there. Then David said, God has broken through my enemies by my hand like a bursting flood of waters: therefore they called the name of that place Ba῾al-perażim.


**The Rashi Ketuvim by Rabbi Shraga Silverstein:**
And they went up in the plain of Peratzim and David smote them there. And David said: G-d has breached my foes in my hand as a "breach" [peretz] of water [i.e., as water that breaches its banks], wherefore he called the name of that place "the plain of Peratzim."


###### I Chronicles 14:12

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/I%20Chronicles/r/14:12](https://torahapp.org/share/book/I%20Chronicles/r/14:12)


וַיַּ֥עַזְבוּ שָׁ֖ם אֶת־אֱלֹהֵיהֶ֑ם וַיֹּ֣אמֶר דָּוִ֔יד וַיִּשָּׂרְפ֖וּ בָּאֵֽשׁ׃{פ}


**The Koren Jerusalem Bible:**
And they left their gods there and David gave command, that they should be burned with fire.


**The Rashi Ketuvim by Rabbi Shraga Silverstein:**
And they left their gods there, and David commanded and they were burnt in fire.


###### I Chronicles 14:13

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/I%20Chronicles/r/14:13](https://torahapp.org/share/book/I%20Chronicles/r/14:13)


וַיֹּסִ֤יפוּ עוֹד֙ פְּלִשְׁתִּ֔ים וַֽיִּפְשְׁט֖וּ בָּעֵֽמֶק׃


**The Koren Jerusalem Bible:**
And the Pelishtim once again made a raid in the valley.


**The Rashi Ketuvim by Rabbi Shraga Silverstein:**
And the Philistines ventured again and they spread out in the valley.


###### I Chronicles 14:14

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/I%20Chronicles/r/14:14](https://torahapp.org/share/book/I%20Chronicles/r/14:14)


וַיִּשְׁאַ֨ל ע֤וֹד דָּוִיד֙ בֵּאלֹהִ֔ים וַיֹּ֤אמֶר לוֹ֙ הָאֱלֹהִ֔ים לֹ֥א תַעֲלֶ֖ה אַחֲרֵיהֶ֑ם הָסֵב֙ מֵֽעֲלֵיהֶ֔ם וּבָ֥אתָ לָהֶ֖ם מִמּ֥וּל הַבְּכָאִֽים׃


**The Koren Jerusalem Bible:**
So David inquired again of God; and God said to him, Do not go up after them; turn away from them, and come upon them opposite the bakha trees.


**The Rashi Ketuvim by Rabbi Shraga Silverstein:**
And David inquired of G-d again, and G-d said to him: Do not go up after them. Circle behind them and come against them opposite the mulberry trees.


###### I Chronicles 14:15

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/I%20Chronicles/r/14:15](https://torahapp.org/share/book/I%20Chronicles/r/14:15)

**The Koren Jerusalem Bible:**

**The Rashi Ketuvim by Rabbi Shraga Silverstein:**

**The Rashi Ketuvim by Rabbi Shraga Silverstein:**
