###### I Chronicles 16:18

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/I%20Chronicles/r/16:18](https://torahapp.org/share/book/I%20Chronicles/r/16:18)


לֵאמֹ֗ר לְךָ֙ אֶתֵּ֣ן אֶֽרֶץ־כְּנָ֔עַןחֶ֖בֶל נַחֲלַתְכֶֽם׃


**The Koren Jerusalem Bible:**
saying, To thee will I give the land of Kena῾an, the lot of your inheritance;


**The Rashi Ketuvim by Rabbi Shraga Silverstein:**
saying [(this being the covenant)]: To you shall I give the land of Canaan, the portion of your inheritance.


###### I Chronicles 16:19

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/I%20Chronicles/r/16:19](https://torahapp.org/share/book/I%20Chronicles/r/16:19)


בִּהְיֽוֹתְכֶם֙ מְתֵ֣י מִסְפָּ֔רכִּמְעַ֖ט וְגָרִ֥ים בָּֽהּ׃


**The Koren Jerusalem Bible:**
when you were but a few men in number, of little account and sojourners there:


**The Rashi Ketuvim by Rabbi Shraga Silverstein:**
When they were yet few in number, sparse, and sojourning in it,


###### I Chronicles 16:20

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/I%20Chronicles/r/16:20](https://torahapp.org/share/book/I%20Chronicles/r/16:20)


וַיִּֽתְהַלְּכוּ֙ מִגּ֣וֹי אֶל־גּ֔וֹיוּמִמַּמְלָכָ֖ה אֶל־עַ֥ם אַחֵֽר׃


**The Koren Jerusalem Bible:**
when they went from nation to nation, and from one kingdom to another people;


**The Rashi Ketuvim by Rabbi Shraga Silverstein:**
and they [(the patriarchs)] went around from one nation to another; from one kingdom to a different people.


###### I Chronicles 16:21

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/I%20Chronicles/r/16:21](https://torahapp.org/share/book/I%20Chronicles/r/16:21)


לֹֽא־הִנִּ֤יחַ לְאִישׁ֙ לְעׇשְׁקָ֔םוַיּ֥וֹכַח עֲלֵיהֶ֖ם מְלָכִֽים׃


**The Koren Jerusalem Bible:**
he suffered no man to do them wrong: but he reproved kings for their sakes, saying,


**The Rashi Ketuvim by Rabbi Shraga Silverstein:**
He allowed no man to despoil them and He chastised kings over them. [viz. Genesis 12:17]


###### I Chronicles 16:22

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/I%20Chronicles/r/16:22](https://torahapp.org/share/book/I%20Chronicles/r/16:22)


אַֽל־תִּגְּעוּ֙ בִּמְשִׁיחָ֔יוּבִנְבִיאַ֖י אַל־תָּרֵֽעוּ׃{פ}


**The Koren Jerusalem Bible:**
Do not touch any anointed, and do my prophets no harm.


**The Rashi Ketuvim by Rabbi Shraga Silverstein:**
Touch not My anointed ones [i.e., My great ones], and harm not My prophets.


###### I Chronicles 16:23

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/I%20Chronicles/r/16:23](https://torahapp.org/share/book/I%20Chronicles/r/16:23)


שִׁ֤ירוּ לַֽיהֹוָה֙ כׇּל־הָאָ֔רֶץבַּשְּׂר֥וּ מִיּֽוֹם־אֶל־י֖וֹם יְשׁוּעָתֽוֹ׃


**The Koren Jerusalem Bible:**
Sing to the Lord, all the earth; announce his salvation from day to day.


**The Rashi Ketuvim by Rabbi Shraga Silverstein:**
[viz. Psalms 96] Sing to the L-rd all the earth; relate from day to day His salvation.


###### I Chronicles 16:24

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/I%20Chronicles/r/16:24](https://torahapp.org/share/book/I%20Chronicles/r/16:24)


סַפְּר֤וּ בַגּוֹיִם֙ אֶת־כְּבוֹד֔וֹבְּכׇל־הָעַמִּ֖ים נִפְלְאֹתָֽיו׃


**The Koren Jerusalem Bible:**
Declare his glory among the nations; his marvellous works among all peoples.


**The Rashi Ketuvim by Rabbi Shraga Silverstein:**
Tell of His glory among the nations; among all the peoples, (tell) of His wonders.


###### I Chronicles 16:25

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/I%20Chronicles/r/16:25](https://torahapp.org/share/book/I%20Chronicles/r/16:25)


כִּי֩ גָד֨וֹל יְהֹוָ֤ה וּמְהֻלָּל֙ מְאֹ֔דוְנוֹרָ֥א ה֖וּא עַל־כׇּל־אֱלֹהִֽים׃


**The Koren Jerusalem Bible:**
For great is the Lord, and greatly to be praised: and he is to be feared above all gods.


**The Rashi Ketuvim by Rabbi Shraga Silverstein:**
For great is the L-rd and greatly praised; He is awesome above all in heaven.


###### I Chronicles 16:26

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/I%20Chronicles/r/16:26](https://torahapp.org/share/book/I%20Chronicles/r/16:26)


כִּ֠י כׇּל־אֱלֹהֵ֤י הָֽעַמִּים֙ אֱלִילִ֔יםוַיהֹוָ֖ה שָׁמַ֥יִם עָשָֽׂה׃


**The Koren Jerusalem Bible:**
For all the gods of the peoples are idols: but the Lord made the heavens.


**The Rashi Ketuvim by Rabbi Shraga Silverstein:**
For all the gods of the peoples are vain, and the L-rd created the heavens.


###### I Chronicles 16:27

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/I%20Chronicles/r/16:27](https://torahapp.org/share/book/I%20Chronicles/r/16:27)


ה֤וֹד וְהָדָר֙ לְפָנָ֔יועֹ֥ז וְחֶדְוָ֖ה בִּמְקֹמֽוֹ׃


**The Koren Jerusalem Bible:**
Honour and majesty are before him; strength and gladness are in his place.


**The Rashi Ketuvim by Rabbi Shraga Silverstein:**
Majesty and glory [go] before Him; might and joy, in His place.


###### I Chronicles 16:28

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/I%20Chronicles/r/16:28](https://torahapp.org/share/book/I%20Chronicles/r/16:28)


הָב֤וּ לַֽיהֹוָה֙ מִשְׁפְּח֣וֹת עַמִּ֔יםהָב֥וּ לַיהֹוָ֖ה כָּב֥וֹד וָעֹֽז׃


**The Koren Jerusalem Bible:**
Ascribe to the Lord, you families of the peoples, ascribe to the Lord glory and strength.


**The Rashi Ketuvim by Rabbi Shraga Silverstein:**
Accord to the L-rd, you families of the peoples, accord to the L-rd glory and might.


###### I Chronicles 16:29

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/I%20Chronicles/r/16:29](https://torahapp.org/share/book/I%20Chronicles/r/16:29)


הָב֥וּ לַֽיהֹוָ֖ה כְּב֣וֹד שְׁמ֑וֹשְׂא֤וּ מִנְחָה֙ וּבֹ֣אוּ לְפָנָ֔יו הִשְׁתַּחֲו֥וּ לַֽיהֹוָ֖ה בְּהַדְרַת־קֹֽדֶשׁ׃


**The Koren Jerusalem Bible:**
Ascribe to the Lord the glory due to his name: bring an offering, and come before him: worship the Lord in the beauty of holiness.


**The Rashi Ketuvim by Rabbi Shraga Silverstein:**
Accord to the L-rd the glory of His name; raise an offering and come before Him; bow down to the L-rd in the splendor of holiness.


###### I Chronicles 16:30

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/I%20Chronicles/r/16:30](https://torahapp.org/share/book/I%20Chronicles/r/16:30)


חִ֤ילוּ מִלְּפָנָיו֙ כׇּל־הָאָ֔רֶץ אַף־תִּכּ֥וֹן תֵּבֵ֖ל בַּל־תִּמּֽוֹט׃


**The Koren Jerusalem Bible:**
Tremble before him, all the earth: the world also is established, so that it shall not be moved.


**The Rashi Ketuvim by Rabbi Shraga Silverstein:**
Tremble before Him, all the earth! You have also founded the world that it not give way.


###### I Chronicles 16:31

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/I%20Chronicles/r/16:31](https://torahapp.org/share/book/I%20Chronicles/r/16:31)


יִשְׂמְח֤וּ הַשָּׁמַ֙יִם֙ וְתָגֵ֣ל הָאָ֔רֶץ וְיֹאמְר֥וּ בַגּוֹיִ֖ם יְהֹוָ֥ה מָלָֽךְ׃


**The Koren Jerusalem Bible:**
Let the heavens be glad, and let the earth rejoice: and let it be said among the nations, The Lord reigns.


**The Rashi Ketuvim by Rabbi Shraga Silverstein:**
The heavens will rejoice and the earth regale, and they will say among the nations: 'The L-rd reigns!'


###### I Chronicles 16:32

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/I%20Chronicles/r/16:32](https://torahapp.org/share/book/I%20Chronicles/r/16:32)


יִרְעַ֤ם הַיָּם֙ וּמְלוֹא֔וֹ יַעֲלֹ֥ץ הַשָּׂדֶ֖ה וְכׇל־אֲשֶׁר־בּֽוֹ׃


**The Koren Jerusalem Bible:**
Let the sea roar, and the fulness thereof: let the field rejoice, and all that is in it.


**The Rashi Ketuvim by Rabbi Shraga Silverstein:**
The sea will thunder and the fulness thereof [(It will reverberate His praise)]. The field will rejoice and all that is in it.


###### I Chronicles 16:33

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/I%20Chronicles/r/16:33](https://torahapp.org/share/book/I%20Chronicles/r/16:33)


אָ֥ז יְרַנְּנ֖וּ עֲצֵ֣י הַיָּ֑עַר מִלִּפְנֵ֣י יְהֹוָ֔ה כִּי־בָ֖א לִשְׁפּ֥וֹט אֶת־הָאָֽרֶץ׃


**The Koren Jerusalem Bible:**
Then shall the trees of the wood sing for joy at the presence of the Lord, because he comes to judge the earth.


**The Rashi Ketuvim by Rabbi Shraga Silverstein:**
Then, the trees of the forest [(the rulers of the nations)] will sing before the L-rd. For He has come to judge the earth.


###### I Chronicles 16:34

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/I%20Chronicles/r/16:34](https://torahapp.org/share/book/I%20Chronicles/r/16:34)


הוֹד֤וּ לַֽיהֹוָה֙ כִּ֣י ט֔וֹב כִּ֥י לְעוֹלָ֖ם חַסְדּֽוֹ׃


**The Koren Jerusalem Bible:**
O give thanks to the Lord; for he is good; for his steadfast love endures forever.


**The Rashi Ketuvim by Rabbi Shraga Silverstein:**
[viz. Psalms 106:1, 47-48] Give thanks to the L-rd, for He is good; for His lovingkindness endures forever.


###### I Chronicles 16:35

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/I%20Chronicles/r/16:35](https://torahapp.org/share/book/I%20Chronicles/r/16:35)


וְאִמְר֕וּ הוֹשִׁיעֵ֙נוּ֙ אֱלֹהֵ֣י יִשְׁעֵ֔נוּ וְקַבְּצֵ֥נוּ וְהַצִּילֵ֖נוּ מִן־הַגּוֹיִ֑םלְהֹדוֹת֙ לְשֵׁ֣ם קׇדְשֶׁ֔ךָ לְהִשְׁתַּבֵּ֖חַ בִּתְהִלָּתֶֽךָ׃


**The Koren Jerusalem Bible:**
And say, Save us, O God of our salvation, and gather us together, and deliver us from the nations, that we may give thanks to Thy holy name, and glory in Thy praise.


**The Rashi Ketuvim by Rabbi Shraga Silverstein:**
And say: Save us, O L-rd our G-d, and gather us and rescue us from the nations, to give thanks to Your holy name, to glory in Your praise.


###### I Chronicles 16:36

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/I%20Chronicles/r/16:36](https://torahapp.org/share/book/I%20Chronicles/r/16:36)


בָּר֤וּךְ יְהֹוָה֙ אֱלֹהֵ֣י יִשְׂרָאֵ֔ל מִן־הָעוֹלָ֖ם וְעַ֣ד הָעֹלָ֑ם וַיֹּאמְר֤וּ כׇל־הָעָם֙ אָמֵ֔ןוְהַלֵּ֖ל לַיהֹוָֽה׃{פ}


**The Koren Jerusalem Bible:**
Blessed be the Lord God of Yisra᾽el for ever and ever. And all the people said, Amen, and praised the Lord.


**The Rashi Ketuvim by Rabbi Shraga Silverstein:**
Blessed is the L-rd, the G-d of Israel, from the world [i.e., from this world] until the world [to come]." And all the people said "Amen" and [gave] praise to the L-rd.


###### I Chronicles 16:37

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/I%20Chronicles/r/16:37](https://torahapp.org/share/book/I%20Chronicles/r/16:37)


וַיַּעֲזׇב־שָׁ֗ם לִפְנֵי֙ אֲר֣וֹן בְּרִית־יְהֹוָ֔ה לְאָסָ֖ף וּלְאֶחָ֑יו לְשָׁרֵ֞ת לִפְנֵ֧י הָאָר֛וֹן תָּמִ֖יד לִדְבַר־י֥וֹם בְּיוֹמֽוֹ׃


**The Koren Jerusalem Bible:**
So he left there before the ark of the covenant of the Lord Asaf and his brethren, to minister before the ark continually, as every day’s work required:


**The Rashi Ketuvim by Rabbi Shraga Silverstein:**
And he left there, before the ark of the covenant of the L-rd, Asaf and his brothers, to minister before the ark continually, the service of each day in its day,


###### I Chronicles 16:38

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/I%20Chronicles/r/16:38](https://torahapp.org/share/book/I%20Chronicles/r/16:38)


וְעֹבֵ֥ד אֱדֹ֛ם וַאֲחֵיהֶ֖ם שִׁשִּׁ֣ים וּשְׁמוֹנָ֑ה{ס}וְעֹבֵ֨ד אֱדֹ֧ם בֶּן־יְדִית֛וּן וְחֹסָ֖ה לְשֹׁעֲרִֽים׃


**The Koren Jerusalem Bible:**
and ῾Oved-edom with their brethren, sixty eight; and ῾Oved-edom the son of Yeditun and Ḥosa to be doorkeepers:


**The Rashi Ketuvim by Rabbi Shraga Silverstein:**
and Oved-Edom and their brethren, sixty-eight; and Oved-edom the son of Yedithun and Chosah as sentries.


###### I Chronicles 16:39

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/I%20Chronicles/r/16:39](https://torahapp.org/share/book/I%20Chronicles/r/16:39)


וְאֵ֣ת**׀** צָד֣וֹק הַכֹּהֵ֗ן וְאֶחָיו֙ הַכֹּ֣הֲנִ֔ים לִפְנֵ֖י מִשְׁכַּ֣ן יְהֹוָ֑ה בַּבָּמָ֖ה אֲשֶׁ֥ר בְּגִבְעֽוֹן׃


**The Koren Jerusalem Bible:**
and Żadoq the priest, and his brethren the priests, before the tabernacle of the Lord in the high place that was at Giv῾on,


**The Rashi Ketuvim by Rabbi Shraga Silverstein:**
And Tzaddok the priest and his brothers the priests (he placed) before the tabernacle of the L-rd in the high place in Giveon,


###### I Chronicles 16:40

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/I%20Chronicles/r/16:40](https://torahapp.org/share/book/I%20Chronicles/r/16:40)


לְֽהַעֲלוֹת֩ עֹל֨וֹת לַיהֹוָ֜ה עַל־מִזְבַּ֧ח הָעֹלָ֛ה תָּמִ֖יד לַבֹּ֣קֶר וְלָעָ֑רֶב וּלְכׇל־הַכָּתוּב֙ בְּתוֹרַ֣ת יְהֹוָ֔ה אֲשֶׁ֥ר צִוָּ֖ה עַל־יִשְׂרָאֵֽל׃


**The Koren Jerusalem Bible:**
to offer burnt offerings to the Lord upon the altar of the burnt offering continually morning and evening, and to do according to all that is written in the Tora of the Lord, which he commanded Yisra᾽el;


**The Rashi Ketuvim by Rabbi Shraga Silverstein:**
to offer up burnt-offerings to the L-rd on the altar of the burnt-offering, continually, morning and evening, and for all that is written in the Torah of the L-rd, which He commanded to Israel.


###### I Chronicles 16:41

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/I%20Chronicles/r/16:41](https://torahapp.org/share/book/I%20Chronicles/r/16:41)


וְעִמָּהֶם֙ הֵימָ֣ן וִידוּת֔וּן וּשְׁאָר֙ הַבְּרוּרִ֔ים אֲשֶׁ֥ר נִקְּב֖וּ בְּשֵׁמ֑וֹת לְהֹדוֹת֙ לַֽיהֹוָ֔ה כִּ֥י לְעוֹלָ֖ם חַסְדּֽוֹ׃


**The Koren Jerusalem Bible:**
And with them Heman and Yedutun, and the rest who were chosen, who were expressly named, to give thanks to the Lord, because his steadfast love endures forever;


**The Rashi Ketuvim by Rabbi Shraga Silverstein:**
And with them, Heiman and Yeduthun and the rest of the chosen ones singled out by name to give thanks to the L-rd — "for His lovingkindness endures forever!"


###### I Chronicles 16:42

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/I%20Chronicles/r/16:42](https://torahapp.org/share/book/I%20Chronicles/r/16:42)


וְעִמָּהֶם֩ הֵימָ֨ן וִידוּת֜וּן חֲצֹצְר֤וֹת וּמְצִלְתַּ֙יִם֙ לְמַשְׁמִיעִ֔ים וּכְלֵ֖י שִׁ֣יר הָאֱלֹהִ֑ים וּבְנֵ֥י יְדוּת֖וּן לַשָּֽׁעַר׃


**The Koren Jerusalem Bible:**
and with them Heman and Yedutun with trumpets and cymbals for making a loud sound, and with musical instruments of God. And the sons of Yedutun were posted at the gate.


**The Rashi Ketuvim by Rabbi Shraga Silverstein:**
And with them, (with) Heiman and Yeduthun, (were) trumpets and cymbals for sounding, and (other) instruments (for the service) of G-d. And the sons of Yeduthun (were appointed) for the (keeping of) the gate.


###### I Chronicles 16:43

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/I%20Chronicles/r/16:43](https://torahapp.org/share/book/I%20Chronicles/r/16:43)


וַיֵּלְכ֥וּ כׇל־הָעָ֖ם אִ֣ישׁ לְבֵית֑וֹ וַיִּסֹּ֥ב דָּוִ֖יד לְבָרֵ֥ךְ אֶת־בֵּיתֽוֹ׃{פ}


**The Koren Jerusalem Bible:**
And all the people departed every man to his house: and David returned to bless his house.


**The Rashi Ketuvim by Rabbi Shraga Silverstein:**
And all the people went, each to his house, and David returned to bless his household.



## Chapter 17

###### I Chronicles 17:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/I%20Chronicles/r/17:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/I%20Chronicles/r/17:1)


וַיְהִ֕י כַּאֲשֶׁ֛ר יָשַׁ֥ב דָּוִ֖יד בְּבֵית֑וֹ וַיֹּ֨אמֶר דָּוִ֜יד אֶל־נָתָ֣ן הַנָּבִ֗יא הִנֵּ֨ה אָנֹכִ֤י יוֹשֵׁב֙ בְּבֵ֣ית הָאֲרָזִ֔ים וַאֲר֥וֹן בְּרִית־יְהֹוָ֖ה תַּ֥חַת יְרִיעֽוֹת׃


**The Koren Jerusalem Bible:**
Now it came to pass, as David sat in his house, that David said to Natan the prophet, Lo, I dwell in a house of cedar wood, but the ark of the covenant of the Lord remains under curtains.


**The Rashi Ketuvim by Rabbi Shraga Silverstein:**
[viz. II Samuel 7] And it was, when David dwelt in his house, that David said to Nathan the prophet: Behold, I dwell in a house of cedars, and the ark of the L-rd under curtains [of the tent]!


###### I Chronicles 17:2

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/I%20Chronicles/r/17:2](https://torahapp.org/share/book/I%20Chronicles/r/17:2)


וַיֹּ֤אמֶר נָתָן֙ אֶל־דָּוִ֔יד כֹּ֛ל אֲשֶׁ֥ר בִּֽלְבָבְךָ֖ עֲשֵׂ֑ה כִּ֥י הָאֱלֹהִ֖ים עִמָּֽךְ׃{ס}


**The Koren Jerusalem Bible:**
Then Natan said to David, Do all that is in thy heart; for God is with thee.


**The Rashi Ketuvim by Rabbi Shraga Silverstein:**
And Nathan said to David: All that is in your heart, go and do, for G-d is with you.


###### I Chronicles 17:3

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/I%20Chronicles/r/17:3](https://torahapp.org/share/book/I%20Chronicles/r/17:3)


וַיְהִ֖י בַּלַּ֣יְלָה הַה֑וּא וַֽיְהִי֙ דְּבַר־אֱלֹהִ֔ים אֶל־נָתָ֖ן לֵאמֹֽר׃


**The Koren Jerusalem Bible:**
And it came to pass the same night, that the word of God came to Natan, saying,


**The Rashi Ketuvim by Rabbi Shraga Silverstein:**
And it was in that night that the word of G-d came to Nathan, saying:


###### I Chronicles 17:4

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/I%20Chronicles/r/17:4](https://torahapp.org/share/book/I%20Chronicles/r/17:4)


לֵ֤ךְ וְאָֽמַרְתָּ֙ אֶל־דָּוִ֣יד עַבְדִּ֔י כֹּ֖ה אָמַ֣ר יְהֹוָ֑ה לֹ֥א אַתָּ֛ה תִּבְנֶה־לִּ֥י הַבַּ֖יִת לָשָֽׁבֶת׃


**The Koren Jerusalem Bible:**
Go and tell David my servant, Thus says the Lord, Thou shalt not build me a house to dwell in:


**The Rashi Ketuvim by Rabbi Shraga Silverstein:**
Go and say to David, My servant: Thus has the L-rd said: You will not build Me a house to dwell in.


###### I Chronicles 17:5

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/I%20Chronicles/r/17:5](https://torahapp.org/share/book/I%20Chronicles/r/17:5)


כִּ֣י לֹ֤א יָשַׁ֙בְתִּי֙ בְּבַ֔יִת מִן־הַיּ֗וֹם אֲשֶׁ֤ר הֶעֱלֵ֙יתִי֙ אֶת־יִשְׂרָאֵ֔ל עַ֖ד הַיּ֣וֹם הַזֶּ֑ה וָאֶהְיֶ֛ה מֵאֹ֥הֶל אֶל־אֹ֖הֶל וּמִמִּשְׁכָּֽן׃


**The Koren Jerusalem Bible:**
for I have not dwelt in a house since the day that I brought up Yisra᾽el to this day; but have gone from tent to tent, and from one tabernacle to another.


**The Rashi Ketuvim by Rabbi Shraga Silverstein:**
For I have not dwelt in a house from the day that I brought up the children of Israel [from Egypt] until this day, and I was [i.e., went] from tent to tent and from tabernacle [to tabernacle].


###### I Chronicles 17:6

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/I%20Chronicles/r/17:6](https://torahapp.org/share/book/I%20Chronicles/r/17:6)


בְּכֹ֥ל אֲשֶֽׁר־הִתְהַלַּ֘כְתִּי֮ בְּכׇל־יִשְׂרָאֵל֒ הֲדָבָ֣ר דִּבַּ֗רְתִּי אֶת־אַחַד֙ שֹׁפְטֵ֣י יִשְׂרָאֵ֔ל אֲשֶׁ֥ר צִוִּ֛יתִי לִרְע֥וֹת אֶת־עַמִּ֖י לֵאמֹ֑ר לָ֛מָּה לֹא־בְנִיתֶ֥ם לִ֖י בֵּ֥ית אֲרָזִֽים׃


**The Koren Jerusalem Bible:**
Everywhere where I have walked among all Yisra᾽el, did I speak a word to any of the judges of Yisra᾽el whom I commanded to be shepherds to my people, saying, Why have you not built me a house of cedar wood?


**The Rashi Ketuvim by Rabbi Shraga Silverstein:**
Wherever I walked with all [the children of] Israel, did I ever speak this thing with any of the tribes [i.e., judges] of Israel whom I commanded to graze My people, saying: Why did you not build for Me a house of cedars?


###### I Chronicles 17:7

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/I%20Chronicles/r/17:7](https://torahapp.org/share/book/I%20Chronicles/r/17:7)


וְ֠עַתָּ֠ה כֹּֽה־תֹאמַ֞ר לְעַבְדִּ֣י לְדָוִ֗יד{ס}כֹּ֤ה אָמַר֙ יְהֹוָ֣ה צְבָא֔וֹת אֲנִ֤י לְקַחְתִּ֙יךָ֙ מִן־הַנָּוֶ֔ה מִֽן־אַחֲרֵ֖י הַצֹּ֑אן לִֽהְי֣וֹת נָגִ֔יד עַ֖ל עַמִּ֥י יִשְׂרָאֵֽל׃


**The Koren Jerusalem Bible:**
Now therefore thus shalt thou say to my servant David, Thus says the Lord of hosts, I took thee from the pasture, from following the sheep, that thou shouldst be ruler over my people Yisra᾽el:


**The Rashi Ketuvim by Rabbi Shraga Silverstein:**
And now, thus shall you say to My servant David: Thus said the L-rd of hosts: I took you from the sheep-cote, from following the sheep, to be a leader over My people Israel.


###### I Chronicles 17:8

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/I%20Chronicles/r/17:8](https://torahapp.org/share/book/I%20Chronicles/r/17:8)


וָאֶהְיֶ֣ה עִמְּךָ֗ בְּכֹל֙ אֲשֶׁ֣ר הָלַ֔כְתָּ וָאַכְרִ֥ית אֶת־כׇּל־אוֹיְבֶ֖יךָ מִפָּנֶ֑יךָ וְעָשִׂ֤יתִֽי לְךָ֙ שֵׁ֔ם כְּשֵׁ֥ם הַגְּדוֹלִ֖ים אֲשֶׁ֥ר בָּאָֽרֶץ׃


**The Koren Jerusalem Bible:**
and I have been with thee wherever thou hast walked, and have cut off all thy enemies from before thee, and have made thee a name like the name of the great men who are on the earth.


**The Rashi Ketuvim by Rabbi Shraga Silverstein:**
And I was with you wherever you went, and I cut off all your foes before you, [(wherefore [as per Deuteronomy 12:10] it entered your mind to build Me a temple)], and I made you a [great] name [i.e., "the Shield of David"], like the name of the great ones in the land [i.e., "the Shield of Avraham"].


###### I Chronicles 17:9

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/I%20Chronicles/r/17:9](https://torahapp.org/share/book/I%20Chronicles/r/17:9)


וְשַׂמְתִּ֣י מָ֠ק֠וֹם לְעַמִּ֨י יִשְׂרָאֵ֤ל וּנְטַעְתִּ֙יהוּ֙ וְשָׁכַ֣ן תַּחְתָּ֔יו וְלֹ֥א יִרְגַּ֖ז ע֑וֹד וְלֹֽא־יוֹסִ֤יפוּ בְנֵֽי־עַוְלָה֙ לְבַלֹּת֔וֹ כַּאֲשֶׁ֖ר בָּרִאשׁוֹנָֽה׃


**The Koren Jerusalem Bible:**
And I will ordain a place for my people Yisra᾽el, and I will plant them, and they shall dwell in their place, and shall be moved no more; nor shall the children of wickedness waste them any more, as at the beginning,


**The Rashi Ketuvim by Rabbi Shraga Silverstein:**
[(But I desire even more peace before the Temple is built.)] I shall make a place for My people Israel, and I shall plant it. And it [Israel] shall dwell in its place and be vexed no more. And wicked men will no more afflict them as at first [(before the establishment of the judges)]


###### I Chronicles 17:10

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/I%20Chronicles/r/17:10](https://torahapp.org/share/book/I%20Chronicles/r/17:10)


וּלְמִיָּמִ֗ים אֲשֶׁ֨ר צִוִּ֤יתִי שֹֽׁפְטִים֙ עַל־עַמִּ֣י יִשְׂרָאֵ֔ל וְהִכְנַ֖עְתִּי אֶת־כׇּל־אוֹיְבֶ֑יךָ וָאַגִּ֣ד לָ֔ךְ וּבַ֖יִת יִֽבְנֶה־לְּךָ֥ יְהֹוָֽה׃


**The Koren Jerusalem Bible:**
and ever since the time when I commanded judges to be over my people Yisra᾽el. And I will subdue all thy enemies. Moreover I declare to thee that the Lord will build thee a house.


**The Rashi Ketuvim by Rabbi Shraga Silverstein:**
and [(as they have been afflicting them)] from the days that I ordained judges over My people Israel. And I will humble all of your foes. And I tell you that the L-rd will build a house for you.


###### I Chronicles 17:11

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/I%20Chronicles/r/17:11](https://torahapp.org/share/book/I%20Chronicles/r/17:11)


וְהָיָ֗ה כִּֽי־מָלְא֤וּ יָמֶ֙יךָ֙ לָלֶ֣כֶת עִם־אֲבֹתֶ֔יךָ וַהֲקִימוֹתִ֤י אֶֽת־זַרְעֲךָ֙ אַחֲרֶ֔יךָ אֲשֶׁ֥ר יִהְיֶ֖ה מִבָּנֶ֑יךָ וַהֲכִינוֹתִ֖י אֶת־מַלְכוּתֽוֹ׃


**The Koren Jerusalem Bible:**
And it shall come to pass, when thy days are fulfilled when thou must go to be with thy fathers, that I will raise up thy seed after thee, who shall be of thy sons; and I will establish his kingdom.


**The Rashi Ketuvim by Rabbi Shraga Silverstein:**
And it will be, when your days have been filled to go with your fathers, and I raise up your seed after you, who will be of your sons, and I establish his kingdom —


###### I Chronicles 17:12

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/I%20Chronicles/r/17:12](https://torahapp.org/share/book/I%20Chronicles/r/17:12)


ה֥וּא יִבְנֶה־לִּ֖י בָּ֑יִת וְכֹנַנְתִּ֥י אֶת־כִּסְא֖וֹ עַד־עוֹלָֽם׃


**The Koren Jerusalem Bible:**
He shall build me a house, and I will establish his throne for ever.


**The Rashi Ketuvim by Rabbi Shraga Silverstein:**
he will build Me a house, and I will establish his throne forever.


###### I Chronicles 17:13

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/I%20Chronicles/r/17:13](https://torahapp.org/share/book/I%20Chronicles/r/17:13)


אֲנִי֙ אֶֽהְיֶה־לּ֣וֹ לְאָ֔ב וְה֖וּא יִֽהְיֶה־לִּ֣י לְבֵ֑ן וְחַסְדִּי֙ לֹא־אָסִ֣יר מֵעִמּ֔וֹ כַּאֲשֶׁ֣ר הֲסִיר֔וֹתִי מֵאֲשֶׁ֥ר הָיָ֖ה לְפָנֶֽיךָ׃


**The Koren Jerusalem Bible:**
I will be his father, and he will be my son: and I will not take my steadfast love away from him, as I took it from the one who was before thee:


**The Rashi Ketuvim by Rabbi Shraga Silverstein:**
I will be a Father to him and he will be a son to Me. And I will not remove My lovingkindness from him, as I removed it from [Saul] who was before you.


###### I Chronicles 17:14

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/I%20Chronicles/r/17:14](https://torahapp.org/share/book/I%20Chronicles/r/17:14)


וְהַעֲמַדְתִּ֛יהוּ בְּבֵיתִ֥י וּבְמַלְכוּתִ֖י עַד־הָעוֹלָ֑ם וְכִסְא֕וֹ יִהְיֶ֥ה נָכ֖וֹן עַד־עוֹלָֽם׃


**The Koren Jerusalem Bible:**
but I will settle him in my house and in my kingdom forever: and his throne shall be established for evermore.


**The Rashi Ketuvim by Rabbi Shraga Silverstein:**
And I will plant him in My house and in My kingdom forever, and his throne will be established forever.


###### I Chronicles 17:15

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/I%20Chronicles/r/17:15](https://torahapp.org/share/book/I%20Chronicles/r/17:15)


כְּכֹל֙ הַדְּבָרִ֣ים הָאֵ֔לֶּה וּכְכֹ֖ל הֶחָז֣וֹן הַזֶּ֑ה כֵּ֛ן דִּבֶּ֥ר נָתָ֖ן אֶל־דָּוִֽיד׃{פ}


**The Koren Jerusalem Bible:**
According to all these words, and according to all this vision, did Natan speak to David.


**The Rashi Ketuvim by Rabbi Shraga Silverstein:**
According to all of these words and according to all of this vision did Nathan speak with David.


###### I Chronicles 17:16

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/I%20Chronicles/r/17:16](https://torahapp.org/share/book/I%20Chronicles/r/17:16)


וַיָּבֹא֙ הַמֶּ֣לֶךְ דָּוִ֔יד וַיֵּ֖שֶׁב לִפְנֵ֣י יְהֹוָ֑ה וַיֹּ֗אמֶר מִֽי־אֲנִ֞י יְהֹוָ֤ה אֱלֹהִים֙ וּמִ֣י בֵיתִ֔י כִּ֥י הֲבִיאֹתַ֖נִי עַד־הֲלֹֽם׃


**The Koren Jerusalem Bible:**
And David the king came and sat before the Lord, and said, Who am I, O Lord God, and what is my house, that Thou hast brought me so far?


**The Rashi Ketuvim by Rabbi Shraga Silverstein:**
And King David came and he sat before the L-rd [i.e., before the ark], and he said: Who am I, O L-rd, and who [are those of] my house that You have brought me thus far [i.e., to kingdom]?


###### I Chronicles 17:17

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/I%20Chronicles/r/17:17](https://torahapp.org/share/book/I%20Chronicles/r/17:17)


וַתִּקְטַ֨ן זֹ֤את בְּעֵינֶ֙יךָ֙ אֱלֹהִ֔ים וַתְּדַבֵּ֥ר עַל־בֵּֽית־עַבְדְּךָ֖ לְמֵרָח֑וֹק וּרְאִיתַ֗נִי כְּת֧וֹר הָאָדָ֛ם הַֽמַּעֲלָ֖ה יְהֹוָ֥ה אֱלֹהִֽים׃


**The Koren Jerusalem Bible:**
And as though this was too little in Thy eyes, O God, Thou hast also spoken of Thy servant’s house for a great while to come, and hast regarded me as a man of high degree, O Lord God.


**The Rashi Ketuvim by Rabbi Shraga Silverstein:**
And if this were too little in Your eyes, O G-d, You have [also] spoken of the house of Your servant from afar [i.e., to enthrone my son after me]. And You have seen me as a man of high degree, O L-rd G-d.


###### I Chronicles 17:18

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/I%20Chronicles/r/17:18](https://torahapp.org/share/book/I%20Chronicles/r/17:18)


מַה־יּוֹסִ֨יף ע֥וֹד דָּוִ֛יד אֵלֶ֖יךָ לְכָב֣וֹד אֶת־עַבְדֶּ֑ךָ וְאַתָּ֖ה אֶֽת־עַבְדְּךָ֥ יָדָֽעְתָּ׃


**The Koren Jerusalem Bible:**
What more can David say to Thee concerning the honour done to Thy servant? for Thou knowst Thy servant.


**The Rashi Ketuvim by Rabbi Shraga Silverstein:**
What more can David [speak] to You [i.e., What more can I ask] whereby You can honor Your servant, when You know Your servant [i.e., when You know my needs and have filled all of them]?


###### I Chronicles 17:19

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/I%20Chronicles/r/17:19](https://torahapp.org/share/book/I%20Chronicles/r/17:19)


יְהֹוָ֕ה בַּעֲב֤וּר עַבְדְּךָ֙ וּֽכְלִבְּךָ֔ עָשִׂ֕יתָ אֵ֥ת כׇּל־הַגְּדוּלָּ֖ה הַזֹּ֑את לְהֹדִ֖יעַ אֶת־כׇּל־הַגְּדֻלּֽוֹת׃


**The Koren Jerusalem Bible:**
O Lord, for Thy servant’s sake, and according to Thy own heart, hast Thou done all this greatness, in making known all these great things.


**The Rashi Ketuvim by Rabbi Shraga Silverstein:**
O L-rd, for the sake of Your servant and for [the doing of] Your will [(and not for my worthiness)] have You wrought all of this greatness, to apprise [Your servant] of all of these great things.


###### I Chronicles 17:20

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/I%20Chronicles/r/17:20](https://torahapp.org/share/book/I%20Chronicles/r/17:20)


יְהֹוָה֙ אֵ֣ין כָּמ֔וֹךָ וְאֵ֥ין אֱלֹהִ֖ים זוּלָתֶ֑ךָ בְּכֹ֥ל אֲשֶׁר־שָׁמַ֖עְנוּ בְּאׇזְנֵֽינוּ׃


**The Koren Jerusalem Bible:**
O Lord, there is none like Thee, nor is there any God besides Thee, according to all that we have heard with our ears.


**The Rashi Ketuvim by Rabbi Shraga Silverstein:**
O L-rd, there is none like You and there is no god beside You, as all that we have heard with our ears.


###### I Chronicles 17:21

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/I%20Chronicles/r/17:21](https://torahapp.org/share/book/I%20Chronicles/r/17:21)


וּמִי֙ כְּעַמְּךָ֣ יִשְׂרָאֵ֔ל גּ֥וֹי אֶחָ֖ד בָּאָ֑רֶץ אֲשֶׁר֩ הָלַ֨ךְ הָאֱלֹהִ֜ים לִפְדּ֧וֹת ל֣וֹ עָ֗ם לָשׂ֤וּם לְךָ֙ שֵׁ֚ם גְּדֻלּ֣וֹת וְנֹרָא֔וֹת לְגָרֵ֗שׁ מִפְּנֵ֧י עַמְּךָ֛ אֲשֶׁר־פָּדִ֥יתָ מִמִּצְרַ֖יִם גּוֹיִֽם׃


**The Koren Jerusalem Bible:**
And who is like Thy people Yisra᾽el, a singular nation on the earth, whom God went to redeem to be his own people, to make Thee a name of greatness and awesomeness, by driving out nations from before Thy people, whom Thou didst redeem out of Miżrayim?


**The Rashi Ketuvim by Rabbi Shraga Silverstein:**
And who is like Your people Israel, one nation in the land, before whom G-d walked to redeem (them) for Himself as a people, to make for Yourself a name of grandeurs and awesomenesses, to drive from before Your people, whom You redeemed from Egypt — nations.


###### I Chronicles 17:22

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/I%20Chronicles/r/17:22](https://torahapp.org/share/book/I%20Chronicles/r/17:22)


וַ֠תִּתֵּ֠ן אֶת־עַמְּךָ֨ יִשְׂרָאֵ֧ל׀לְךָ֛ לְעָ֖ם עַד־עוֹלָ֑ם וְאַתָּ֣ה יְהֹוָ֔ה הָיִ֥יתָ לָהֶ֖ם לֵאלֹהִֽים׃


**The Koren Jerusalem Bible:**
For Thy people Yisra᾽el didst Thou make Thy own people forever; and Thou, Lord, didst become their God.


**The Rashi Ketuvim by Rabbi Shraga Silverstein:**
And You established Your people Israel for Yourself as a people forever. And You, O L-rd, were a G-d for them.


###### I Chronicles 17:23

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/I%20Chronicles/r/17:23](https://torahapp.org/share/book/I%20Chronicles/r/17:23)


וְעַתָּ֣ה יְהֹוָ֔ה הַדָּבָ֗ר אֲשֶׁ֨ר דִּבַּ֤רְתָּ עַֽל־עַבְדְּךָ֙ וְעַל־בֵּית֔וֹ יֵאָמֵ֖ן עַד־עוֹלָ֑ם וַעֲשֵׂ֖ה כַּאֲשֶׁ֥ר דִּבַּֽרְתָּ׃


**The Koren Jerusalem Bible:**
Therefore now, Lord, let the thing that Thou hast spoken concerning Thy servant and concerning his house be established forever, and do as Thou hast said,


**The Rashi Ketuvim by Rabbi Shraga Silverstein:**
And now, O L-rd G-d, the thing that You spoke for Your servant and for his house, establish (it) forever. And do as You have spoken.


###### I Chronicles 17:24

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/I%20Chronicles/r/17:24](https://torahapp.org/share/book/I%20Chronicles/r/17:24)


וְ֠יֵאָמֵ֠ן וְיִגְדַּ֨ל שִׁמְךָ֤ עַד־עוֹלָם֙ לֵאמֹ֔ר יְהֹוָ֤ה צְבָאוֹת֙ אֱלֹהֵ֣י יִשְׂרָאֵ֔ל אֱלֹהִ֖ים לְיִשְׂרָאֵ֑ל וּבֵית־דָּוִ֥יד עַבְדְּךָ֖ נָכ֥וֹן לְפָנֶֽיךָ׃


**The Koren Jerusalem Bible:**
So let it be established, that Thy name may be magnified forever, saying, The Lord of hosts, the God of Yisra᾽el, is a God to Yisra᾽el: and let the house of David Thy servant be established before Thee.


**The Rashi Ketuvim by Rabbi Shraga Silverstein:**
And let Your name be established and exalted forever, [(men)] saying: The L-rd of hosts, the G-d of Israel, is G-d to Israel, and may the house of David Your servant be established before You.


###### I Chronicles 17:25

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/I%20Chronicles/r/17:25](https://torahapp.org/share/book/I%20Chronicles/r/17:25)


כִּ֣י**׀** אַתָּ֣ה אֱלֹהַ֗י גָּלִ֙יתָ֙ אֶת־אֹ֣זֶן עַבְדְּךָ֔ לִבְנ֥וֹת ל֖וֹ בָּ֑יִת עַל־כֵּן֙ מָצָ֣א עַבְדְּךָ֔ לְהִתְפַּלֵּ֖ל לְפָנֶֽיךָ׃


**The Koren Jerusalem Bible:**
For Thou, O my God, hast told Thy servant that Thou wilt build him a house: therefore Thy servant has found in his heart to pray before Thee.


**The Rashi Ketuvim by Rabbi Shraga Silverstein:**
For You, my G-d, have revealed to Your servant's ear that You would build him a house. Therefore, Your servant found [it in his heart] to pray before You.


###### I Chronicles 17:26

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/I%20Chronicles/r/17:26](https://torahapp.org/share/book/I%20Chronicles/r/17:26)


וְעַתָּ֣ה יְהֹוָ֔ה אַתָּה־ה֖וּא הָאֱלֹהִ֑ים וַתְּדַבֵּר֙ עַֽל־עַבְדְּךָ֔ הַטּוֹבָ֖ה הַזֹּֽאת׃


**The Koren Jerusalem Bible:**
And now, Lord, Thou art God, and hast promised this goodness to Thy servant:


**The Rashi Ketuvim by Rabbi Shraga Silverstein:**
And now, O L-rd, You are G-d [(the Ruler, and You have the power to fulfill)], and You have spoken this good to Your servant.


###### I Chronicles 17:27

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/I%20Chronicles/r/17:27](https://torahapp.org/share/book/I%20Chronicles/r/17:27)


וְעַתָּ֗ה הוֹאַ֙לְתָּ֙ לְבָרֵךְ֙ אֶת־בֵּ֣ית עַבְדְּךָ֔ לִֽהְי֥וֹת לְעוֹלָ֖ם לְפָנֶ֑יךָ כִּֽי־אַתָּ֤ה יְהֹוָה֙ בֵּרַ֔כְתָּ וּמְבֹרָ֖ךְ לְעוֹלָֽם׃{פ}


**The Koren Jerusalem Bible:**
and now it has pleased Thee to bless the house of Thy servant, that it may be before Thee forever: for Thou, O Lord, hast blessed it, and it shall be blessed forever.


**The Rashi Ketuvim by Rabbi Shraga Silverstein:**
And now, deign to bless Your servant's house to be forever before You. For You, O L-rd, have blessed, and [it will be] blessed forever.



## Chapter 18

###### I Chronicles 18:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/I%20Chronicles/r/18:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/I%20Chronicles/r/18:1)


וַֽיְהִי֙ אַֽחֲרֵי־כֵ֔ן וַיַּ֥ךְ דָּוִ֛יד אֶת־פְּלִשְׁתִּ֖ים וַיַּכְנִיעֵ֑ם וַיִּקַּ֛ח אֶת־גַּ֥ת וּבְנֹתֶ֖יהָ מִיַּ֥ד פְּלִשְׁתִּֽים׃


**The Koren Jerusalem Bible:**
Now after this it came to pass, that David smote the Pelishtim, and subdued them, and took Gat and its hamlets out of the hand of the Pelishtim.


**The Rashi Ketuvim by Rabbi Shraga Silverstein:**
[viz. II Samuel 8] And it was after this that David smote the Philistines and humbled them, and he took Gath and its villages from the hand of the Philistines.


###### I Chronicles 18:2

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/I%20Chronicles/r/18:2](https://torahapp.org/share/book/I%20Chronicles/r/18:2)


וַיַּ֖ךְ אֶת־מוֹאָ֑ב וַיִּהְי֤וּ מוֹאָב֙ עֲבָדִ֣ים לְדָוִ֔יד נֹשְׂאֵ֖י מִנְחָֽה׃


**The Koren Jerusalem Bible:**
And he smote Mo᾽av; and Mo᾽av became David’s servants, and brought tribute.


**The Rashi Ketuvim by Rabbi Shraga Silverstein:**
And he smote Moav, and Moav became servants for David for the bearing of gifts.


###### I Chronicles 18:3

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/I%20Chronicles/r/18:3](https://torahapp.org/share/book/I%20Chronicles/r/18:3)


וַיַּ֥ךְ דָּוִ֛יד אֶת־הֲדַדְעֶ֥זֶר מֶלֶךְ־צוֹבָ֖ה חֲמָ֑תָה בְּלֶכְתּ֕וֹ לְהַצִּ֥יב יָד֖וֹ בִּֽנְהַר־פְּרָֽת׃


**The Koren Jerusalem Bible:**
And David smote Hadad῾ezer king of Żova as far as Ḥamat, as he went to set up his dominion by the river Perat.


**The Rashi Ketuvim by Rabbi Shraga Silverstein:**
And David smote Hadadezer the king of Tzovah in Chamath, when he [(Hadadezer)] went to extend his border to the Euphrates River.


###### I Chronicles 18:4

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/I%20Chronicles/r/18:4](https://torahapp.org/share/book/I%20Chronicles/r/18:4)


וַיִּלְכֹּד֩ דָּוִ֨יד מִמֶּ֜נּוּ אֶ֣לֶף רֶ֗כֶב וְשִׁבְעַ֤ת אֲלָפִים֙ פָּרָשִׁ֔ים וְעֶשְׂרִ֥ים אֶ֖לֶף אִ֣ישׁ רַגְלִ֑י וַיְעַקֵּ֤ר דָּוִיד֙ אֶת־כׇּל־הָרֶ֔כֶב וַיּוֹתֵ֥ר מִמֶּ֖נּוּ מֵ֥אָה רָֽכֶב׃


**The Koren Jerusalem Bible:**
And David took from him one thousand chariots, and seven thousand horsemen, and twenty thousand footmen: and David lamed all the chariot horses, but reserved from them enough for a hundred chariots.


**The Rashi Ketuvim by Rabbi Shraga Silverstein:**
And David took from him one thousand chariots and seven thousand riders and twenty thousand footmen. And David hamstrung all of the chariot [-horses (because of the interdict against a king's multiplying horses [viz. Deuteronomy 17:16])], and he left over of them [(for his personal use)] one hundred chariot [-teams of horses, i.e., four hundred horses].


###### I Chronicles 18:5

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/I%20Chronicles/r/18:5](https://torahapp.org/share/book/I%20Chronicles/r/18:5)


וַיָּבֹא֙ אֲרַ֣ם דַּרְמֶ֔שֶׂק לַעְז֕וֹר לַהֲדַדְעֶ֖זֶר מֶ֣לֶךְ צוֹבָ֑ה וַיַּ֤ךְ דָּוִיד֙ בַּאֲרָ֔ם עֶשְׂרִֽים־וּשְׁנַ֥יִם אֶ֖לֶף אִֽישׁ׃


**The Koren Jerusalem Bible:**
And when Aram of Darmeseq came to help Hadad῾ezer king of Żova, David slew of Aram twenty two thousand men.


**The Rashi Ketuvim by Rabbi Shraga Silverstein:**
And [the men of] Aram-darmesek came to aid Hadadezer, king of Tzovah, and David smote of Aram twenty-two thousand men.


###### I Chronicles 18:6

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/I%20Chronicles/r/18:6](https://torahapp.org/share/book/I%20Chronicles/r/18:6)


וַיָּ֤שֶׂם דָּוִיד֙ בַּאֲרַ֣ם דַּרְמֶ֔שֶׂק וַיְהִ֤י אֲרָם֙ לְדָוִ֔יד עֲבָדִ֖ים נֹשְׂאֵ֣י מִנְחָ֑ה וַיּ֤וֹשַׁע יְהֹוָה֙ לְדָוִ֔יד בְּכֹ֖ל אֲשֶׁ֥ר הָלָֽךְ׃


**The Koren Jerusalem Bible:**
Then David put garrisons in Aram of Darmeseq; and the people of Aram became David’s servants, and brought tribute. Thus the Lord preserved David wherever he went.


**The Rashi Ketuvim by Rabbi Shraga Silverstein:**
And David placed tax collectors in Aram-darmesek, and (the men of) Aram were servants for David to bear gifts. And the L-rd helped David in all that he undertook.


###### I Chronicles 18:7

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/I%20Chronicles/r/18:7](https://torahapp.org/share/book/I%20Chronicles/r/18:7)


וַיִּקַּ֣ח דָּוִ֗יד אֵ֚ת שִׁלְטֵ֣י הַזָּהָ֔ב אֲשֶׁ֣ר הָי֔וּ עַ֖ל עַבְדֵ֣י הֲדַדְעָ֑זֶר וַיְבִיאֵ֖ם יְרוּשָׁלָֽ͏ִם׃


**The Koren Jerusalem Bible:**
And David took the shields of gold that were on the servants of Hadad῾ezer, and brought them to Yerushalayim.


**The Rashi Ketuvim by Rabbi Shraga Silverstein:**
And David took the golden quivers which were on the servants of Hadadezer and he brought them to Jerusalem.


###### I Chronicles 18:8

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/I%20Chronicles/r/18:8](https://torahapp.org/share/book/I%20Chronicles/r/18:8)


וּמִטִּבְחַ֤ת וּמִכּוּן֙ עָרֵ֣י הֲדַדְעֶ֔זֶר לָקַ֥ח דָּוִ֛יד נְחֹ֖שֶׁת רַבָּ֣ה מְאֹ֑ד בָּ֣הּ**׀** עָשָׂ֣ה שְׁלֹמֹ֗ה אֶת־יָ֤ם הַנְּחֹ֙שֶׁת֙ וְאֶת־הָֽעַמּוּדִ֔ים וְאֵ֖ת כְּלֵ֥י הַנְּחֹֽשֶׁת׃{פ}


**The Koren Jerusalem Bible:**
Likewise from Tivĥat, and from Kun, cities of Hadad῾ezer, David took very much brass, with which Shelomo made the brazen sea, and the pillars, and the vessels of brass.


**The Rashi Ketuvim by Rabbi Shraga Silverstein:**
And from Tivchath and Kun, cities of Hadadezer, David took very much copper, with which Solomon made the copper Sea and the pillars and the copper vessels.


###### I Chronicles 18:9

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/I%20Chronicles/r/18:9](https://torahapp.org/share/book/I%20Chronicles/r/18:9)


וַיִּשְׁמַ֕ע תֹּ֖עוּ מֶ֣לֶךְ חֲמָ֑ת כִּ֚י הִכָּ֣ה דָוִ֔יד אֶת־כׇּל־חֵ֖יל הֲדַדְעֶ֥זֶר מֶלֶךְ־צוֹבָֽה׃


**The Koren Jerusalem Bible:**
Now when To῾u king of Ḥamat heard how David had smitten all the host of Hadad῾ezer king of Żova;


**The Rashi Ketuvim by Rabbi Shraga Silverstein:**
And To'i, the king of Chamath, heard that David had defeated the entire army of Hadadezer, king of Tzovah.


###### I Chronicles 18:10

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/I%20Chronicles/r/18:10](https://torahapp.org/share/book/I%20Chronicles/r/18:10)


וַיִּשְׁלַ֣ח אֶת־הֲדֽוֹרָם־בְּנ֣וֹ אֶל־הַמֶּֽלֶךְ־דָּ֠וִ֠יד (לשאול) [לִשְׁאׇל־]ל֨וֹ לְשָׁל֜וֹם וּֽלְבָרְכ֗וֹ עַל֩ אֲשֶׁ֨ר נִלְחַ֤ם בַּהֲדַדְעֶ֙זֶר֙ וַיַּכֵּ֔הוּ כִּי־אִ֛ישׁ מִלְחֲמ֥וֹת תֹּ֖עוּ הָיָ֣ה הֲדַדְעָ֑זֶר וְכֹ֗ל כְּלֵ֛י זָהָ֥ב וָכֶ֖סֶף וּנְחֹֽשֶׁת׃


**The Koren Jerusalem Bible:**
he sent Hadoram his son to king David, to inquire of his welfare, and to congratulate him, because he had fought against Hadad῾ezer, and smitten him; (for Hadad῾ezer had wars with To῾u;) and he had with him all manner of vessels of gold and silver and brass.


**The Rashi Ketuvim by Rabbi Shraga Silverstein:**
And he sent Hadoram his son to King David to greet him and to bless him for having warred with and smitten Hadadezer (Hadadezer having been Toi's (arch) war rival); and [Hadoram brought] all [manner of] vessels of gold and silver and copper.


###### I Chronicles 18:11

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/I%20Chronicles/r/18:11](https://torahapp.org/share/book/I%20Chronicles/r/18:11)


גַּם־אֹתָ֗ם הִקְדִּ֞ישׁ הַמֶּ֤לֶךְ דָּוִיד֙ לַֽיהֹוָ֔ה עִם־הַכֶּ֙סֶף֙ וְהַזָּהָ֔ב אֲשֶׁ֥ר נָשָׂ֖א מִכׇּל־הַגּוֹיִ֑ם מֵאֱד֤וֹם וּמִמּוֹאָב֙ וּמִבְּנֵ֣י עַמּ֔וֹן וּמִפְּלִשְׁתִּ֖ים וּמֵעֲמָלֵֽק׃


**The Koren Jerusalem Bible:**
These also did king David dedicate to the Lord, with the silver and the gold that he carried away from all these nations; from Edom, and from Mo᾽av, and from the children of ῾Ammon, and from the Pelishtim, and from ῾Amaleq.


**The Rashi Ketuvim by Rabbi Shraga Silverstein:**
These, too, did King David dedicate to the [(temple of the) L-rd with the silver and the gold that he had taken from all the nations [that he had conquered]: from Edom and from Moav and from the sons of Ammon and from the Philistines and from Amalek.


###### I Chronicles 18:12

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/I%20Chronicles/r/18:12](https://torahapp.org/share/book/I%20Chronicles/r/18:12)


וְאַבְשַׁ֣י בֶּן־צְרוּיָ֗ה הִכָּ֤ה אֶת־אֱדוֹם֙ בְּגֵ֣יא הַמֶּ֔לַח שְׁמוֹנָ֥ה עָשָׂ֖ר אָֽלֶף׃


**The Koren Jerusalem Bible:**
Moreover Avshay the son of Żeruya slew of Edom in the valley of salt eighteen thousand.


**The Rashi Ketuvim by Rabbi Shraga Silverstein:**
And Avshai the son of Tzeruyah smote Edom in the valley of Salt, eighteen thousand (men).


###### I Chronicles 18:13

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/I%20Chronicles/r/18:13](https://torahapp.org/share/book/I%20Chronicles/r/18:13)


וַיָּ֤שֶׂם בֶּאֱדוֹם֙ נְצִיבִ֔ים וַיִּהְי֥וּ כׇל־אֱד֖וֹם עֲבָדִ֣ים לְדָוִ֑יד וַיּ֤וֹשַׁע יְהֹוָה֙ אֶת־דָּוִ֔יד בְּכֹ֖ל אֲשֶׁ֥ר הָלָֽךְ׃


**The Koren Jerusalem Bible:**
And he put garrisons in Edom; and all Edom became David’s servants. Thus the Lord preserved David wherever he went.


**The Rashi Ketuvim by Rabbi Shraga Silverstein:**
And he placed tax collectors in Edom. And all (the men of) Edom were servants to David. And the L-rd helped David in all that he undertook.


###### I Chronicles 18:14

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/I%20Chronicles/r/18:14](https://torahapp.org/share/book/I%20Chronicles/r/18:14)


וַיִּמְלֹ֥ךְ דָּוִ֖יד עַל־כׇּל־יִשְׂרָאֵ֑ל וַיְהִ֗י עֹשֶׂ֛ה מִשְׁפָּ֥ט וּצְדָקָ֖ה לְכׇל־עַמּֽוֹ׃


**The Koren Jerusalem Bible:**
So David reigned over all Yisra᾽el, and executed judgment and justice among all his people.


**The Rashi Ketuvim by Rabbi Shraga Silverstein:**
And David ruled over all of Israel. And David dispensed justice and charity for all of his people.


###### I Chronicles 18:15

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/I%20Chronicles/r/18:15](https://torahapp.org/share/book/I%20Chronicles/r/18:15)


וְיוֹאָ֥ב בֶּן־צְרוּיָ֖ה עַל־הַצָּבָ֑א וִיהוֹשָׁפָ֥ט בֶּן־אֲחִיל֖וּד מַזְכִּֽיר׃


**The Koren Jerusalem Bible:**
And Yo᾽av the son of Żeruya was over the host; and Yehoshafat the son of Aĥilud, was recorder.


**The Rashi Ketuvim by Rabbi Shraga Silverstein:**
And Yoav the son of Tzeruyah was over the army [i.e., Because David dispensed justice and charity, Yoav succeeded as a commander; and because Yoav succeeded in the implementation of justice, David succeeded in its administration], and Yehoshafat the son of Achilud was recorder [(of the order of judgments to come before David)].


###### I Chronicles 18:16

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/I%20Chronicles/r/18:16](https://torahapp.org/share/book/I%20Chronicles/r/18:16)


וְצָד֧וֹק בֶּן־אֲחִיט֛וּב וַאֲבִימֶ֥לֶךְ בֶּן־אֶבְיָתָ֖ר כֹּהֲנִ֑ים וְשַׁוְשָׁ֖א סוֹפֵֽר׃


**The Koren Jerusalem Bible:**
And Żadoq the son of Aĥituv, and Avimelekh the son of Evyatar, were the priests; and Shavsha was scribe;


**The Rashi Ketuvim by Rabbi Shraga Silverstein:**
And Tzaddok the son of Achituv and Avimelech the son of Evyathar were priests [(in official capacities)], and Shavsha was the scribe.


###### I Chronicles 18:17

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/I%20Chronicles/r/18:17](https://torahapp.org/share/book/I%20Chronicles/r/18:17)


וּבְנָיָ֙הוּ֙ בֶּן־יְה֣וֹיָדָ֔ע עַל־הַכְּרֵתִ֖י וְהַפְּלֵתִ֑י וּבְנֵי־דָוִ֥יד הָרִֽאשֹׁנִ֖ים לְיַ֥ד הַמֶּֽלֶךְ׃{פ}


**The Koren Jerusalem Bible:**
and Benayahu the son of Yehoyada was over the Kereti and the Peleti; and the sons of David were chief ministers about the king.


**The Rashi Ketuvim by Rabbi Shraga Silverstein:**
And Benayahu the son of Yehoyada was (over) the archers and the slingers. And the sons of David were the first at the hand of the king.



## Chapter 19

###### I Chronicles 19:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/I%20Chronicles/r/19:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/I%20Chronicles/r/19:1)


וַֽיְהִי֙ אַחֲרֵי־כֵ֔ן וַיָּ֕מׇת נָחָ֖שׁ מֶ֣לֶךְ בְּנֵֽי־עַמּ֑וֹן וַיִּמְלֹ֥ךְ בְּנ֖וֹ תַּחְתָּֽיו׃


**The Koren Jerusalem Bible:**
Now it came to pass after this, that Naĥash the king of the children of ῾Ammon died, and his son reigned in his stead.


**The Rashi Ketuvim by Rabbi Shraga Silverstein:**
[viz. II Samuel 10] And it was after this that Nachash, the king of the children of Ammon died, and his son reigned after him.


###### I Chronicles 19:2

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/I%20Chronicles/r/19:2](https://torahapp.org/share/book/I%20Chronicles/r/19:2)


וַיֹּ֨אמֶר דָּוִ֜יד אֶעֱשֶׂה־חֶ֣סֶד**׀** עִם־חָנ֣וּן בֶּן־נָחָ֗שׁ כִּֽי־עָשָׂ֨ה אָבִ֤יו עִמִּי֙ חֶ֔סֶד וַיִּשְׁלַ֥ח דָּוִ֛יד מַלְאָכִ֖ים לְנַחֲמ֣וֹ עַל־אָבִ֑יו וַיָּבֹ֩אוּ֩ עַבְדֵ֨י דָוִ֜יד אֶל־אֶ֧רֶץ בְּנֵֽי־עַמּ֛וֹן אֶל־חָנ֖וּן לְנַחֲמֽוֹ׃


**The Koren Jerusalem Bible:**
And David said, I will deal loyally with Ḥanun the son of Naĥash, because his father dealt loyally with me. And David sent messengers to comfort him concerning his father. So the servants of David came into the land of the children of ῾Ammon to Ḥanun, to comfort him.


**The Rashi Ketuvim by Rabbi Shraga Silverstein:**
And David said: I will do lovingkindness with Chanun the son of Nachash, for his father did lovingkindness with me [viz. I Samuel 22:4 (The king of Moav killed David's family, except one who was granted asylum by Nachash)]. And David sent messengers to console him for [the loss of] his father. And the servants of David came to the land of the children of Ammon.


###### I Chronicles 19:3

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/I%20Chronicles/r/19:3](https://torahapp.org/share/book/I%20Chronicles/r/19:3)


וַיֹּאמְרוּ֩ שָׂרֵ֨י בְנֵי־עַמּ֜וֹן לְחָנ֗וּן הַֽמְכַבֵּ֨ד דָּוִ֤יד אֶת־אָבִ֙יךָ֙ בְּעֵינֶ֔יךָ כִּֽי־שָׁלַ֥ח לְךָ֖ מְנַחֲמִ֑ים הֲלֹ֡א בַּ֠עֲב֠וּר לַחְקֹ֨ר וְלַהֲפֹ֤ךְ וּלְרַגֵּל֙ הָאָ֔רֶץ בָּ֥אוּ עֲבָדָ֖יו אֵלֶֽיךָ׃


**The Koren Jerusalem Bible:**
But the princes of the children of ῾Ammon said to Ḥanun, Thinkst thou that David honours thy father, that he has sent comforters to thee? are not his servants come to thee to search, and to overthrow, and to spy out the land?


**The Rashi Ketuvim by Rabbi Shraga Silverstein:**
And the officers of the children of Ammon said to Chanun: Is David honoring your father in your eyes by sending you consolers? [(Israel is exhorted (Deuteronomy 23:7): "Do not seek their (Ammon and Moav's) peace." And he is seeking your peace!)] Is it not to search out and to turn over and to spy out the land that his servants have come to you?


###### I Chronicles 19:4

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/I%20Chronicles/r/19:4](https://torahapp.org/share/book/I%20Chronicles/r/19:4)


וַיִּקַּ֨ח חָנ֜וּן אֶת־עַבְדֵ֤י דָוִיד֙ וַֽיְגַלְּחֵ֔ם וַיִּכְרֹ֧ת אֶת־מַדְוֵיהֶ֛ם בַּחֵ֖צִי עַד־הַמִּפְשָׂעָ֑ה וַֽיְשַׁלְּחֵֽם׃


**The Koren Jerusalem Bible:**
So Ḥanun took David’s servants, and shaved them, and cut off their garments in the middle to their hips, and sent them away.


**The Rashi Ketuvim by Rabbi Shraga Silverstein:**
And Chanun took the servants of David and shaved [half of their beards] and cut off half their garments until the buttocks and sent them away.


###### I Chronicles 19:5

וַיֵּלְכוּ֩ וַיַּגִּ֨ידוּ לְדָוִ֤יד עַל־הָֽאֲנָשִׁים֙ וַיִּשְׁלַ֣ח לִקְרָאתָ֔ם כִּֽי־הָי֥וּ הָאֲנָשִׁ֖ים נִכְלָמִ֣ים מְאֹ֑ד וַיֹּ֤אמֶר הַמֶּ֙לֶךְ֙ שְׁב֣וּ בִֽירֵח֔וֹ עַ֛ד אֲשֶׁר־יְצַמַּ֥ח זְקַנְכֶ֖ם וְשַׁבְתֶּֽם׃{ס}


**The Koren Jerusalem Bible:**

**The Rashi Ketuvim by Rabbi Shraga Silverstein:**
And they went and told David about the men. And he sent to them (for the men were greatly ashamed) and the king said: Remain in Jericho until your beards grow and then return.


###### I Chronicles 19:6

וַיִּרְאוּ֙ בְּנֵ֣י עַמּ֔וֹן כִּ֥י הִֽתְבָּאֲשׁ֖וּ עִם־דָּוִ֑יד וַיִּשְׁלַ֣ח חָ֠נ֠וּן וּבְנֵ֨י עַמּ֜וֹן אֶ֣לֶף כִּכַּר־כֶּ֗סֶף לִשְׂכֹּ֣ר לָ֠הֶ֠ם מִן־אֲרַ֨ם נַהֲרַ֜יִם וּמִן־אֲרַ֤ם מַֽעֲכָה֙ וּמִצּוֹבָ֔ה רֶ֖כֶב וּפָרָשִֽׁים׃


**The Koren Jerusalem Bible:**

**The Rashi Ketuvim by Rabbi Shraga Silverstein:**

## Chapter 20

###### I Chronicles 20:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/I%20Chronicles/r/20:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/I%20Chronicles/r/20:1)


וַיְהִ֡י לְעֵת֩ תְּשׁוּבַ֨ת הַשָּׁנָ֜ה לְעֵ֣ת**׀** צֵ֣את הַמְּלָכִ֗ים וַיִּנְהַ֣ג יוֹאָב֩ אֶת־חֵ֨יל הַצָּבָ֜א וַיַּשְׁחֵ֣ת**׀** אֶת־אֶ֣רֶץ בְּנֵי־עַמּ֗וֹן וַיָּבֹא֙ וַיָּ֣צַר אֶת־רַבָּ֔ה וְדָוִ֖יד יֹשֵׁ֣ב בִּירוּשָׁלָ֑͏ִם וַיַּ֥ךְ יוֹאָ֛ב אֶת־רַבָּ֖ה וַיֶּהֶרְסֶֽהָ׃


**The Koren Jerusalem Bible:**
And it came to pass, at the time of the return of the year, at the time when the kings go out to battle, that Yo᾽av led forth the power of the army, and ravaged the country of the children of ῾Ammon, and came and besieged Rabba. But David remained at Yerushalayim. And Yo᾽av smote Rabba, and destroyed it.


**The Rashi Ketuvim by Rabbi Shraga Silverstein:**
And it was, at the return of the (day of the) year of the kings' having gone out [to war against Israel] that Yoav led the army, and he destroyed the land of the children of Ammon, and he came and besieged Rabbah; but David stayed in Jerusalem. And Yoav smote Rabbah and destroyed it.


###### I Chronicles 20:2

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/I%20Chronicles/r/20:2](https://torahapp.org/share/book/I%20Chronicles/r/20:2)


וַיִּקַּ֣ח דָּוִ֣יד אֶת־עֲטֶֽרֶת־מַלְכָּם֩ מֵעַ֨ל רֹאשׁ֜וֹ וַֽיִּמְצָאָ֣הּ**׀** מִשְׁקַ֣ל כִּכַּר־זָהָ֗ב וּבָהּ֙ אֶ֣בֶן יְקָרָ֔ה וַתְּהִ֖י עַל־רֹ֣אשׁ דָּוִ֑יד וּשְׁלַ֥ל הָעִ֛יר הוֹצִ֖יא הַרְבֵּ֥ה מְאֹֽד׃


**The Koren Jerusalem Bible:**
And David took the crown of their king from off his head, and found it to weigh a talent of gold, and there were precious stones in it; and it was set upon David’s head: and he also brought very much spoil out of the city.


**The Rashi Ketuvim by Rabbi Shraga Silverstein:**
And David took the crown of Malkam [(Molech, the abomination of Ammon)] off its head (it was found to weigh a talent of gold and in it was a precious stone), and [(its idolatrous status having been nullified by Ittai the Gittite)], it was (set) on David's head. And he brought out the spoils of the city in great abundance.


###### I Chronicles 20:3

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/I%20Chronicles/r/20:3](https://torahapp.org/share/book/I%20Chronicles/r/20:3)


וְאֶת־הָעָ֨ם אֲשֶׁר־בָּ֜הּ הוֹצִ֗יא וַיָּ֨שַׂר בַּמְּגֵרָ֜ה וּבַחֲרִיצֵ֤י הַבַּרְזֶל֙ וּבַמְּגֵר֔וֹת וְכֵן֙ יַעֲשֶׂ֣ה דָוִ֔יד לְכֹ֖ל עָרֵ֣י בְנֵי־עַמּ֑וֹן וַיָּ֧שׇׁב דָּוִ֛יד וְכׇל־הָעָ֖ם יְרוּשָׁלָֽ͏ִם׃{ס}


**The Koren Jerusalem Bible:**
And he brought out the people who were in it, and set them to work with saws, and with harrows of iron, and with axes. Even so did David deal with all the cities of the children of ῾Ammon. And David and all the people returned to Yerushalayim.


**The Rashi Ketuvim by Rabbi Shraga Silverstein:**
And the people in it he took out and put them under saws and under sledges of iron and under axes. And thus did David do with all the cities of the children of Ammon. And David and all of the people returned to Jerusalem.


###### I Chronicles 20:4

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/I%20Chronicles/r/20:4](https://torahapp.org/share/book/I%20Chronicles/r/20:4)


וַֽיְהִי֙ אַחֲרֵי־כֵ֔ן וַתַּעֲמֹ֧ד מִלְחָמָ֛ה בְּגֶ֖זֶר עִם־פְּלִשְׁתִּ֑ים אָ֣ז הִכָּ֞ה סִבְּכַ֣י הַחֻשָׁתִ֗י אֶת־סִפַּ֛י מִילִידֵ֥י הָרְפָאִ֖ים וַיִּכָּנֵֽעוּ׃


**The Koren Jerusalem Bible:**
And it came to pass after this that there arose war at Gezer with the Pelishtim; at which time Sibbekhay the Ḥushatite slew Sippay, that was of the children of the Rafa; and they were subdued.


**The Rashi Ketuvim by Rabbi Shraga Silverstein:**
And it was after this that war broke out in Gezer with the Philistines. Then Sivchai the Chushathite smote Sippai, of those begotten by the Refaim, and they were humbled.


###### I Chronicles 20:5

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/I%20Chronicles/r/20:5](https://torahapp.org/share/book/I%20Chronicles/r/20:5)


וַתְּהִי־ע֥וֹד מִלְחָמָ֖ה אֶת־פְּלִשְׁתִּ֑ים וַיַּ֞ךְ אֶלְחָנָ֣ן בֶּן־[יָעִ֗יר] (יעור) אֶת־לַחְמִי֙ אֲחִי֙ גׇּלְיָ֣ת הַגִּתִּ֔י וְעֵ֣ץ חֲנִית֔וֹ כִּמְנ֖וֹר אֹרְגִֽים׃{ס}


**The Koren Jerusalem Bible:**
And there was war again with the Pelishtim; and Elĥanan the son of Ya᾽ir slew Laĥmi the brother of Golyat the Gittite, the staff of whose spear was like a weaver’s beam.


**The Rashi Ketuvim by Rabbi Shraga Silverstein:**
And there was another war with the Philistines, in which Elchanan the son of Yair smote Lachmi, the brother of Goliath the Gittite, the haft of whose spear was as (thick as) a weaver's beam.


###### I Chronicles 20:6

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/I%20Chronicles/r/20:6](https://torahapp.org/share/book/I%20Chronicles/r/20:6)


וַתְּהִי־ע֥וֹד מִלְחָמָ֖ה בְּגַ֑ת וַיְהִ֣י**׀** אִ֣ישׁ מִדָּ֗ה וְאֶצְבְּעֹתָ֤יו שֵׁשׁ־וָשֵׁשׁ֙ עֶשְׂרִ֣ים וְאַרְבַּ֔ע וְגַם־ה֖וּא נוֹלַ֥ד לְהָרָפָֽא׃


**The Koren Jerusalem Bible:**
And yet again there was war at Gat, where there was a man of great stature, whose fingers and toes were twenty four, six on each hand, and six on each foot: and he also was the son of the Rafa.


**The Rashi Ketuvim by Rabbi Shraga Silverstein:**
And there was anther war in Gath where there was a man of great stature, his fingers [(the fingers of his hands and the toes of his feet)] being six and six [(i.e., six on each of the four limbs)], twenty-four (all together). And he, too, had been born to the Raphah.


###### I Chronicles 20:7

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/I%20Chronicles/r/20:7](https://torahapp.org/share/book/I%20Chronicles/r/20:7)


וַיְחָרֵ֖ף אֶת־יִשְׂרָאֵ֑ל וַיַּכֵּ֙הוּ֙ יְה֣וֹנָתָ֔ן בֶּן־שִׁמְעָ֖א אֲחִ֥י דָוִֽיד׃


**The Koren Jerusalem Bible:**
And he insulted Yisra᾽el, so Yehonatan the son of Shim῾a, David’s brother, slew him.


**The Rashi Ketuvim by Rabbi Shraga Silverstein:**
And he taunted Israel. And he was smitten by Jonathan the son of Shima, the brother of David.

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/I%20Chronicles/r/20:8](https://torahapp.org/share/book/I%20Chronicles/r/20:8)


אֵ֛ל נוּלְּד֥וּ לְהָרָפָ֖א בְּגַ֑ת וַיִּפְּל֥וּ בְיַד־דָּוִ֖יד וּבְיַד־עֲבָדָֽיו׃{פ}


**The Koren Jerusalem Bible:**
These were born to the Rafa in Gat; and they fell by the hand of David, and by the hand of his servants.


**The Rashi Ketuvim by Rabbi Shraga Silverstein:**
These were born to the Raphah in Gath. And they fell by the hand of David and by the hand of his servants.



## Chapter 21

###### I Chronicles 21:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/I%20Chronicles/r/21:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/I%20Chronicles/r/21:1)


וַיַּעֲמֹ֥ד שָׂטָ֖ן עַל־יִשְׂרָאֵ֑ל וַיָּ֙סֶת֙ אֶת־דָּוִ֔יד לִמְנ֖וֹת אֶת־יִשְׂרָאֵֽל׃


**The Koren Jerusalem Bible:**
And an adversary angel stood up against Yisra᾽el, and provoked David to number Yisra᾽el.


**The Rashi Ketuvim by Rabbi Shraga Silverstein:**
[viz. II Samuel 24] And Satan rose against Israel and incited David to count Israel.


###### I Chronicles 21:2

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/I%20Chronicles/r/21:2](https://torahapp.org/share/book/I%20Chronicles/r/21:2)


וַיֹּ֨אמֶר דָּוִ֤יד אֶל־יוֹאָב֙ וְאֶל־שָׂרֵ֣י הָעָ֔ם לְכ֗וּ סִפְרוּ֙ אֶת־יִשְׂרָאֵ֔ל מִבְּאֵ֥ר שֶׁ֖בַע וְעַד־דָּ֑ן וְהָבִ֣יאוּ אֵלַ֔י וְאֵדְעָ֖ה אֶת־מִסְפָּרָֽם׃


**The Koren Jerusalem Bible:**
And David said to Yo᾽av and to the rulers of the people, Go, number Yisra᾽el from Be᾽er-sheva to Dan; and bring the number of them to me, that I may know it.


**The Rashi Ketuvim by Rabbi Shraga Silverstein:**
And David said to Yoav and to the officers of the people: Go and count Israel from Beersheva until Dan that I may know their number.


###### I Chronicles 21:3

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/I%20Chronicles/r/21:3](https://torahapp.org/share/book/I%20Chronicles/r/21:3)


וַיֹּ֣אמֶר יוֹאָ֗ב יוֹסֵף֩ יְהֹוָ֨ה עַל־עַמּ֤וֹ׀כָּהֵם֙ מֵאָ֣ה פְעָמִ֔ים הֲלֹא֙ אֲדֹנִ֣י הַמֶּ֔לֶךְ כֻּלָּ֥ם לַאדֹנִ֖י לַעֲבָדִ֑ים לָ֣מָּה יְבַקֵּ֥שׁ זֹאת֙ אֲדֹנִ֔י לָ֛מָּה יִֽהְיֶ֥ה לְאַשְׁמָ֖ה לְיִשְׂרָאֵֽל׃


**The Koren Jerusalem Bible:**
And Yo᾽av answered, The Lord make his people a hundred times so many more as they are: but, my lord the king, are they not all my lord’s servants? why then does my lord require this thing? why will he be a cause of trespass to Yisra᾽el?


**The Rashi Ketuvim by Rabbi Shraga Silverstein:**
And Yoav said: May the L-rd add to His people, like them, one hundred times. Are they not, my lord the king, all servants of my lord? Why does my lord desire this [(to number the people)], for the guilt of Israel?


###### I Chronicles 21:4

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/I%20Chronicles/r/21:4](https://torahapp.org/share/book/I%20Chronicles/r/21:4)


וּדְבַר־הַמֶּ֖לֶךְ חָזַ֣ק עַל־יוֹאָ֑ב וַיֵּצֵ֣א יוֹאָ֗ב וַיִּתְהַלֵּךְ֙ בְּכׇל־יִשְׂרָאֵ֔ל וַיָּבֹ֖א יְרוּשָׁלָֽ͏ִם׃


**The Koren Jerusalem Bible:**
But the king’s word prevailed against Yo᾽av. So Yo᾽av departed, and went throughout all Yisra᾽el, and came to Yerushalayim.


**The Rashi Ketuvim by Rabbi Shraga Silverstein:**
But the word of the king prevailed against Yoav. And Yoav went, and he traversed all of Israel, and he came to Jerusalem.


###### I Chronicles 21:5

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/I%20Chronicles/r/21:5](https://torahapp.org/share/book/I%20Chronicles/r/21:5)


וַיִּתֵּ֥ן יוֹאָ֛ב אֶת־מִסְפַּ֥ר מִפְקַד־הָעָ֖ם אֶל־דָּוִ֑יד וַיְהִ֣י כׇֽל־יִשְׂרָאֵ֡ל אֶ֣לֶף אֲלָפִים֩ וּמֵאָ֨ה אֶ֤לֶף אִישׁ֙ שֹׁ֣לֵֽף חֶ֔רֶב וִיהוּדָ֕ה אַרְבַּע֩ מֵא֨וֹת וְשִׁבְעִ֥ים אֶ֛לֶף אִ֖ישׁ שֹׁ֥לֵֽף־חָֽרֶב׃


**The Koren Jerusalem Bible:**
And Yo᾽av gave the sum of the number of the people to David. In all Yisra᾽el there were one million one hundred thousand men that drew sword: and Yehuda was four hundred and seventy thousand men that drew sword.


**The Rashi Ketuvim by Rabbi Shraga Silverstein:**
And Yoav gave the count of the people to the king. And all of Israel were a thousand thousands and one hundred thousand men that drew the sword; and Judah, five hundred thousand men that drew the sword.


###### I Chronicles 21:6

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/I%20Chronicles/r/21:6](https://torahapp.org/share/book/I%20Chronicles/r/21:6)

**The Koren Jerusalem Bible:**
But he did not count Levi and Binyamin among them: for the king’s word was abhorrent to Yo᾽av.


**The Rashi Ketuvim by Rabbi Shraga Silverstein:**
And Levi and Benjamin he did not count among them, for the word of the king [(to count the people)] was repugnant to Yoav.


###### I Chronicles 21:7

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/I%20Chronicles/r/21:7](https://torahapp.org/share/book/I%20Chronicles/r/21:7)


וַיֵּ֙רַע֙ בְּעֵינֵ֣י הָאֱלֹהִ֔ים עַל־הַדָּבָ֖ר הַזֶּ֑ה וַיַּ֖ךְ אֶת־יִשְׂרָאֵֽל׃{ס}


**The Koren Jerusalem Bible:**
And God was displeased with this thing; therefore he smote Yisra᾽el.


**The Rashi Ketuvim by Rabbi Shraga Silverstein:**
And this thing [(the count)] was evil in the eyes of the L-rd, and He smote Israel.


###### I Chronicles 21:8

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/I%20Chronicles/r/21:8](https://torahapp.org/share/book/I%20Chronicles/r/21:8)


וַיֹּ֤אמֶר דָּוִיד֙ אֶל־הָ֣אֱלֹהִ֔ים חָטָ֣אתִֽי מְאֹ֔ד אֲשֶׁ֥ר עָשִׂ֖יתִי אֶת־הַדָּבָ֣ר הַזֶּ֑ה וְעַתָּ֗ה הַֽעֲבֶר־נָא֙ אֶת־עֲו֣וֹן עַבְדְּךָ֔ כִּ֥י נִסְכַּ֖לְתִּי מְאֹֽד׃{פ}


**The Koren Jerusalem Bible:**
And David said to God, I have sinned greatly, because I have done this thing: but now, I pray thee, take away the iniquity of Thy servant; for I have done very foolishly.


**The Rashi Ketuvim by Rabbi Shraga Silverstein:**
And David said to G-d: I have sinned greatly in having done this thing. And now, remove, I pray You, the sin of Your servant, for I have been very foolish.


###### I Chronicles 21:9

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/I%20Chronicles/r/21:9](https://torahapp.org/share/book/I%20Chronicles/r/21:9)


וַיְדַבֵּ֤ר יְהֹוָה֙ אֶל־גָּ֔ד חֹזֵ֥ה דָוִ֖יד לֵאמֹֽר׃


**The Koren Jerusalem Bible:**
And the Lord spoke to Gad, David’s seer, saying,


**The Rashi Ketuvim by Rabbi Shraga Silverstein:**
And the L-rd spoke to Gad, David's seer, saying:


###### I Chronicles 21:10

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/I%20Chronicles/r/21:10](https://torahapp.org/share/book/I%20Chronicles/r/21:10)


לֵךְ֩ וְדִבַּרְתָּ֨ אֶל־דָּוִ֜יד לֵאמֹ֗ר כֹּ֚ה אָמַ֣ר יְהֹוָ֔ה שָׁל֕וֹשׁ אֲנִ֖י נֹטֶ֣ה עָלֶ֑יךָ בְּחַר־לְךָ֛ אַחַ֥ת מֵהֵ֖נָּה וְאֶעֱשֶׂה־לָּֽךְ׃


**The Koren Jerusalem Bible:**
Go and tell David, saying, Thus says the Lord, I offer thee three things: choose one of them, that I may do it to thee.


**The Rashi Ketuvim by Rabbi Shraga Silverstein:**
Go and speak to David: Thus has the L-rd said: I give you three [choices (corresponding to those you "gave" Saul [viz. I Samuel 26:10])]. Pick one of them and I will do it to you.


###### I Chronicles 21:11

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/I%20Chronicles/r/21:11](https://torahapp.org/share/book/I%20Chronicles/r/21:11)


וַיָּ֥בֹא גָ֖ד אֶל־דָּוִ֑יד וַיֹּ֥אמֶר ל֛וֹ כֹּה־אָמַ֥ר יְהֹוָ֖ה קַבֶּל־לָֽךְ׃


**The Koren Jerusalem Bible:**
So Gad came to David, and said to him, Thus say the Lord, Choose,


**The Rashi Ketuvim by Rabbi Shraga Silverstein:**
And Gad came to David and said to him: Thus has the L-rd said; take it upon you:


###### I Chronicles 21:12

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/I%20Chronicles/r/21:12](https://torahapp.org/share/book/I%20Chronicles/r/21:12)


אִם־שָׁל֨וֹשׁ שָׁנִ֜ים רָעָ֗ב וְאִם־שְׁלֹשָׁ֨ה חֳדָשִׁ֜ים נִסְפֶּ֥ה מִפְּנֵֽי־צָרֶ֘יךָ֮ וְחֶ֣רֶב אוֹיְבֶ֣יךָ׀לְמַשֶּׂ֒גֶת֒ וְאִם־שְׁלֹ֣שֶׁת יָ֠מִ֠ים חֶ֣רֶב יְהֹוָ֤ה וְדֶ֙בֶר֙ בָּאָ֔רֶץ וּמַלְאַ֣ךְ יְהֹוָ֔ה מַשְׁחִ֖ית בְּכׇל־גְּב֣וּל יִשְׂרָאֵ֑ל וְעַתָּ֣ה רְאֵ֔ה מָה־אָשִׁ֥יב אֶת־שֹׁלְחִ֖י דָּבָֽר׃{ס}


**The Koren Jerusalem Bible:**
either three years famine; or three months to be driven away before thy foes, while the sword of thy enemies overtakes thee; or else three days of the Lord’s sword, namely, pestilence in the land, and the angel of the Lord destroying throughout all the border of Yisra᾽el. Now therefore consider what word I shall bring back to him who sent me.


**The Rashi Ketuvim by Rabbi Shraga Silverstein:**
Either three years of famine, or three months in which you perish at the hand of your foes, the sword of your foes overtaking (you), or three days of the sword of the L-rd, pestilence in the land, and the angel of the L-rd destroying in all the border of Israel. And now, see, what answer shall I return to my Sender?


###### I Chronicles 21:13

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/I%20Chronicles/r/21:13](https://torahapp.org/share/book/I%20Chronicles/r/21:13)


וַיֹּ֧אמֶר דָּוִ֛יד אֶל־גָּ֖ד צַר־לִ֣י מְאֹ֑ד אֶפְּלָה־נָּ֣א בְיַד־יְהֹוָ֗ה כִּֽי־רַבִּ֤ים רַחֲמָיו֙ מְאֹ֔ד וּבְיַד־אָדָ֖ם אַל־אֶפֹּֽל׃


**The Koren Jerusalem Bible:**
And David said to Gad, I am in great distress: let me fall rather into the hand of the Lord; for very great are his mercies: but let me not fall into the hand of man.


**The Rashi Ketuvim by Rabbi Shraga Silverstein:**
And David said to Gad: I am sorely oppressed. Let me fall now into the hand of the L-rd [(i.e., pestilence, which is entirely in the hand of the L-rd, but not sword or famine, which, to a certain extent, is mediated by man)], for His mercies are very many, but let me not fall into the hand of man.


###### I Chronicles 21:14

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/I%20Chronicles/r/21:14](https://torahapp.org/share/book/I%20Chronicles/r/21:14)


וַיִּתֵּ֧ן יְהֹוָ֛ה דֶּ֖בֶר בְּיִשְׂרָאֵ֑ל וַיִּפֹּל֙ מִיִּשְׂרָאֵ֔ל שִׁבְעִ֥ים אֶ֖לֶף אִֽישׁ׃


**The Koren Jerusalem Bible:**
So the Lord sent a plague upon Yisra᾽el: and there fell of Yisra᾽el seventy thousand men.


**The Rashi Ketuvim by Rabbi Shraga Silverstein:**
And the L-rd sent a pestilence upon Israel. And there died of Israel seventy thousand men.


###### I Chronicles 21:15

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/I%20Chronicles/r/21:15](https://torahapp.org/share/book/I%20Chronicles/r/21:15)


וַיִּשְׁלַח֩ הָאֱלֹהִ֨ים׀מַלְאָ֥ךְ׀לִֽירוּשָׁלַ֘͏ִם֮ לְהַשְׁחִיתָהּ֒ וּכְהַשְׁחִ֗ית רָאָ֤ה יְהֹוָה֙ וַיִּנָּ֣חֶם עַל־הָרָעָ֔ה וַיֹּ֨אמֶר לַמַּלְאָ֤ךְ הַמַּשְׁחִית֙ רַ֔ב עַתָּ֖ה הֶ֣רֶף יָדֶ֑ךָ וּמַלְאַ֤ךְ יְהֹוָה֙ עֹמֵ֔ד עִם־גֹּ֖רֶן אׇרְנָ֥ן הַיְבוּסִֽי׃{ס}


**The Koren Jerusalem Bible:**
And God sent an angel to Yerushalayim to destroy it: and as he was about to destroy, the Lord beheld, and he relented of the evil, and said to the angel that destroyed, It is enough, now hold thy hand. And the angel of the Lord stood by the threshing floor of Ornan the Yevusite.


**The Rashi Ketuvim by Rabbi Shraga Silverstein:**
And G-d sent an angel to Jerusalem to destroy it. And, as he destroyed, the L-rd saw and He bethought Himself of the evil. And He said to the angel devastating the people: "Enough! Now stay your hand!" And the angel of the L-rd was standing by the threshing floor of Arnan the Yevusite.


###### I Chronicles 21:16

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/I%20Chronicles/r/21:16](https://torahapp.org/share/book/I%20Chronicles/r/21:16)


וַיִּשָּׂ֨א דָוִ֜יד אֶת־עֵינָ֗יו וַיַּ֞רְא אֶת־מַלְאַ֤ךְ יְהֹוָה֙ עֹמֵ֗ד בֵּ֤ין הָאָ֙רֶץ֙ וּבֵ֣ין הַשָּׁמַ֔יִם וְחַרְבּ֤וֹ שְׁלוּפָה֙ בְּיָד֔וֹ נְטוּיָ֖ה עַל־יְרוּשָׁלָ֑͏ִם וַיִּפֹּ֨ל דָּוִ֧יד וְהַזְּקֵנִ֛ים מְכֻסִּ֥ים בַּשַּׂקִּ֖ים עַל־פְּנֵיהֶֽם׃


**The Koren Jerusalem Bible:**
And David lifted up his eyes, and saw the angel of the Lord standing between the earth and the heaven, with a drawn sword in his hand stretched out over Yerushalayim. Then David and the elders, who were clothed in sackcloth, fell upon their faces.


**The Rashi Ketuvim by Rabbi Shraga Silverstein:**
And David lifted his eyes, and he saw the angel of the L-rd standing between earth and heaven with his sword drawn in his hand, stretched over Jerusalem. And David and the elders fell, covered with sackcloth, on their faces.


###### I Chronicles 21:17

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/I%20Chronicles/r/21:17](https://torahapp.org/share/book/I%20Chronicles/r/21:17)


וַיֹּ֣אמֶר דָּוִ֣יד אֶֽל־הָאֱלֹהִ֡ים הֲלֹא֩ אֲנִ֨י אָמַ֜רְתִּי לִמְנ֣וֹת בָּעָ֗ם וַאֲנִי־ה֤וּא אֲשֶׁר־חָטָ֙אתִי֙ וְהָרֵ֣עַ הֲרֵע֔וֹתִי וְאֵ֥לֶּה הַצֹּ֖אן מֶ֣ה עָשׂ֑וּ יְהֹוָ֣ה אֱלֹהַ֗י תְּהִ֨י נָ֤א יָֽדְךָ֙ בִּ֚י וּבְבֵ֣ית אָבִ֔י וּֽבְעַמְּךָ֖ לֹ֥א לְמַגֵּפָֽה׃{ס}


**The Koren Jerusalem Bible:**
And David said to God, Is it not I who commanded the people to be numbered? so that it is I who have sinned and done very wickedly; but as for these sheep, what have they done? let Thy hand, I pray Thee, O Lord my God, be on me, and on my father’s house; but not on Thy people, that they should be plagued.


**The Rashi Ketuvim by Rabbi Shraga Silverstein:**
And David said to G-d: Was it not I who said to count the people? It is I, then, who have sinned and who have done the evil. And these sheep, what did they do? Let Your hand be against me and against my father's house, but not against Your people, for plague!


###### I Chronicles 21:18

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/I%20Chronicles/r/21:18](https://torahapp.org/share/book/I%20Chronicles/r/21:18)


וּמַלְאַ֧ךְ יְהֹוָ֛ה אָמַ֥ר אֶל־גָּ֖ד לֵאמֹ֣ר לְדָוִ֑יד כִּ֣י**׀** יַעֲלֶ֣ה דָוִ֗יד לְהָקִ֤ים מִזְבֵּ֙חַ֙ לַֽיהֹוָ֔ה בְּגֹ֖רֶן אׇרְנָ֥ן הַיְבֻסִֽי׃


**The Koren Jerusalem Bible:**
Then the angel of the Lord commanded Gad to say to David, that David should go up, and set up an altar to the Lord on the threshing floor of Ornan the Yevusite.


**The Rashi Ketuvim by Rabbi Shraga Silverstein:**
And an angel of the L-rd said to Gad to tell David that David should go up to erect an altar to the L-rd in the threshing floor of Arnan the Yevusite.


###### I Chronicles 21:19

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/I%20Chronicles/r/21:19](https://torahapp.org/share/book/I%20Chronicles/r/21:19)


וַיַּ֤עַל דָּוִיד֙ בִּדְבַר־גָּ֔ד אֲשֶׁ֥ר דִּבֶּ֖ר בְּשֵׁ֥ם יְהֹוָֽה׃


**The Koren Jerusalem Bible:**
And David went up at the saying of Gad, which he spoke in the name of the Lord.


**The Rashi Ketuvim by Rabbi Shraga Silverstein:**
And David went up by the word of Gad that he had spoken in the name of the L-rd.


###### I Chronicles 21:20

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/I%20Chronicles/r/21:20](https://torahapp.org/share/book/I%20Chronicles/r/21:20)


וַיָּ֣שׇׁב אׇרְנָ֗ן וַיַּרְא֙ אֶת־הַמַּלְאָ֔ךְ וְאַרְבַּ֧עַת בָּנָ֛יו עִמּ֖וֹ מִֽתְחַבְּאִ֑ים וְאׇרְנָ֖ן דָּ֥שׁ חִטִּֽים׃


**The Koren Jerusalem Bible:**
And Ornan turned back, and saw the angel; and his four sons with him hid themselves. Now Ornan was threshing wheat.


**The Rashi Ketuvim by Rabbi Shraga Silverstein:**
And Arnan returned, and he saw the angel. And his four sons, with him, hid themselves. (And Arnan had been threshing wheat.)


###### I Chronicles 21:21

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/I%20Chronicles/r/21:21](https://torahapp.org/share/book/I%20Chronicles/r/21:21)


וַיָּבֹ֥א דָוִ֖יד עַד־אׇרְנָ֑ן וַיַּבֵּ֤ט אׇרְנָן֙ וַיַּ֣רְא אֶת־דָּוִ֔יד וַיֵּצֵא֙ מִן־הַגֹּ֔רֶן וַיִּשְׁתַּ֧חוּ לְדָוִ֛יד אַפַּ֖יִם אָֽרְצָה׃


**The Koren Jerusalem Bible:**
And as David came to Ornan, Ornan looked and saw David, and went out of the threshing floor, and bowed himself to David with his face to the ground.


**The Rashi Ketuvim by Rabbi Shraga Silverstein:**
And David came towards Arnan. And Arnan looked and he saw David. And he went out from the threshing floor and he bowed down to David with his face to the ground.


###### I Chronicles 21:22

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/I%20Chronicles/r/21:22](https://torahapp.org/share/book/I%20Chronicles/r/21:22)


וַיֹּ֨אמֶר דָּוִ֜יד אֶל־אׇרְנָ֗ן תְּנָה־לִּי֙ מְק֣וֹם הַגֹּ֔רֶן וְאֶבְנֶה־בּ֥וֹ מִזְבֵּ֖חַ לַֽיהֹוָ֑ה בְּכֶ֤סֶף מָלֵא֙ תְּנֵ֣הוּ לִ֔י וְתֵעָצַ֥ר הַמַּגֵּפָ֖ה מֵעַ֥ל הָעָֽם׃


**The Koren Jerusalem Bible:**
Then David said to Ornan, Grant me the place of this threshing floor, that I may build an altar on it to the Lord: thou shalt give it me for the full price: that the plague may be stayed from the people.


**The Rashi Ketuvim by Rabbi Shraga Silverstein:**
And David said to Arnan: Give me the place of the threshing floor, and I will build in it an altar to the L-rd. Give it to me for its full price, and the plague will cease from the people.


###### I Chronicles 21:23

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/I%20Chronicles/r/21:23](https://torahapp.org/share/book/I%20Chronicles/r/21:23)


וַיֹּ֨אמֶר אׇרְנָ֤ן אֶל־דָּוִיד֙ קַֽח־לָ֔ךְ וְיַ֛עַשׂ אֲדֹנִ֥י הַמֶּ֖לֶךְ הַטּ֣וֹב בְּעֵינָ֑יו רְאֵה֩ נָתַ֨תִּי הַבָּקָ֜ר לָעֹל֗וֹת וְהַמּוֹרִגִּ֧ים לָעֵצִ֛ים וְהַחִטִּ֥ים לַמִּנְחָ֖ה הַכֹּ֥ל נָתָֽתִּי׃


**The Koren Jerusalem Bible:**
And Ornan said to David, Take it to thee, and let my lord the king do that which is good in his eyes: lo, I give thee the oxen also for burnt offerings, and the threshing instruments for wood, and the wheat for the meal offering; I give it all.


**The Rashi Ketuvim by Rabbi Shraga Silverstein:**
And Arnan said to David: Take it for yourself, and let my lord the king do what is good in his eyes. See, I have given the oxen for burnt-offerings and the threshing tools for [fire-] wood, and the wheat for the meal-offering. I have given all.


###### I Chronicles 21:24

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/I%20Chronicles/r/21:24](https://torahapp.org/share/book/I%20Chronicles/r/21:24)


וַיֹּ֨אמֶר הַמֶּ֤לֶךְ דָּוִיד֙ לְאׇרְנָ֔ן לֹ֕א כִּֽי־קָנֹ֥ה אֶקְנֶ֖ה בְּכֶ֣סֶף מָלֵ֑א כִּ֠י לֹא־אֶשָּׂ֤א אֲשֶׁר־לְךָ֙ לַיהֹוָ֔ה וְהַעֲל֥וֹת עוֹלָ֖ה חִנָּֽם׃


**The Koren Jerusalem Bible:**
And king David said to Ornan, No; but I will surely buy it for the full price: for I will not take that which is thine for the Lord, nor offer burnt offerings without payment.


**The Rashi Ketuvim by Rabbi Shraga Silverstein:**
And King David said to Arnan: No, but I will buy (it from you) at full price. For I will not take what is yours for the L-rd and offer up a burnt-offering for nothing.


###### I Chronicles 21:25

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/I%20Chronicles/r/21:25](https://torahapp.org/share/book/I%20Chronicles/r/21:25)


וַיִּתֵּ֥ן דָּוִ֛יד לְאׇרְנָ֖ן בַּמָּק֑וֹם שִׁקְלֵ֣י זָהָ֔ב מִשְׁקָ֖ל שֵׁ֥שׁ מֵאֽוֹת׃


**The Koren Jerusalem Bible:**
So David gave to Ornan for the place six hundred shekels of gold by weight.


**The Rashi Ketuvim by Rabbi Shraga Silverstein:**
And David gave to Arnan for the place shekels of gold, six hundred by weight.


###### I Chronicles 21:26

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/I%20Chronicles/r/21:26](https://torahapp.org/share/book/I%20Chronicles/r/21:26)


וַיִּ֩בֶן֩ שָׁ֨ם דָּוִ֤יד מִזְבֵּ֙חַ֙ לַֽיהֹוָ֔ה וַיַּ֥עַל עֹל֖וֹת וּשְׁלָמִ֑ים וַיִּקְרָא֙ אֶל־יְהֹוָ֔ה וַֽיַּעֲנֵ֤הוּ בָאֵשׁ֙ מִן־הַשָּׁמַ֔יִם עַ֖ל מִזְבַּ֥ח הָעֹלָֽה׃{ס}


**The Koren Jerusalem Bible:**
And David built there an altar to the Lord, and offered burnt offerings and peace offerings, and called upon the Lord; and he answered him from heaven by fire upon the altar of burnt offering.


**The Rashi Ketuvim by Rabbi Shraga Silverstein:**
And David built there an altar to the L-rd, and he offered up burnt-offerings and peace-offerings. And he called to the L-rd, and He answered him with fire from heaven on the altar of the burnt-offering.


###### I Chronicles 21:27

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/I%20Chronicles/r/21:27](https://torahapp.org/share/book/I%20Chronicles/r/21:27)


וַיֹּ֤אמֶר יְהֹוָה֙ לַמַּלְאָ֔ךְ וַיָּ֥שֶׁב חַרְבּ֖וֹ אֶל־נְדָנָֽהּ׃


**The Koren Jerusalem Bible:**
And the Lord commanded the angel; and he put up his sword again into its sheath.


**The Rashi Ketuvim by Rabbi Shraga Silverstein:**
And the L-rd spoke to the angel, and he returned his sword to its sheath.


###### I Chronicles 21:28

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/I%20Chronicles/r/21:28](https://torahapp.org/share/book/I%20Chronicles/r/21:28)


בָּעֵ֣ת הַהִ֔יא בִּרְא֤וֹת דָּוִיד֙ כִּֽי־עָנָ֣הוּ יְהֹוָ֔ה בְּגֹ֖רֶן אׇרְנָ֣ן הַיְבוּסִ֑י וַיִּזְבַּ֖ח שָֽׁם׃


**The Koren Jerusalem Bible:**
At that time when David saw that the Lord had answered him on the threshing floor of Ornan the Yevusite, then he sacrificed there.


**The Rashi Ketuvim by Rabbi Shraga Silverstein:**
At that time, when David saw that the L-rd had answered him in the threshing floor of Arnan the Yevusite, he sacrificed (i.e., he continued to sacrifice) there.


###### I Chronicles 21:29

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/I%20Chronicles/r/21:29](https://torahapp.org/share/book/I%20Chronicles/r/21:29)


וּמִשְׁכַּ֣ן יְ֠הֹוָ֠ה אֲשֶׁר־עָשָׂ֨ה מֹשֶׁ֧ה בַמִּדְבָּ֛ר וּמִזְבַּ֥ח הָעוֹלָ֖ה בָּעֵ֣ת הַהִ֑יא בַּבָּמָ֖ה בְּגִבְעֽוֹן׃


**The Koren Jerusalem Bible:**
But the tabernacle of the Lord, which Moshe made in the wilderness, and the altar of the burnt offering, were at that time in the high place at Giv῾on.


**The Rashi Ketuvim by Rabbi Shraga Silverstein:**
And the tabernacle of the L-rd, which Moses had made in the desert, and the altar of the burnt-offering, at that time, were at the high place in Giveon.


###### I Chronicles 21:30

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/I%20Chronicles/r/21:30](https://torahapp.org/share/book/I%20Chronicles/r/21:30)


וְלֹא־יָכֹ֥ל דָּוִ֛יד לָלֶ֥כֶת לְפָנָ֖יו לִדְרֹ֣שׁ אֱלֹהִ֑ים כִּ֣י נִבְעַ֔ת מִפְּנֵ֕י חֶ֖רֶב מַלְאַ֥ךְ יְהֹוָֽה׃{ס}


**The Koren Jerusalem Bible:**
And David could not go before it to inquire of God: for he was terrified because of the sword of the angel of the Lord.


**The Rashi Ketuvim by Rabbi Shraga Silverstein:**
But David could not go before it to solicit G-d, for he feared the sword of the angel of the L-rd.



## Chapter 22

###### I Chronicles 22:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/I%20Chronicles/r/22:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/I%20Chronicles/r/22:1)


וַיֹּ֣אמֶר דָּוִ֔יד זֶ֣ה ה֔וּא בֵּ֖ית יְהֹוָ֣ה הָאֱלֹהִ֑ים וְזֶה־מִּזְבֵּ֥חַ לְעֹלָ֖ה לְיִשְׂרָאֵֽל׃{פ}


**The Koren Jerusalem Bible:**
Then David said, This is the house of the Lord God, and this is the altar of the burnt offering for Yisra᾽el.


**The Rashi Ketuvim by Rabbi Shraga Silverstein:**
And David said: This is the house of the L-rd G-d and this is the altar for the burnt-offering of Israel.


###### I Chronicles 22:2

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/I%20Chronicles/r/22:2](https://torahapp.org/share/book/I%20Chronicles/r/22:2)


וַיֹּ֣אמֶר דָּוִ֔יד לִכְנוֹס֙ אֶת־הַגֵּרִ֔ים אֲשֶׁ֖ר בְּאֶ֣רֶץ יִשְׂרָאֵ֑ל וַיַּעֲמֵ֣ד חֹצְבִ֗ים לַחְצוֹב֙ אַבְנֵ֣י גָזִ֔ית לִבְנ֖וֹת בֵּ֥ית הָאֱלֹהִֽים׃


**The Koren Jerusalem Bible:**
And David commanded to gather together the strangers who were in the land of Yisra᾽el and he set masons to hew dressed stones to build the house of God.


**The Rashi Ketuvim by Rabbi Shraga Silverstein:**
And David ordered to gather the strangers who were in Eretz Yisrael, and he set up hewers to hew wrought stone to build the house of G-d.


###### I Chronicles 22:3

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/I%20Chronicles/r/22:3](https://torahapp.org/share/book/I%20Chronicles/r/22:3)


וּבַרְזֶ֣ל׀לָ֠רֹ֠ב לַֽמִּסְמְרִ֞ים לְדַלְת֧וֹת הַשְּׁעָרִ֛ים וְלַֽמְחַבְּר֖וֹת הֵכִ֣ין דָּוִ֑יד וּנְחֹ֥שֶׁת לָרֹ֖ב אֵ֥ין מִשְׁקָֽל׃


**The Koren Jerusalem Bible:**
And David prepared iron in abundance for the nails for the doors of the gates, and for the joinings; and brass in abundance without weight;


**The Rashi Ketuvim by Rabbi Shraga Silverstein:**
And David prepared iron in abundance for the nails for the doors of the gates and for the couplings, and copper in abundance, without weight [i.e., beggaring weight].


###### I Chronicles 22:4

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/I%20Chronicles/r/22:4](https://torahapp.org/share/book/I%20Chronicles/r/22:4)


וַעֲצֵ֥י אֲרָזִ֖ים לְאֵ֣ין מִסְפָּ֑ר כִּֽי־הֵ֠בִ֠יאוּ הַצִּידֹנִ֨ים וְהַצֹּרִ֜ים עֲצֵ֧י אֲרָזִ֛ים לָרֹ֖ב לְדָוִֽיד׃{פ}


**The Koren Jerusalem Bible:**
and cedar trees in abundance: for the men of Żidon and of Żor brought much cedar wood to David.


**The Rashi Ketuvim by Rabbi Shraga Silverstein:**
And cedar trees without numbers; for the Tziddonites and the Tzorites brought cedar trees in abundance to David.


###### I Chronicles 22:5

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/I%20Chronicles/r/22:5](https://torahapp.org/share/book/I%20Chronicles/r/22:5)


וַיֹּ֣אמֶר דָּוִ֗יד שְׁלֹמֹ֣ה בְנִי֮ נַ֣עַר וָרָךְ֒ וְהַבַּ֜יִת לִבְנ֣וֹת לַֽיהֹוָ֗ה לְהַגְדִּ֨יל׀לְמַ֜עְלָה לְשֵׁ֤ם וּלְתִפְאֶ֙רֶת֙ לְכׇל־הָ֣אֲרָצ֔וֹת אָכִ֥ינָה נָּ֖א ל֑וֹ וַיָּ֧כֶן דָּוִ֛יד לָרֹ֖ב לִפְנֵ֥י מוֹתֽוֹ׃


**The Koren Jerusalem Bible:**
And David said, Shelomo my son is young and tender, and the house that is to be built for the Lord must be exceedingly magnificent, of fame and of glory throughout all lands: I will therefore now make preparation for it. So David provided abundant materials before his death.


**The Rashi Ketuvim by Rabbi Shraga Silverstein:**
And David said: Solomon my son is young and untried and the house must be built for the L-rd, to be greatly exalted for fame and glory through all the lands. I shall prepare it for him. And David prepared (it) abundantly before his death.


###### I Chronicles 22:6

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/I%20Chronicles/r/22:6](https://torahapp.org/share/book/I%20Chronicles/r/22:6)


וַיִּקְרָ֖א לִשְׁלֹמֹ֣ה בְנ֑וֹ וַיְצַוֵּ֙הוּ֙ לִבְנ֣וֹת בַּ֔יִת לַיהֹוָ֖ה אֱלֹהֵ֥י יִשְׂרָאֵֽל׃{ס}


**The Koren Jerusalem Bible:**
Then he called for Shelomo his son, and charged him to build a house for the Lord God of Yisra᾽el.


**The Rashi Ketuvim by Rabbi Shraga Silverstein:**
And David called to Solomon his son and commanded him to build a house to the L-rd, the G-d of Israel.

**The Koren Jerusalem Bible:**
And David said to Shelomo, My son, as for me, it was in my mind to build a house to the name of the Lord my God:


**The Rashi Ketuvim by Rabbi Shraga Silverstein:**
And David said to Solomon his son: I — it was in my heart to build a house for the name of the L-rd my G-d.


###### I Chronicles 22:8

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/I%20Chronicles/r/22:8](https://torahapp.org/share/book/I%20Chronicles/r/22:8)


וַיְהִ֨י עָלַ֤י דְּבַר־יְהֹוָה֙ לֵאמֹ֔ר דָּ֤ם לָרֹב֙ שָׁפַ֔כְתָּ וּמִלְחָמ֥וֹת גְּדֹל֖וֹת עָשִׂ֑יתָ לֹא־תִבְנֶ֥ה בַ֙יִת֙ לִשְׁמִ֔י כִּ֚י דָּמִ֣ים רַבִּ֔ים שָׁפַ֥כְתָּ אַ֖רְצָה לְפָנָֽי׃
