but the word of the Lord came to me, saying, Thou hast shed blood abundantly, and hast made great wars: thou shalt not build a house to my name, because thou hast shed much blood upon the earth in my sight.


**The Rashi Ketuvim by Rabbi Shraga Silverstein:**
And the word of the L-rd came to me, saying: "Blood in abundance did you spill, and great wars did you wage. You will not build a house for My name, for blood in abundance did you spill to the ground before Me.


###### I Chronicles 22:9

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/I%20Chronicles/r/22:9](https://torahapp.org/share/book/I%20Chronicles/r/22:9)


הִנֵּה־בֵ֞ן נוֹלָ֣ד לָ֗ךְ ה֤וּא יִֽהְיֶה֙ אִ֣ישׁ מְנוּחָ֔ה וַהֲנִיח֥וֹתִי ל֛וֹ מִכׇּל־אוֹיְבָ֖יו מִסָּבִ֑יב כִּ֤י שְׁלֹמֹה֙ יִֽהְיֶ֣ה שְׁמ֔וֹ וְשָׁל֥וֹם וָשֶׁ֛קֶט אֶתֵּ֥ן עַל־יִשְׂרָאֵ֖ל בְּיָמָֽיו׃


**The Koren Jerusalem Bible:**
Behold, a son shall be born to thee, who shall be a man of tranquillity; and I will give him rest from all his enemies round about: for his name shall be Shelomo, and I will give peace and quietness to Yisra᾽el in his days.


**The Rashi Ketuvim by Rabbi Shraga Silverstein:**
Behold, a son will be born to you. He will be a man of repose, and I will give him rest from all of his foes roundabout. For Shlomoh will be his name. and peace [(shalom)] and quiet will I give to Israel in his days.


###### I Chronicles 22:10

הֽוּא־יִבְנֶ֥ה בַ֙יִת֙ לִשְׁמִ֔י וְהוּא֙ יִֽהְיֶה־לִּ֣י לְבֵ֔ן וַאֲנִי־ל֖וֹ לְאָ֑ב וַהֲכִ֨ינוֹתִ֜י כִּסֵּ֧א מַלְכוּת֛וֹ עַל־יִשְׂרָאֵ֖ל עַד־עוֹלָֽם׃


**The Koren Jerusalem Bible:**
He shall build a house for my name; and he shall be my son, and I will be his father; and I will establish the throne of his kingdom over Yisra᾽el forever.


**The Rashi Ketuvim by Rabbi Shraga Silverstein:**
He will build a house in My name, and he will be a son to Me, and I [(will be)] to him a Father. And I will establish the throne of his kingdom over Israel forever."

Now, my son, the Lord be with thee; and prosper thou, and build the house of the Lord thy God, as he has said of thee,


**The Rashi Ketuvim by Rabbi Shraga Silverstein:**

###### I Chronicles 22:12

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/I%20Chronicles/r/22:12](https://torahapp.org/share/book/I%20Chronicles/r/22:12)


אַ֣ךְ יִֽתֶּן־לְךָ֤ יְהֹוָה֙ שֵׂ֣כֶל וּבִינָ֔ה וִֽיצַוְּךָ֖ עַל־יִשְׂרָאֵ֑ל וְלִשְׁמ֕וֹר אֶת־תּוֹרַ֖ת יְהֹוָ֥ה אֱלֹהֶֽיךָ׃

Only the Lord give thee wisdom and understanding, and give thee charge over Yisra᾽el that thou mayst keep the Tora of the Lord thy God.


**The Rashi Ketuvim by Rabbi Shraga Silverstein:**

Then shalt thou prosper, if thou takest heed to fulfil the statutes and judgments which the Lord commanded Moshe for Yisra᾽el: be strong, and of good courage; do not dread, or be dismayed.


**The Rashi Ketuvim by Rabbi Shraga Silverstein:**
Then you will succeed, if you take heed to do the statutes and the judgments which the L-rd commanded Moses for Israel. Be strong and firm. Do not fear and do not break.


###### I Chronicles 22:14

וְהִנֵּ֨ה בְעׇנְיִ֜י הֲכִינ֣וֹתִי לְבֵית־יְהֹוָ֗ה זָהָ֞ב כִּכָּרִ֤ים מֵֽאָה־אֶ֙לֶף֙ וְכֶ֗סֶף אֶ֤לֶף אֲלָפִים֙ כִּכָּרִ֔ים וְלַנְּחֹ֤שֶׁת וְלַבַּרְזֶל֙ אֵ֣ין מִשְׁקָ֔ל כִּ֥י לָרֹ֖ב הָיָ֑ה וְעֵצִ֤ים וַֽאֲבָנִים֙ הֲכִינ֔וֹתִי וַעֲלֵיהֶ֖ם תּוֹסִֽיף׃


**The Koren Jerusalem Bible:**
Now, behold, in my trouble I have prepared for the house of the Lord a hundred thousand talents of gold, and a million talents of silver; and brass and iron without weight; for it is in abundance: and timber and stone have I prepared; and thou mayst add to it.


**The Rashi Ketuvim by Rabbi Shraga Silverstein:**
Behold, in my [(relative)] poverty, I have prepared for the house of the L-rd, gold, one hundred thousand talents, and silver, a thousand thousand talents, and copper and iron without weight, for it was (available) in abundance. And wood and stones did I prepare, and you shall add to them.

Of the gold, the silver, and the brass, and the iron, there is no account. Arise therefore, and be doing, and the Lord be with thee.

for gold and silver and copper and iron, without number. Arise and do, and may the L-rd be with you.

And David commanded all the princes of Yisra᾽el to help Shelomo his son, saying,


**The Rashi Ketuvim by Rabbi Shraga Silverstein:**

###### I Chronicles 22:18

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/I%20Chronicles/r/22:18](https://torahapp.org/share/book/I%20Chronicles/r/22:18)


הֲלֹ֨א יְהֹוָ֤ה אֱלֹֽהֵיכֶם֙ עִמָּכֶ֔ם וְהֵנִ֥יחַ לָכֶ֖ם מִסָּבִ֑יב כִּ֣י**׀** נָתַ֣ן בְּיָדִ֗י אֵ֚ת יֹשְׁבֵ֣י הָאָ֔רֶץ וְנִכְבְּשָׁ֥ה הָאָ֛רֶץ לִפְנֵ֥י יְהֹוָ֖ה וְלִפְנֵ֥י עַמּֽוֹ׃

Is not the Lord your God with you? and has he not given you rest on every side? for he has given the inhabitants of the land into my hand; and the land is subdued before the Lord, and before his people.


**The Rashi Ketuvim by Rabbi Shraga Silverstein:**
[(saying:)] Is the L-rd your G-d not with you? And He will give you rest roundabout. For the L-rd has delivered the dwellers of the land into my hand. And the land has been conquered before the L-rd and before His people.


###### I Chronicles 22:19

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/I%20Chronicles/r/22:19](https://torahapp.org/share/book/I%20Chronicles/r/22:19)


עַתָּ֗ה תְּנ֤וּ לְבַבְכֶם֙ וְנַפְשְׁכֶ֔ם לִדְר֖וֹשׁ לַיהֹוָ֣ה אֱלֹהֵיכֶ֑ם וְק֗וּמוּ וּבְנוּ֙ אֶת־מִקְדַּשׁ֙ יְהֹוָ֣ה הָאֱלֹהִ֔ים לְהָבִ֞יא אֶת־אֲר֣וֹן בְּרִית־יְהֹוָ֗ה וּכְלֵי֙ קֹ֣דֶשׁ הָאֱלֹהִ֔ים לַבַּ֖יִת הַנִּבְנֶ֥ה לְשֵׁם־יְהֹוָֽה׃{פ}


**The Koren Jerusalem Bible:**
Now set your heart and your soul to seek the Lord your God; arise therefore, and build the sanctuary of the Lord God, to bring the ark of the covenant of the Lord, and the holy vessels of God, into the house that is to be built to the name of the Lord.


**The Rashi Ketuvim by Rabbi Shraga Silverstein:**
Now, set your hearts and your souls to soliciting the L-rd your G-d. And arise and build the sanctuary of the L-rd G-d, to bring the ark of the covenant of the L-rd and the holy vessels of G-d to the house to be built in the name of the L-rd.



## Chapter 23

###### I Chronicles 23:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/I%20Chronicles/r/23:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/I%20Chronicles/r/23:1)


וְדָוִ֥יד זָקֵ֖ן וְשָׂבַ֣ע יָמִ֑ים וַיַּמְלֵ֛ךְ אֶת־שְׁלֹמֹ֥ה בְנ֖וֹ עַל־יִשְׂרָאֵֽל׃


**The Koren Jerusalem Bible:**
So when David was old and full of days, he made Shelomo his son king over Yisra᾽el.


**The Rashi Ketuvim by Rabbi Shraga Silverstein:**
And David was old and sated of days and he made Solomon his son king over Israel.


###### I Chronicles 23:2

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/I%20Chronicles/r/23:2](https://torahapp.org/share/book/I%20Chronicles/r/23:2)


וַיֶּֽאֱסֹף֙ אֶת־כׇּל־שָׂרֵ֣י יִשְׂרָאֵ֔ל וְהַכֹּהֲנִ֖ים וְהַלְוִיִּֽם׃


**The Koren Jerusalem Bible:**
And he gathered together all the princes of Yisra᾽el, with the priests and the Levites.


**The Rashi Ketuvim by Rabbi Shraga Silverstein:**
And he gathered together all the officers of Israel and the priests and the Levites.


###### I Chronicles 23:3

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/I%20Chronicles/r/23:3](https://torahapp.org/share/book/I%20Chronicles/r/23:3)


וַיִּסָּֽפְרוּ֙ הַלְוִיִּ֔ם מִבֶּ֛ן שְׁלֹשִׁ֥ים שָׁנָ֖ה וָמָ֑עְלָה וַיְהִ֨י מִסְפָּרָ֤ם לְגֻלְגְּלֹתָם֙ לִגְבָרִ֔ים שְׁלֹשִׁ֥ים וּשְׁמוֹנָ֖ה אָֽלֶף׃


**The Koren Jerusalem Bible:**
Now the Levites were numbered from the age of thirty years and upwards: and their number by their polls, man by man, was thirty eight thousand.


**The Rashi Ketuvim by Rabbi Shraga Silverstein:**
And the Levites were counted from thirty years old and above, and their number by their heads was thirty-three thousand men.


###### I Chronicles 23:4

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/I%20Chronicles/r/23:4](https://torahapp.org/share/book/I%20Chronicles/r/23:4)


מֵאֵ֗לֶּה לְנַצֵּ֙חַ֙ עַל־מְלֶ֣אכֶת בֵּית־יְהֹוָ֔ה עֶשְׂרִ֥ים וְאַרְבָּעָ֖ה אָ֑לֶף וְשֹׁטְרִ֥ים וְשֹׁפְטִ֖ים שֵׁ֥שֶׁת אֲלָפִֽים׃

Twenty four thousand of these were to oversee the work of the house of the Lord; and six thousand were officers and judges:


**The Rashi Ketuvim by Rabbi Shraga Silverstein:**

###### I Chronicles 23:5

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/I%20Chronicles/r/23:5](https://torahapp.org/share/book/I%20Chronicles/r/23:5)


וְאַרְבַּ֥עַת אֲלָפִ֖ים שֹׁעֲרִ֑ים וְאַרְבַּ֤עַת אֲלָפִים֙ מְהַֽלְלִ֣ים לַיהֹוָ֔ה בַּכֵּלִ֕ים אֲשֶׁ֥ר עָשִׂ֖יתִי לְהַלֵּֽל׃{ס}

and four thousand were gatekeepers; and four thousand praised the Lord with the instruments which I made for praise.

And David divided them into sections according to the sons of Levi; Gershon, Qehat, and Merari.

And David made divisions of the sons of Levi: Gershom, Kehoth and Merari:


###### I Chronicles 23:7

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/I%20Chronicles/r/23:7](https://torahapp.org/share/book/I%20Chronicles/r/23:7)


לַגֵּרְשֻׁנִּ֖י לַעְדָּ֥ן וְשִׁמְעִֽי׃{ס}


**The Koren Jerusalem Bible:**
Of the Gershoni there were: La῾adan, and Shim῾i.


**The Rashi Ketuvim by Rabbi Shraga Silverstein:**
Of the Gershunites: La'adan and Shim'i.


###### I Chronicles 23:8

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/I%20Chronicles/r/23:8](https://torahapp.org/share/book/I%20Chronicles/r/23:8)


בְּנֵ֣י לַעְדָּ֗ן הָרֹ֧אשׁ יְחִיאֵ֛ל וְזֵתָ֥ם וְיוֹאֵ֖ל שְׁלֹשָֽׁה׃{ס}


**The Koren Jerusalem Bible:**
The sons of La῾adan; the chief was Yeĥi᾽el, and Zetam, and Yo᾽el three.


**The Rashi Ketuvim by Rabbi Shraga Silverstein:**
The sons of La'adan: the head, Yechiel, and Zetham and Yoel, three.


###### I Chronicles 23:9

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/I%20Chronicles/r/23:9](https://torahapp.org/share/book/I%20Chronicles/r/23:9)


בְּנֵ֣י שִׁמְעִ֗י (שלמות) [שְׁלֹמִ֧ית] וַחֲזִיאֵ֛ל וְהָרָ֖ן שְׁלֹשָׁ֑ה אֵ֛לֶּה רָאשֵׁ֥י הָאָב֖וֹת לְלַעְדָּֽן׃{ס}


**The Koren Jerusalem Bible:**
The sons of Shim῾i; Shelomit, and Ḥazi᾽el, and Ḥaran; three. These were the chiefs of the fathers’ houses of La῾adan.


**The Rashi Ketuvim by Rabbi Shraga Silverstein:**

**The Koren Jerusalem Bible:**
And the sons of Shim῾i were, Yaĥat, Zina, and Ye῾ush, and Beri῾a. These four were the sons of Shim῾i.


**The Rashi Ketuvim by Rabbi Shraga Silverstein:**

**The Koren Jerusalem Bible:**

**The Rashi Ketuvim by Rabbi Shraga Silverstein:**

The sons of Qehat; ῾Amram, Yiżhar, Ḥevron, and ῾Uzzi᾽el; four.

**The Koren Jerusalem Bible:**

**The Rashi Ketuvim by Rabbi Shraga Silverstein:**

Of the sons of Yiżhar; Shelomit the chief.

###### I Chronicles 23:24

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/I%20Chronicles/r/23:24](https://torahapp.org/share/book/I%20Chronicles/r/23:24)


אֵ֣לֶּה בְנֵֽי־לֵוִי֩ לְבֵ֨ית אֲבוֹתֵיהֶ֜ם רָאשֵׁ֧י הָאָב֣וֹת לִפְקוּדֵיהֶ֗ם בְּמִסְפַּ֤ר שֵׁמוֹת֙ לְגֻלְגְּלֹתָ֔ם עֹשֵׂה֙ הַמְּלָאכָ֔ה לַעֲבֹדַ֖ת בֵּ֣ית יְהֹוָ֑ה מִבֶּ֛ן עֶשְׂרִ֥ים שָׁנָ֖ה וָמָֽעְלָה׃


**The Koren Jerusalem Bible:**

**The Rashi Ketuvim by Rabbi Shraga Silverstein:**

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/I%20Chronicles/r/23:25](https://torahapp.org/share/book/I%20Chronicles/r/23:25)

**The Koren Jerusalem Bible:**
For David said, The Lord God of Yisra᾽el has given rest to his people, that they may dwell in Yerushalayim forever:


**The Rashi Ketuvim by Rabbi Shraga Silverstein:**

###### I Chronicles 23:27

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/I%20Chronicles/r/23:27](https://torahapp.org/share/book/I%20Chronicles/r/23:27)


כִּ֣י בְדִבְרֵ֤י דָוִיד֙ הָאַ֣חֲרוֹנִ֔ים הֵ֖מָּה מִסְפַּ֣ר בְּנֵֽי־לֵוִ֑י מִבֶּ֛ן עֶשְׂרִ֥ים שָׁנָ֖ה וּלְמָֽעְלָה׃

**The Rashi Ketuvim by Rabbi Shraga Silverstein:**

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/I%20Chronicles/r/23:28](https://torahapp.org/share/book/I%20Chronicles/r/23:28)


כִּ֣י מַעֲמָדָ֞ם לְיַד־בְּנֵ֣י אַהֲרֹ֗ן לַֽעֲבֹדַת֙ בֵּ֣ית יְהֹוָ֔ה עַל־הַֽחֲצֵרוֹת֙ וְעַל־הַלְּשָׁכ֔וֹת וְעַֽל־טׇהֳרַ֖ת לְכׇל־קֹ֑דֶשׁ וּמַֽעֲשֵׂ֔ה עֲבֹדַ֖ת בֵּ֥ית הָאֱלֹהִֽים׃


**The Koren Jerusalem Bible:**
because their station was at the side of the sons of Aharon for the service of the house of the Lord, in the courts, and in the chambers, and in the purifying of all holy things, and the work of the service of the house of God;


**The Rashi Ketuvim by Rabbi Shraga Silverstein:**
For their posts were alongside the sons of Aaron for the service of the house of the L-rd, for the [(Temple)] courts and for the chambers and for the purity of all sacred (objects) and (for) performance of the service of the house of G-d.


###### I Chronicles 23:29

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/I%20Chronicles/r/23:29](https://torahapp.org/share/book/I%20Chronicles/r/23:29)


וּלְלֶ֨חֶם הַֽמַּעֲרֶ֜כֶת וּלְסֹ֤לֶת לְמִנְחָה֙ וְלִרְקִיקֵ֣י הַמַּצּ֔וֹת וְלַֽמַּחֲבַ֖ת וְלַמֻּרְבָּ֑כֶת וּלְכׇל־מְשׂוּרָ֖ה וּמִדָּֽה׃

both for the showbread, and for the fine flour for a meal offering, and for the unleavened cakes, and for that which is baked in the pan, and for that which is fried, and for all manner of measure and size;


**The Rashi Ketuvim by Rabbi Shraga Silverstein:**
And for the showbread and for the fine flour for the meal-offering, and for unleavened wafers and for griddle [meal-offerings] and for soaked [fine flour meal-offerings] and for all liquid measures and dry measures.


###### I Chronicles 23:30

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/I%20Chronicles/r/23:30](https://torahapp.org/share/book/I%20Chronicles/r/23:30)


וְלַֽעֲמֹד֙ בַּבֹּ֣קֶר בַּבֹּ֔קֶר לְהֹד֥וֹת וּלְהַלֵּ֖ל לַֽיהֹוָ֑ה וְכֵ֖ן לָעָֽרֶב׃


**The Koren Jerusalem Bible:**
and to stand every morning to thank and praise the Lord, and likewise at evening;


**The Rashi Ketuvim by Rabbi Shraga Silverstein:**

###### I Chronicles 23:31

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/I%20Chronicles/r/23:31](https://torahapp.org/share/book/I%20Chronicles/r/23:31)


וּלְכֹ֨ל הַעֲל֤וֹת עֹלוֹת֙ לַֽיהֹוָ֔ה לַשַּׁ֨בָּת֔וֹת לֶחֳדָשִׁ֖ים וְלַמֹּעֲדִ֑ים בְּמִסְפָּ֨ר כְּמִשְׁפָּ֧ט עֲלֵיהֶ֛ם תָּמִ֖יד לִפְנֵ֥י יְהֹוָֽה׃


**The Koren Jerusalem Bible:**
and whenever burnt sacrifices are offered to the Lord on the sabbaths, on the new moons, and on the appointed seasons, by number, according to the order commanded to them, continually before the Lord:


**The Rashi Ketuvim by Rabbi Shraga Silverstein:**

###### I Chronicles 23:32

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/I%20Chronicles/r/23:32](https://torahapp.org/share/book/I%20Chronicles/r/23:32)


וְשָׁמְר֞וּ אֶת־מִשְׁמֶ֣רֶת אֹֽהֶל־מוֹעֵ֗ד וְאֵת֙ מִשְׁמֶ֣רֶת הַקֹּ֔דֶשׁ וּמִשְׁמֶ֕רֶת בְּנֵ֥י אַהֲרֹ֖ן אֲחֵיהֶ֑ם לַעֲבֹדַ֖ת בֵּ֥ית יְהֹוָֽה׃{פ}


**The Koren Jerusalem Bible:**
and that they should keep the charge of the Tent of Meeting, and the charge of the holy place, and the charge of the sons of Aharon their brethren, for the service of the house of the Lord.


**The Rashi Ketuvim by Rabbi Shraga Silverstein:**

## Chapter 24

###### I Chronicles 24:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/I%20Chronicles/r/24:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/I%20Chronicles/r/24:1)


וְלִבְנֵ֥י אַהֲרֹ֖ן מַחְלְקוֹתָ֑ם בְּנֵ֣י אַהֲרֹ֔ן נָדָב֙ וַאֲבִיה֔וּא אֶלְעָזָ֖ר וְאִיתָמָֽר׃


**The Koren Jerusalem Bible:**
The divisions of the sons of Aharon. The sons of Aharon; Nadav, and Avihu, El῾azar, and Itamar.


**The Rashi Ketuvim by Rabbi Shraga Silverstein:**
And of the sons of Aaron, their divisions: The sons of Aaron: Nadav and Avihu, Elazar and Ithamar.


###### I Chronicles 24:2

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/I%20Chronicles/r/24:2](https://torahapp.org/share/book/I%20Chronicles/r/24:2)


וַיָּ֨מׇת נָדָ֤ב וַֽאֲבִיהוּא֙ לִפְנֵ֣י אֲבִיהֶ֔ם וּבָנִ֖ים לֹא־הָי֣וּ לָהֶ֑ם וַֽיְכַהֲנ֔וּ אֶלְעָזָ֖ר וְאִיתָמָֽר׃


**The Koren Jerusalem Bible:**
But Nadav and Avihu died before their father, and had no children: therefore El῾azar and Itamar executed the priest’s office.


**The Rashi Ketuvim by Rabbi Shraga Silverstein:**
And Nadav and Avihu died before their father. And they did not have sons, and Elazar and Ithamar served [(in their stead)].


###### I Chronicles 24:3

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/I%20Chronicles/r/24:3](https://torahapp.org/share/book/I%20Chronicles/r/24:3)


וַיֶּחָלְקֵ֣ם דָּוִ֔יד וְצָדוֹק֙ מִן־בְּנֵ֣י אֶלְעָזָ֔ר וַאֲחִימֶ֖לֶךְ מִן־בְּנֵ֣י אִיתָמָ֑ר לִפְקֻדָּתָ֖ם בַּעֲבֹדָתָֽם׃


**The Koren Jerusalem Bible:**

**The Rashi Ketuvim by Rabbi Shraga Silverstein:**

###### I Chronicles 24:4

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/I%20Chronicles/r/24:4](https://torahapp.org/share/book/I%20Chronicles/r/24:4)


וַיִּמָּצְא֣וּ בְֽנֵי־אֶלְעָזָ֡ר רַבִּ֞ים לְרָאשֵׁ֧י הַגְּבָרִ֛ים מִן־בְּנֵ֥י אִיתָמָ֖ר וַֽיַּחְלְק֑וּם לִבְנֵ֨י אֶלְעָזָ֜ר רָאשִׁ֤ים לְבֵית־אָבוֹת֙ שִׁשָּׁ֣ה עָשָׂ֔ר וְלִבְנֵ֧י אִיתָמָ֛ר לְבֵ֥ית אֲבוֹתָ֖ם שְׁמוֹנָֽה׃


**The Koren Jerusalem Bible:**
And there were more chief men found of the sons of El῾azar than of the sons of Itamar; and thus they were divided. Among the sons of El῾azar there were sixteen heads of fathers’ houses and among the sons of Itamar eight for the houses of their fathers.


**The Rashi Ketuvim by Rabbi Shraga Silverstein:**
And the sons of Elazar were found to be more, according to the heads of the men, than the sons of Ithamar, and they divided them: Of the sons of Elazar, heads of fathers' houses, sixteen; and of the sons of Ithamar, by their fathers' houses, eight.


###### I Chronicles 24:5

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/I%20Chronicles/r/24:5](https://torahapp.org/share/book/I%20Chronicles/r/24:5)


וַיַּחְלְק֥וּם בְּגוֹרָל֖וֹת אֵ֣לֶּה עִם־אֵ֑לֶּה כִּֽי־הָי֤וּ שָׂרֵי־קֹ֙דֶשׁ֙ וְשָׂרֵ֣י הָאֱלֹהִ֔ים מִבְּנֵ֥י אֶלְעָזָ֖ר וּבִבְנֵ֥י אִיתָמָֽר׃{ס}


**The Koren Jerusalem Bible:**

**The Rashi Ketuvim by Rabbi Shraga Silverstein:**

**The Koren Jerusalem Bible:**
And Shema῾ya the son of Netan᾽el the scribe, one of the Levites, recorded them in the presence of the king, and the princes, and Żadoq the priest, and Aĥimelekh the son of Evyatar, and the heads of the fathers’ houses of the priests and Levites: one father’s house being chosen for El῾azar, and one chosen for Itamar.


**The Rashi Ketuvim by Rabbi Shraga Silverstein:**

And the sons of Mushi: Machli and Eder and Yerimoth [i.e., their fathers' houses remained the same] These are the sons of the Levites by their fathers' houses [(twenty-four, corresponding to the twenty-four of the Cohanim)].


###### I Chronicles 24:31

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/I%20Chronicles/r/24:31](https://torahapp.org/share/book/I%20Chronicles/r/24:31)


וַיַּפִּ֩ילוּ֩ גַם־הֵ֨ם גּוֹרָל֜וֹת לְעֻמַּ֣ת**׀** אֲחֵיהֶ֣ם בְּנֵי־אַהֲרֹ֗ן לִפְנֵ֨י דָוִ֤יד הַמֶּ֙לֶךְ֙ וְצָד֣וֹק וַאֲחִימֶ֔לֶךְ וְרָאשֵׁי֙ הָאָב֔וֹת לַכֹּהֲנִ֖ים וְלַלְוִיִּ֑ם אָב֣וֹת הָרֹ֔אשׁ לְעֻמַּ֖ת אָחִ֥יו הַקָּטָֽן׃{ס}


**The Koren Jerusalem Bible:**
These also cast lots in the same manner as their brethren the sons of Aharon, in the presence of David the king, and Żadoq, and Aĥimelekh, and the heads of the fathers’ houses of the priests and Levites; the head of each father’s house in the same manner as his younger brother.


**The Rashi Ketuvim by Rabbi Shraga Silverstein:**
And they, too, cast lots [(for order of service)] alongside their brothers, the sons of Aaron, before King David and Tzaddok and Achimelech and the heads of the houses of the priests and the Levites — Avoth, the head [(of all the fathers' houses)] alongside his brother, the minor [i.e. no precedence in serving order being granted him because of his superior status]

###### I Chronicles 25:1

וַיַּבְדֵּ֣ל דָּוִיד֩ וְשָׂרֵ֨י הַצָּבָ֜א לַעֲבֹדָ֗ה לִבְנֵ֤י אָסָף֙ וְהֵימָ֣ן וִידוּת֔וּן (הנביאים) [הַֽנִּבְּאִ֛ים] בְּכִנֹּר֥וֹת בִּנְבָלִ֖ים וּבִמְצִלְתָּ֑יִם וַֽיְהִי֙ מִסְפָּרָ֔ם אַנְשֵׁ֥י מְלָאכָ֖ה לַעֲבֹדָתָֽם׃

**The Rashi Ketuvim by Rabbi Shraga Silverstein:**
And David and the officers of the host for the [sacred] service set apart [from the rest of the Levites, for service of song] the [(four)] sons of Asaph and [(the fourteen sons of)] Heiman, and [(the six sons of)] Yeduthun [(all together, twenty-four, corresponding to the twenty-four priestly watches)], who prophesied [(by the Holy Spirit)], with harps, lyres, and cymbals. And their number, skilled in their service:

###### I Chronicles 26:1

**The Rashi Ketuvim by Rabbi Shraga Silverstein:**

**The Rashi Ketuvim by Rabbi Shraga Silverstein:**

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/I%20Chronicles/r/26:3](https://torahapp.org/share/book/I%20Chronicles/r/26:3)

**The Koren Jerusalem Bible:**
῾Elam the fifth, Yehoĥanan the sixth, Elyeho῾enay the seventh.


**The Rashi Ketuvim by Rabbi Shraga Silverstein:**

῾Ammi᾽el the sixth, Yissakhar the seventh, Pe῾ulletay the eighth: for God blessed him.

**The Rashi Ketuvim by Rabbi Shraga Silverstein:**

**The Rashi Ketuvim by Rabbi Shraga Silverstein:**

**The Rashi Ketuvim by Rabbi Shraga Silverstein:**

###### I Chronicles 26:21

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/I%20Chronicles/r/26:21](https://torahapp.org/share/book/I%20Chronicles/r/26:21)

###### I Chronicles 27:1

**The Koren Jerusalem Bible:**
Now the children of Yisra᾽el after their number, namely, heads of fathers’ houses and captains of thousands and hundreds, and their officers who served the king in any matter of the divisions, who came in and went out month by month throughout all the months of the year; in every division were twenty four thousand.


**The Rashi Ketuvim by Rabbi Shraga Silverstein:**
And the children of Israel, by their number, [(among them)] heads of the fathers' houses and officers of thousands and hundreds, and their supervisors, who ministered before the king for all functions of the divisions, one coming in, the other going out, month by month for all the months of the year — one division, twenty-four thousand (men).

## Chapter 28

###### I Chronicles 28:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/I%20Chronicles/r/28:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/I%20Chronicles/r/28:1)


וַיַּקְהֵ֣ל דָּוִ֣יד אֶת־כׇּל־שָׂרֵ֣י יִשְׂרָאֵ֡ל שָׂרֵ֣י הַשְּׁבָטִ֣ים וְשָׂרֵ֣י הַמַּחְלְק֣וֹת הַמְשָׁרְתִ֪ים אֶת־הַמֶּ֟לֶךְ וְשָׂרֵ֣י הָאֲלָפִ֣ים וְשָׂרֵ֣י הַמֵּא֡וֹת וְשָׂרֵ֣י כׇל־רְכוּשׁ־וּמִקְנֶה֩׀לַמֶּ֨לֶךְ וּלְבָנָ֜יו עִם־הַסָּרִיסִ֧ים וְהַגִּבּוֹרִ֛ים וּֽלְכׇל־גִּבּ֥וֹר חָ֖יִל אֶל־יְרוּשָׁלָֽ͏ִם׃


**The Koren Jerusalem Bible:**
And David assembled all the princes of Yisra᾽el, the princes of the tribes, and the captains of the companies that served the king by divisions, and the captains over the thousands, and captains over the hundreds, and the stewards over all the property and possessions of the king, and of his sons, with the officers, and with the mighty men, and with all the men at arms to Yerushalayim.


**The Rashi Ketuvim by Rabbi Shraga Silverstein:**
And David assembled all the officers of Israel: the officers of the tribes and the officers of the divisions ministering unto the king, and the officers of the thousands, and the officers of the hundreds and the officers of all the property and the livestock of the king and his sons, with the eunuchs and the stalwarts and all the men of valor, in Jerusalem.


###### I Chronicles 28:2

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/I%20Chronicles/r/28:2](https://torahapp.org/share/book/I%20Chronicles/r/28:2)


וַיָּ֨קׇם דָּוִ֤יד הַמֶּ֙לֶךְ֙ עַל־רַגְלָ֔יו וַיֹּ֕אמֶר שְׁמָע֖וּנִי אַחַ֣י וְעַמִּ֑י אֲנִ֣י עִם־לְבָבִ֡י לִבְנוֹת֩ בֵּ֨ית מְנוּחָ֜ה לַאֲר֣וֹן בְּרִית־יְהֹוָ֗ה וְלַֽהֲדֹם֙ רַגְלֵ֣י אֱלֹהֵ֔ינוּ וַהֲכִינ֖וֹתִי לִבְנֽוֹת׃


**The Koren Jerusalem Bible:**
Then David the king stood up upon his feet, and said, Hear me, my brethren, and my people: As for me, I had it in my heart to build a house of rest for the ark of the covenant of the Lord, and for the footstool of our God, and I had made ready for building:


**The Rashi Ketuvim by Rabbi Shraga Silverstein:**
And King David rose to his feet and said: Hear me, my brothers and my people. It is in my heart to build a house of rest for the ark of the L-rd and for the footstool of our G-d, and I have prepared [all that is needed] to build (it).


###### I Chronicles 28:3

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/I%20Chronicles/r/28:3](https://torahapp.org/share/book/I%20Chronicles/r/28:3)


וְהָֽאֱלֹהִים֙ אָ֣מַר לִ֔י לֹא־תִבְנֶ֥ה בַ֖יִת לִשְׁמִ֑י כִּ֣י אִ֧ישׁ מִלְחָמ֛וֹת אַ֖תָּה וְדָמִ֥ים שָׁפָֽכְתָּ׃


**The Koren Jerusalem Bible:**
but God said to me, Thou shalt not build a house for my name, because thou hast been a man of battles, and hast shed blood.


**The Rashi Ketuvim by Rabbi Shraga Silverstein:**
But G-d said to me: Do not build a house to My name, for you are a man of war and you have spilled blood.


###### I Chronicles 28:4

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/I%20Chronicles/r/28:4](https://torahapp.org/share/book/I%20Chronicles/r/28:4)


וַיִּבְחַ֡ר יְהֹוָ֣ה אֱלֹהֵי֩ יִשְׂרָאֵ֨ל בִּ֜י מִכֹּ֣ל בֵּית־אָבִ֗י לִהְי֨וֹת לְמֶ֤לֶךְ עַל־יִשְׂרָאֵל֙ לְעוֹלָ֔ם כִּ֤י בִֽיהוּדָה֙ בָּחַ֣ר לְנָגִ֔יד וּבְבֵ֥ית יְהוּדָ֖ה בֵּ֣ית אָבִ֑י וּבִבְנֵ֣י אָבִ֔י בִּ֣י רָצָ֔ה לְהַמְלִ֖יךְ עַל־כׇּל־יִשְׂרָאֵֽל׃


**The Koren Jerusalem Bible:**
Yet the Lord God of Yisra᾽el chose me before all the house of my father to be king over Yisra᾽el forever: for he has chosen Yehuda to be the ruler; and out of the house of Yehuda, the house of my father; and among the sons of my father he took pleasure in me to make me king over all Yisra᾽el:


**The Rashi Ketuvim by Rabbi Shraga Silverstein:**
And the L-rd G-d of Israel chose me of all of my father's house to be king over Israel forever. For in Judah He chose a leader; and in the house of Judah, my father's house; and of the sons of my father, me did He want to make king over Israel.


###### I Chronicles 28:5

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/I%20Chronicles/r/28:5](https://torahapp.org/share/book/I%20Chronicles/r/28:5)


וּמִ֨כׇּל־בָּנַ֔י כִּ֚י רַבִּ֣ים בָּנִ֔ים נָ֥תַן לִ֖י יְהֹוָ֑ה וַיִּבְחַר֙ בִּשְׁלֹמֹ֣ה בְנִ֔י לָשֶׁ֗בֶת עַל־כִּסֵּ֛א מַלְכ֥וּת יְהֹוָ֖ה עַל־יִשְׂרָאֵֽל׃


**The Koren Jerusalem Bible:**
and of all my sons, (for the Lord has given me many sons), he has chosen Shelomo my son to sit upon the throne of the kingdom of the Lord over Yisra᾽el.


**The Rashi Ketuvim by Rabbi Shraga Silverstein:**
And of all of my sons (for many sons did the L-rd give me) He chose Solomon my son to sit on the throne of the kingdom of the L-rd over Israel.


###### I Chronicles 28:6

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/I%20Chronicles/r/28:6](https://torahapp.org/share/book/I%20Chronicles/r/28:6)


וַיֹּ֣אמֶר לִ֔י שְׁלֹמֹ֣ה בִנְךָ֔ הוּא־יִבְנֶ֥ה בֵיתִ֖י וַחֲצֵרוֹתָ֑י כִּֽי־בָחַ֨רְתִּי ב֥וֹ לִי֙ לְבֵ֔ן וַאֲנִ֖י אֶהְיֶה־לּ֥וֹ לְאָֽב׃


**The Koren Jerusalem Bible:**
And he said to me, Shelomo thy son, he shall build my house and my courts: for I have chosen him to be my son, and I will be a father to him.


**The Rashi Ketuvim by Rabbi Shraga Silverstein:**
And He said to me: Solomon your son, he will build My house and My courts. For I have chosen him for Me as a son, and I shall be to him as a Father.


###### I Chronicles 28:7

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/I%20Chronicles/r/28:7](https://torahapp.org/share/book/I%20Chronicles/r/28:7)


וַהֲכִינוֹתִ֥י אֶת־מַלְכוּת֖וֹ עַד־לְעוֹלָ֑ם אִם־יֶחֱזַ֗ק לַעֲשׂ֛וֹת מִצְוֺתַ֥י וּמִשְׁפָּטַ֖י כַּיּ֥וֹם הַזֶּֽה׃


**The Koren Jerusalem Bible:**
Moreover I will establish his kingdom forever, if he be constant to do my commandments and my judgments, as at this day.


**The Rashi Ketuvim by Rabbi Shraga Silverstein:**
And I have established his kingdom forever if he will strengthen himself to do My mitzvoth and My judgments as of this day.


###### I Chronicles 28:8

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/I%20Chronicles/r/28:8](https://torahapp.org/share/book/I%20Chronicles/r/28:8)


וְ֠עַתָּ֠ה לְעֵינֵ֨י כׇל־יִשְׂרָאֵ֤ל קְהַל־יְהֹוָה֙ וּבְאׇזְנֵ֣י אֱלֹהֵ֔ינוּ שִׁמְר֣וּ וְדִרְשׁ֔וּ כׇּל־מִצְוֺ֖ת יְהֹוָ֣ה אֱלֹהֵיכֶ֑ם לְמַ֤עַן תִּֽירְשׁוּ֙ אֶת־הָאָ֣רֶץ הַטּוֹבָ֔ה וְהִנְחַלְתֶּ֛ם לִבְנֵיכֶ֥ם אַחֲרֵיכֶ֖ם עַד־עוֹלָֽם׃


**The Koren Jerusalem Bible:**
Now therefore in the sight of all Yisra᾽el the congregation of the Lord, and in the hearing of our God, observe and seek out all the commandments of the Lord your God: that you may possess this good land, and leave it for an inheritance for your children after you forever.


**The Rashi Ketuvim by Rabbi Shraga Silverstein:**
And now, before the eyes of all of Israel, the congregation of the L-rd [(I exhort you:)] Keep and seek all the mitzvoth of the L-rd your G-d so that you inherit the good land and so that you bequeath it to your sons after you forever.


###### I Chronicles 28:9

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/I%20Chronicles/r/28:9](https://torahapp.org/share/book/I%20Chronicles/r/28:9)


וְאַתָּ֣ה שְׁלֹמֹֽה־בְנִ֡י דַּע֩ אֶת־אֱלֹהֵ֨י אָבִ֜יךָ וְעׇבְדֵ֗הוּ בְּלֵ֤ב שָׁלֵם֙ וּבְנֶ֣פֶשׁ חֲפֵצָ֔ה כִּ֤י כׇל־לְבָבוֹת֙ דּוֹרֵ֣שׁ יְהֹוָ֔ה וְכׇל־יֵ֥צֶר מַחֲשָׁב֖וֹת מֵבִ֑ין אִֽם־תִּדְרְשֶׁ֙נּוּ֙ יִמָּ֣צֵא לָ֔ךְ וְאִם־תַּעַזְבֶ֖נּוּ יַזְנִיחֲךָ֥ לָעַֽד׃


**The Koren Jerusalem Bible:**
And thou, Shelomo my son, know thou the God of thy father, and serve him with a perfect heart and with a willing mind: for the Lord searches all hearts, and understands all the imaginations of the thoughts: if thou seek him, he will be found by thee; but if thou forsake him, he will cast thee off forever.


**The Rashi Ketuvim by Rabbi Shraga Silverstein:**
And you, Solomon, my son, know the G-d of your father and serve Him with a whole heart and with a willing spirit. For the L-rd seeks out all hearts and He understands the inclination of all thoughts. If you seek Him, He will be found for you; and if you leave Him, He will forsake you forever.


###### I Chronicles 28:10

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/I%20Chronicles/r/28:10](https://torahapp.org/share/book/I%20Chronicles/r/28:10)


רְאֵ֣ה**׀** עַתָּ֗ה כִּֽי־יְהֹוָ֛ה בָּ֧חַר בְּךָ֛ לִבְנֽוֹת־בַּ֥יִת לַמִּקְדָּ֖שׁ חֲזַ֥ק וַעֲשֵֽׂה׃{פ}


**The Koren Jerusalem Bible:**
Take heed now; for the Lord has chosen thee to build a house for the sanctuary: be strong, and do it.


**The Rashi Ketuvim by Rabbi Shraga Silverstein:**
See, now, that the L-rd has chosen you to build a house as a sanctuary [for Him]. Be strong and do!


###### I Chronicles 28:11

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/I%20Chronicles/r/28:11](https://torahapp.org/share/book/I%20Chronicles/r/28:11)


וַיִּתֵּ֣ן דָּוִ֣יד לִשְׁלֹמֹ֣ה בְנ֡וֹ אֶת־תַּבְנִ֣ית הָאוּלָם֩ וְֽאֶת־בָּ֨תָּ֜יו וְגַנְזַכָּ֧יו וַעֲלִיֹּתָ֛יו וַחֲדָרָ֥יו הַפְּנִימִ֖ים וּבֵ֥ית הַכַּפֹּֽרֶת׃


**The Koren Jerusalem Bible:**
Then David gave to Shelomo his son the pattern of the porch, and of its houses and of its treasuries, and of its upper chambers, and of its inner rooms, and of the place of the ark with its covering,


**The Rashi Ketuvim by Rabbi Shraga Silverstein:**
And David gave to Solomon his son the plans for the Ulam [(the entrance hall)] and its houses and its treasuries and its upper stories and its inner chambers and the house of the ark cover [i.e., the holy of holies].


###### I Chronicles 28:12

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/I%20Chronicles/r/28:12](https://torahapp.org/share/book/I%20Chronicles/r/28:12)


וְתַבְנִ֗ית כֹּל֩ אֲשֶׁ֨ר הָיָ֤ה בָר֙וּחַ֙ עִמּ֔וֹ לְחַצְר֧וֹת בֵּית־יְהֹוָ֛ה וּלְכׇל־הַלְּשָׁכ֖וֹת סָבִ֑יב לְאֹֽצְרוֹת֙ בֵּ֣ית הָאֱלֹהִ֔ים וּלְאֹצְר֖וֹת הַקֳּדָשִֽׁים׃


**The Koren Jerusalem Bible:**
and the pattern of all that he had by the spirit, of the courts of the house of the Lord, and of all the chambers round about, of the treasuries of the house of God, and of the treasuries of the holy things:


**The Rashi Ketuvim by Rabbi Shraga Silverstein:**
And the plans of all that was with him by the [Holy] Spirit, for the courts of the house of the L-rd and for all the chambers roundabout, for the treasuries of the house of G-d [(for Temple maintenance)] and for the treasuries of the holies [i.e., for communal sacrifices].


###### I Chronicles 28:13

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/I%20Chronicles/r/28:13](https://torahapp.org/share/book/I%20Chronicles/r/28:13)


וּֽלְמַחְלְקוֹת֙ הַכֹּהֲנִ֣ים וְהַלְוִיִּ֔ם וּֽלְכׇל־מְלֶ֖אכֶת עֲבוֹדַ֣ת בֵּית־יְהֹוָ֑ה וּֽלְכׇל־כְּלֵ֖י עֲבוֹדַ֥ת בֵּית־יְהֹוָֽה׃


**The Koren Jerusalem Bible:**
also for the divisions of the priests and the Levites, and for all the work of the service of the house of the Lord, and for all the vessels of service in the house of the Lord.


**The Rashi Ketuvim by Rabbi Shraga Silverstein:**
And for the divisions of the priests and the Levites and for the work of the service of the house of the L-rd and for all the vessels of the service of the house of the L-rd.


###### I Chronicles 28:14

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/I%20Chronicles/r/28:14](https://torahapp.org/share/book/I%20Chronicles/r/28:14)


לַזָּהָ֤ב בַּמִּשְׁקָל֙ לַזָּהָ֔ב לְכׇל־כְּלֵ֖י עֲבוֹדָ֣ה וַעֲבוֹדָ֑ה לְכֹ֨ל כְּלֵ֤י הַכֶּ֙סֶף֙ בְּמִשְׁקָ֔ל לְכׇל־כְּלֵ֖י עֲבוֹדָ֥ה וַעֲבוֹדָֽה׃


**The Koren Jerusalem Bible:**
He gave of gold by weight for things of gold, for all instruments of all manner of service; silver also for all instruments of silver by weight, for all instruments of every kind of service:


**The Rashi Ketuvim by Rabbi Shraga Silverstein:**
For gold: the weight of the gold for all of the vessels by their respective service. For all of the silver vessels, by weight, for all of the vessels by their respective service.


###### I Chronicles 28:15

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/I%20Chronicles/r/28:15](https://torahapp.org/share/book/I%20Chronicles/r/28:15)


וּמִשְׁקָ֞ל לִמְנֹר֣וֹת הַזָּהָ֗ב וְנֵרֹֽתֵיהֶם֙ זָהָ֔ב בְּמִשְׁקַל־מְנוֹרָ֥ה וּמְנוֹרָ֖ה וְנֵרֹתֶ֑יהָ וְלִמְנֹר֨וֹת הַכֶּ֤סֶף בְּמִשְׁקָל֙ לִמְנוֹרָ֣ה וְנֵרֹתֶ֔יהָ כַּעֲבוֹדַ֖ת מְנוֹרָ֥ה וּמְנוֹרָֽה׃


**The Koren Jerusalem Bible:**
both the weight for the candlesticks of gold, and for their lamps of gold, by weight for every candlestick, and for its lamps: and for the candlesticks of silver by weight, for the candlestick, and for its lamps, according to the use of every candlestick.


**The Rashi Ketuvim by Rabbi Shraga Silverstein:**
And the weight [of gold] for the golden candelabra and their golden lamps, by the weight of each candelabrum and its lamps. And for the silver candelabra, by weight, for the candelabrum and its lamps, according to the respective service of each candelabrum.


###### I Chronicles 28:16

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/I%20Chronicles/r/28:16](https://torahapp.org/share/book/I%20Chronicles/r/28:16)


וְאֶת־הַזָּהָ֥ב מִשְׁקָ֛ל לְשֻׁלְחֲנ֥וֹת הַֽמַּעֲרֶ֖כֶת לְשֻׁלְחַ֣ן וְשֻׁלְחָ֑ן וְכֶ֖סֶף לְשֻׁלְחֲנ֥וֹת הַכָּֽסֶף׃


**The Koren Jerusalem Bible:**
And by weight he gave gold for the tables of showbread, for every table; and likewise silver for the tables of silver:


**The Rashi Ketuvim by Rabbi Shraga Silverstein:**
And the gold, the weight for the showbread tables for each table and silver for the silver tables.


###### I Chronicles 28:17

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/I%20Chronicles/r/28:17](https://torahapp.org/share/book/I%20Chronicles/r/28:17)


וְהַמִּזְלָג֧וֹת וְהַמִּזְרָק֛וֹת וְהַקְּשָׂוֺ֖ת זָהָ֣ב טָה֑וֹר וְלִכְפוֹרֵ֨י הַזָּהָ֤ב בְּמִשְׁקָל֙ לִכְפ֣וֹר וּכְפ֔וֹר וְלִכְפוֹרֵ֥י הַכֶּ֛סֶף בְּמִשְׁקָ֖ל לִכְפ֥וֹר וּכְפֽוֹר׃


**The Koren Jerusalem Bible:**
also pure gold for the forks, and the basins, and the jars: and for the golden bowls by weight for every bowl; and likewise by weight for every bowl of the silver bowls:


**The Rashi Ketuvim by Rabbi Shraga Silverstein:**
And (for) the forks and the basins and the staves, of pure gold. And for the golden cups, by weight, for each cup; and for the silver cups, by weight, for each cup.


###### I Chronicles 28:18

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/I%20Chronicles/r/28:18](https://torahapp.org/share/book/I%20Chronicles/r/28:18)


וּלְמִזְבַּ֧ח הַקְּטֹ֛רֶת זָהָ֥ב מְזֻקָּ֖ק בַּמִּשְׁקָ֑ל וּלְתַבְנִ֣ית הַמֶּרְכָּבָ֗ה הַכְּרוּבִ֤ים זָהָב֙ לְפֹ֣רְשִׂ֔ים וְסֹכְכִ֖ים עַל־אֲר֥וֹן בְּרִית־יְהֹוָֽה׃


**The Koren Jerusalem Bible:**
and for the altar of incense refined gold by weight; and gold for the pattern of the chariot, that is the keruvim, that spread out their wings, and cover the ark of the covenant of the Lord.


**The Rashi Ketuvim by Rabbi Shraga Silverstein:**
And for the incense altar, refined gold, by weight. And for the design of the chariot, the cherubs of gold, spreading out (their wings) and covering the ark of the covenant of the L-rd, all the works of the plan.


###### I Chronicles 28:19

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/I%20Chronicles/r/28:19](https://torahapp.org/share/book/I%20Chronicles/r/28:19)


הַכֹּ֥ל בִּכְתָ֛ב מִיַּ֥ד יְהֹוָ֖ה עָלַ֣י הִשְׂכִּ֑יל כֹּ֖ל מַלְאֲכ֥וֹת הַתַּבְנִֽית׃{פ}


**The Koren Jerusalem Bible:**
All this, said he, is put in writing by the hand of the Lord who instructed me, all the works of this pattern.


**The Rashi Ketuvim by Rabbi Shraga Silverstein:**
All in writing, imparted to me by the hand of the L-rd, all the works of the plan.


###### I Chronicles 28:20

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/I%20Chronicles/r/28:20](https://torahapp.org/share/book/I%20Chronicles/r/28:20)


וַיֹּ֨אמֶר דָּוִ֜יד לִשְׁלֹמֹ֣ה בְנ֗וֹ חֲזַ֤ק וֶֽאֱמַץ֙ וַעֲשֵׂ֔ה אַל־תִּירָ֖א וְאַל־תֵּחָ֑ת כִּי֩ יְהֹוָ֨ה אֱלֹהִ֤ים אֱלֹהַי֙ עִמָּ֔ךְ לֹ֤א יַרְפְּךָ֙ וְלֹ֣א יַעַזְבֶ֔ךָּ עַד־לִכְל֕וֹת כׇּל־מְלֶ֖אכֶת עֲבוֹדַ֥ת בֵּית־יְהֹוָֽה׃


**The Koren Jerusalem Bible:**
And David said to Shelomo his son, Be strong and of good courage, and do it: do not fear, or be dismayed: for the Lord God, my God, will be with thee; he will not fail thee, or forsake thee, until thou hast finished all the work for the service of the house of the Lord.


**The Rashi Ketuvim by Rabbi Shraga Silverstein:**
And David said to Solomon his son: Be strong and firm and do. Do not fear and do not break. For the L-rd G-d, my G-d is with you. He will not let go of you and will not leave you until the completion of all the work of the service of the house of the L-rd.


###### I Chronicles 28:21

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/I%20Chronicles/r/28:21](https://torahapp.org/share/book/I%20Chronicles/r/28:21)


וְהִנֵּ֗ה מַחְלְקוֹת֙ הַכֹּהֲנִ֣ים וְהַלְוִיִּ֔ם לְכׇל־עֲבוֹדַ֖ת בֵּ֣ית הָֽאֱלֹהִ֑ים וְעִמְּךָ֨ בְכׇל־מְלָאכָ֜ה לְכׇל־נָדִ֤יב בַּֽחׇכְמָה֙ לְכׇל־עֲבוֹדָ֔ה וְהַשָּׂרִ֥ים וְכׇל־הָעָ֖ם לְכׇל־דְּבָרֶֽיךָ׃{ס}


**The Koren Jerusalem Bible:**
And, behold, the divisions of the priests and the Levites, for all the service of the house of God: and there shall be with thee for all manner of workmanship every willing skilful man, for any manner of service: also the princes and all the people will be wholly at thy commandment.


**The Rashi Ketuvim by Rabbi Shraga Silverstein:**
And, behold, the divisions of the priests and the Levites for all the service of the house of G-d. And with you, in every (kind of) work, all who volunteer (their services) in wisdom for every (kind of) service. And the officers and the whole people (are at your service) for all that you bid.



## Chapter 29

###### I Chronicles 29:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/I%20Chronicles/r/29:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/I%20Chronicles/r/29:1)


וַיֹּ֨אמֶר דָּוִ֤יד הַמֶּ֙לֶךְ֙ לְכׇל־הַקָּהָ֔ל שְׁלֹמֹ֨ה בְנִ֥י אֶחָ֛ד בָּחַר־בּ֥וֹ אֱלֹהִ֖ים נַ֣עַר וָרָ֑ךְ וְהַמְּלָאכָ֣ה גְדוֹלָ֔ה כִּ֣י לֹ֤א לְאָדָם֙ הַבִּירָ֔ה כִּ֖י לַיהֹוָ֥ה אֱלֹהִֽים׃


**The Koren Jerusalem Bible:**
And David the king said to all the congregation, Shelomo my son, whom alone God has chosen, is yet young and tender, and the work is great: for the palace is not for man, but for the Lord God.


**The Rashi Ketuvim by Rabbi Shraga Silverstein:**
And King David said to the entire congregation: Solomon my son is [only] one. G-d has chosen him, young and untried. And the work is great. For the palace [i.e., the Temple] is not for man, but for the L-rd G-d.


###### I Chronicles 29:2

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/I%20Chronicles/r/29:2](https://torahapp.org/share/book/I%20Chronicles/r/29:2)


וּֽכְכׇל־כֹּחִ֞י הֲכִינ֣וֹתִי לְבֵית־אֱלֹהַ֗י הַזָּהָ֣ב׀לַ֠זָּהָ֠ב וְהַכֶּ֨סֶף לַכֶּ֜סֶף וְהַנְּחֹ֣שֶׁת לַנְּחֹ֗שֶׁת הַבַּרְזֶל֙ לַבַּרְזֶ֔ל וְהָעֵצִ֖ים לָעֵצִ֑ים אַבְנֵי־שֹׁ֨הַם וּמִלּוּאִ֜ים אַבְנֵי־פ֣וּךְ וְרִקְמָ֗ה וְכֹ֨ל אֶ֧בֶן יְקָרָ֛ה וְאַבְנֵי־שַׁ֖יִשׁ לָרֹֽב׃


**The Koren Jerusalem Bible:**
Now I have prepared with all my might for the house of my God the gold for things to be made of gold, and the silver for things of silver, and the brass for things of brass, the iron for things of iron, and wood for things of wood; Shoham stones, and stones to be set, glistering stones, and coloured stones; and all manner of precious stones, and marble stone in abundance.


**The Rashi Ketuvim by Rabbi Shraga Silverstein:**
And with all my strength I prepared for the house of my G-d. Gold for the (things of) gold, silver for the silver, copper for the copper, iron for the iron, wood for the wood, onyx stones and (stones) for setting, carbuncle stones and embroidery, and all precious stones, and marble stones in abundance.


###### I Chronicles 29:3

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/I%20Chronicles/r/29:3](https://torahapp.org/share/book/I%20Chronicles/r/29:3)


וְע֗וֹד בִּרְצוֹתִי֙ בְּבֵ֣ית אֱלֹהַ֔י יֶשׁ־לִ֥י סְגֻלָּ֖ה זָהָ֣ב וָכָ֑סֶף נָתַ֤תִּי לְבֵית־אֱלֹהַי֙ לְמַ֔עְלָה מִכׇּל־הֲכִינ֖וֹתִי לְבֵ֥ית הַקֹּֽדֶשׁ׃


**The Koren Jerusalem Bible:**
Moreover, because I have set my affection on the house of my God, I have given of my own private treasure, of gold and silver, to the house of my God, over and above all that I have prepared for the holy house,


**The Rashi Ketuvim by Rabbi Shraga Silverstein:**
And more, in my desire for the house of my G-d — I have a treasure of gold and silver — I gave (it) for the house of my G-d, over and above all that I prepared for the holy house.


###### I Chronicles 29:4

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/I%20Chronicles/r/29:4](https://torahapp.org/share/book/I%20Chronicles/r/29:4)


שְׁלֹ֧שֶׁת אֲלָפִ֛ים כִּכְּרֵ֥י זָהָ֖ב מִזְּהַ֣ב אוֹפִ֑יר וְשִׁבְעַ֨ת אֲלָפִ֤ים כִּכַּר־כֶּ֙סֶף֙ מְזֻקָּ֔ק לָט֖וּחַ קִיר֥וֹת הַבָּתִּֽים׃


**The Koren Jerusalem Bible:**
three thousand talents of gold, of the gold of Ofir, and seven thousand talents of refined silver, with which to overlay the walls of the houses:


**The Rashi Ketuvim by Rabbi Shraga Silverstein:**
Three thousand talents of gold of the gold of Ophir. And seven thousand talents of refined silver to overlay the walls of the houses.


###### I Chronicles 29:5

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/I%20Chronicles/r/29:5](https://torahapp.org/share/book/I%20Chronicles/r/29:5)


לַזָּהָ֤ב לַזָּהָב֙ וְלַכֶּ֣סֶף לַכֶּ֔סֶף וּלְכׇל־מְלָאכָ֖ה בְּיַ֣ד חָרָשִׁ֑ים וּמִ֣י מִתְנַדֵּ֔ב לְמַלֹּ֥אות יָד֛וֹ הַיּ֖וֹם לַיהֹוָֽה׃


**The Koren Jerusalem Bible:**
the gold for things of gold, and the silver for things of silver, and for all manner of work to be made by the hands of artificers. And who then is willing to consecrate himself this day to the Lord?


**The Rashi Ketuvim by Rabbi Shraga Silverstein:**
The gold for the gold and the silver for the silver and (materials) for all the work, in the hands of the craftsmen. And who [(of you the people)] will volunteer to fill his hand today for the [house of] the L-rd?


###### I Chronicles 29:6

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/I%20Chronicles/r/29:6](https://torahapp.org/share/book/I%20Chronicles/r/29:6)


וַיִּֽתְנַדְּבוּ֩ שָׂרֵ֨י הָאָב֜וֹת וְשָׂרֵ֣י**׀** שִׁבְטֵ֣י יִשְׂרָאֵ֗ל וְשָׂרֵ֤י הָאֲלָפִים֙ וְהַמֵּא֔וֹת וּלְשָׂרֵ֖י מְלֶ֥אכֶת הַמֶּֽלֶךְ׃


**The Koren Jerusalem Bible:**
Then the chief of the fathers’ houses and princes of the tribes of Yisra᾽el, and the captains of thousands and of hundreds, with the rulers of the king’s work, offered willingly,


**The Rashi Ketuvim by Rabbi Shraga Silverstein:**
And there volunteered, the officers of the fathers' [houses] and the officers of the tribes of Israel and the officers of the thousands and the hundreds and the officers of the king's service.


###### I Chronicles 29:7

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/I%20Chronicles/r/29:7](https://torahapp.org/share/book/I%20Chronicles/r/29:7)


וַֽיִּתְּנ֞וּ לַעֲבוֹדַ֣ת בֵּית־הָאֱלֹהִ֗ים זָהָ֞ב כִּכָּרִ֣ים חֲמֵשֶׁת־אֲלָפִים֮ וַאֲדַרְכֹנִ֣ים רִבּוֹ֒ וְכֶ֗סֶף כִּכָּרִים֙ עֲשֶׂ֣רֶת אֲלָפִ֔ים וּנְחֹ֕שֶׁת רִבּ֛וֹ וּשְׁמוֹנַ֥ת אֲלָפִ֖ים כִּכָּרִ֑ים וּבַרְזֶ֖ל מֵאָה־אֶ֥לֶף כִּכָּרִֽים׃


**The Koren Jerusalem Bible:**
and gave for the service of the house of God five thousand talents and ten thousand darics of gold, and ten thousand talents of silver, and eighteen thousand talents of brass, and one hundred thousand talents of iron.


**The Rashi Ketuvim by Rabbi Shraga Silverstein:**
And they gave for the service of the house of G-d: gold, five thousand talents, and ten thousand drachmas; silver, ten thousand talents; and copper, eighteen thousand talents; and iron, a hundred thousand talents.


###### I Chronicles 29:8

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/I%20Chronicles/r/29:8](https://torahapp.org/share/book/I%20Chronicles/r/29:8)


וְהַנִּמְצָ֤א אִתּוֹ֙ אֲבָנִ֔ים נָתְנ֖וּ לְאוֹצַ֣ר בֵּית־יְהֹוָ֑ה עַ֥ל יַד־יְחִיאֵ֖ל הַגֵּרְשֻׁנִּֽי׃


**The Koren Jerusalem Bible:**
And they with whom precious stones were found gave them to the treasure of the house of the Lord, by the hand of Yeĥi᾽el the Gershonite.


**The Rashi Ketuvim by Rabbi Shraga Silverstein:**
And those with whom stones were found gave (them) to the treasury of the house of the L-rd by the hand of Yechiel the Gershunite.


###### I Chronicles 29:9

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/I%20Chronicles/r/29:9](https://torahapp.org/share/book/I%20Chronicles/r/29:9)


וַיִּשְׂמְח֤וּ הָעָם֙ עַל־הִֽתְנַדְּבָ֔ם כִּ֚י בְּלֵ֣ב שָׁלֵ֔ם הִֽתְנַדְּב֖וּ לַֽיהֹוָ֑ה וְגַם֙ דָּוִ֣יד הַמֶּ֔לֶךְ שָׂמַ֖ח שִׂמְחָ֥ה גְדוֹלָֽה׃{פ}


**The Koren Jerusalem Bible:**
Then the people rejoiced, for having offered willingly, because with a perfect heart they offered willingly to the Lord: and David the king also rejoiced with great joy.


**The Rashi Ketuvim by Rabbi Shraga Silverstein:**
And the people rejoiced in their giving, for they gave with a full heart to the L-rd. And King David, too, rejoiced greatly.


###### I Chronicles 29:10

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/I%20Chronicles/r/29:10](https://torahapp.org/share/book/I%20Chronicles/r/29:10)


וַיְבָ֤רֶךְ דָּוִיד֙ אֶת־יְהֹוָ֔ה לְעֵינֵ֖י כׇּל־הַקָּהָ֑ל וַיֹּ֣אמֶר דָּוִ֗יד בָּר֨וּךְ אַתָּ֤ה יְהֹוָה֙ אֱלֹהֵי֙ יִשְׂרָאֵ֣ל אָבִ֔ינוּ מֵעוֹלָ֖ם וְעַד־עוֹלָֽם׃


**The Koren Jerusalem Bible:**
Wherefore David blessed the Lord before all the congregation: and David said, Blessed be Thou, Lord God of Yisra᾽el our father, forever and ever.


**The Rashi Ketuvim by Rabbi Shraga Silverstein:**
And David blessed the L-rd before the eyes of the entire congregation, and David said: Blessed are You, O L-rd, G-d of Israel, our Father, forever and ever.


###### I Chronicles 29:11

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/I%20Chronicles/r/29:11](https://torahapp.org/share/book/I%20Chronicles/r/29:11)


לְךָ֣ יְ֠הֹוָ֠ה הַגְּדֻלָּ֨ה וְהַגְּבוּרָ֤ה וְהַתִּפְאֶ֙רֶת֙ וְהַנֵּ֣צַח וְהַה֔וֹד כִּי־כֹ֖ל בַּשָּׁמַ֣יִם וּבָאָ֑רֶץ לְךָ֤ יְהֹוָה֙ הַמַּמְלָכָ֔ה וְהַמִּתְנַשֵּׂ֖א לְכֹ֥ל׀לְרֹֽאשׁ׃


**The Koren Jerusalem Bible:**
Thine, O Lord, is the greatness, and the power, and the glory, and the victory, and the majesty: for all that is in heaven and on earth is Thine; Thine is the kingdom, O Lord, and Thou art exalted as head above all.


**The Rashi Ketuvim by Rabbi Shraga Silverstein:**
Yours, O L-rd, is the greatness and the strength and the glory and the triumph and the majesty. For all in heaven and earth (is Yours). Yours, O L-rd, is the kingdom and the exaltation over every head.


###### I Chronicles 29:12

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/I%20Chronicles/r/29:12](https://torahapp.org/share/book/I%20Chronicles/r/29:12)


וְהָעֹ֤שֶׁר וְהַכָּבוֹד֙ מִלְּפָנֶ֔יךָ וְאַתָּה֙ מוֹשֵׁ֣ל בַּכֹּ֔ל וּבְיָדְךָ֖ כֹּ֣חַ וּגְבוּרָ֑ה וּבְיָ֣דְךָ֔ לְגַדֵּ֥ל וּלְחַזֵּ֖ק לַכֹּֽל׃


**The Koren Jerusalem Bible:**
Both riches and honour come of Thee, and Thou reignest over all; and in Thy hand is power and might; and in Thy hand it is to make great, and to give strength to all.


**The Rashi Ketuvim by Rabbi Shraga Silverstein:**
And wealth and honor (come) from before You. And You rule over all. And in Your hand is power and strength. And in Your hand (is the power) to exalt and strengthen all.


###### I Chronicles 29:13

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/I%20Chronicles/r/29:13](https://torahapp.org/share/book/I%20Chronicles/r/29:13)


וְעַתָּ֣ה אֱלֹהֵ֔ינוּ מוֹדִ֥ים אֲנַ֖חְנוּ לָ֑ךְ וּֽמְהַלְלִ֖ים לְשֵׁ֥ם תִּפְאַרְתֶּֽךָ׃


**The Koren Jerusalem Bible:**
Now therefore, our God, we thank Thee, and praise Thy glorious name.


**The Rashi Ketuvim by Rabbi Shraga Silverstein:**
And now, our G-d, we thank You, and we praise the name of Your glory.


###### I Chronicles 29:14

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/I%20Chronicles/r/29:14](https://torahapp.org/share/book/I%20Chronicles/r/29:14)


וְכִ֨י מִ֤י אֲנִי֙ וּמִ֣י עַמִּ֔י כִּי־נַעְצֹ֣ר כֹּ֔חַ לְהִתְנַדֵּ֖ב כָּזֹ֑את כִּי־מִמְּךָ֣ הַכֹּ֔ל וּמִיָּדְךָ֖ נָתַ֥נּוּ לָֽךְ׃


**The Koren Jerusalem Bible:**
But who am I, and what is my people, that we should be able thus to offer willingly after this sort? for all things come of Thee, and of Thy own have we given Thee.


**The Rashi Ketuvim by Rabbi Shraga Silverstein:**
For who am I and who are my people that we summon strength to give like this? For all is from You, and from Your hand have we given to You.


###### I Chronicles 29:15

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/I%20Chronicles/r/29:15](https://torahapp.org/share/book/I%20Chronicles/r/29:15)


כִּֽי־גֵרִ֨ים אֲנַ֧חְנוּ לְפָנֶ֛יךָ וְתוֹשָׁבִ֖ים כְּכׇל־אֲבֹתֵ֑ינוּ כַּצֵּ֧ל׀יָמֵ֛ינוּ עַל־הָאָ֖רֶץ וְאֵ֥ין מִקְוֶֽה׃


**The Koren Jerusalem Bible:**
For we are strangers before Thee, and sojourners, as were all our fathers: our days on the earth are as a shadow, and there is no abiding.


**The Rashi Ketuvim by Rabbi Shraga Silverstein:**
For strangers are we before You, and sojourners like all of our fathers. Like a shadow are our days upon the earth, and there is no hope [(against death)].


###### I Chronicles 29:16

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/I%20Chronicles/r/29:16](https://torahapp.org/share/book/I%20Chronicles/r/29:16)


יְהֹוָ֣ה אֱלֹהֵ֔ינוּ כֹּ֣ל הֶהָמ֤וֹן הַזֶּה֙ אֲשֶׁ֣ר הֲכִינֹ֔נוּ לִבְנֽוֹת־לְךָ֥ בַ֖יִת לְשֵׁ֣ם קׇדְשֶׁ֑ךָ מִיָּדְךָ֥ (היא) [ה֖וּא] וּלְךָ֥ הַכֹּֽל׃


**The Koren Jerusalem Bible:**
O Lord our God, all this store that we have prepared, to build Thee a house for Thy holy name comes of Thy hand, and is all Thy own.


**The Rashi Ketuvim by Rabbi Shraga Silverstein:**
O L-rd our G-d, all this store which we have brought forward to build to You a house for Your holy name — it is from Your hand, and all is Yours.


###### I Chronicles 29:17

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/I%20Chronicles/r/29:17](https://torahapp.org/share/book/I%20Chronicles/r/29:17)


וְיָדַ֣עְתִּי אֱלֹהַ֔י כִּ֤י אַתָּה֙ בֹּחֵ֣ן לֵבָ֔ב וּמֵישָׁרִ֖ים תִּרְצֶ֑ה אֲנִ֗י בְּיֹ֤שֶׁר לְבָבִי֙ הִתְנַדַּ֣בְתִּי כׇל־אֵ֔לֶּה וְעַתָּ֗ה עַמְּךָ֙ הַנִּמְצְאוּ־פֹ֔ה רָאִ֥יתִי בְשִׂמְחָ֖ה לְהִֽתְנַדֶּב־לָֽךְ׃


**The Koren Jerusalem Bible:**
I know also, my God, that Thou triest the heart, and hast pleasure in uprightness. As for me, in the uprightness of my heart I have willingly offered all these things: and now I have seen Thy people, who are present here, offering joyfully and freely to Thee.


**The Rashi Ketuvim by Rabbi Shraga Silverstein:**
And I know, my G-d, that You sound the heart and desire what is just. I, in the justness of my heart, have donated all these (things). And now, Your people who are found here, I have seen in joy donating to You.


###### I Chronicles 29:18

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/I%20Chronicles/r/29:18](https://torahapp.org/share/book/I%20Chronicles/r/29:18)


יְהֹוָ֗ה אֱ֠לֹהֵ֠י אַבְרָהָ֞ם יִצְחָ֤ק וְיִשְׂרָאֵל֙ אֲבֹתֵ֔ינוּ שׇׁמְרָה־זֹּ֣את לְעוֹלָ֔ם לְיֵ֥צֶר מַחְשְׁב֖וֹת לְבַ֣ב עַמֶּ֑ךָ וְהָכֵ֥ן לְבָבָ֖ם אֵלֶֽיךָ׃


**The Koren Jerusalem Bible:**
O Lord God of Avraham, Yiżĥaq and of Yisra᾽el, our fathers, keep this forever in the imagination of the thoughts of the heart of Thy people, and direct their hearts to Thee:


**The Rashi Ketuvim by Rabbi Shraga Silverstein:**
O L-rd, G-d of Abraham, Isaac, and Israel, our fathers, preserve this forever, the inclination of the thoughts of the hearts of Your people, and ready their hearts unto You.


###### I Chronicles 29:19

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/I%20Chronicles/r/29:19](https://torahapp.org/share/book/I%20Chronicles/r/29:19)


וְלִשְׁלֹמֹ֣ה בְנִ֗י תֵּ֚ן לֵבָ֣ב שָׁלֵ֔ם לִשְׁמוֹר֙ מִצְוֺתֶ֔יךָ עֵדְוֺתֶ֖יךָ וְחֻקֶּ֑יךָ וְלַעֲשׂ֣וֹת הַכֹּ֔ל וְלִבְנ֖וֹת הַבִּירָ֥ה אֲשֶׁר־הֲכִינֽוֹתִי׃{פ}


**The Koren Jerusalem Bible:**
And give to Shelomo my son a perfect heart, to keep Thy commandments, Thy testimonies, and Thy statutes, and to do all these things, and to build the palace, for which I have made provision.


**The Rashi Ketuvim by Rabbi Shraga Silverstein:**
And to Solomon my son give a whole heart to keep Your mitzvoth, Your testimonies, and Your statutes, and to do all (that is needed), and to build the palace [i.e., the Temple] that I have readied.


###### I Chronicles 29:20

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/I%20Chronicles/r/29:20](https://torahapp.org/share/book/I%20Chronicles/r/29:20)


וַיֹּ֤אמֶר דָּוִיד֙ לְכׇל־הַקָּהָ֔ל בָּ֥רְכוּ נָ֖א אֶת־יְהֹוָ֣ה אֱלֹֽהֵיכֶ֑ם וַיְבָרְכ֣וּ כׇֽל־הַקָּהָ֗ל לַֽיהֹוָה֙ אֱלֹהֵ֣י אֲבֹתֵיהֶ֔ם וַיִּקְּד֧וּ וַיִּֽשְׁתַּחֲו֛וּ לַיהֹוָ֖ה וְלַמֶּֽלֶךְ׃


**The Koren Jerusalem Bible:**
And David said to all the congregation, Now bless the Lord your God. And all the congregation blessed the Lord God of their fathers, and bowed down their heads, and prostrated themselves before the Lord, and the king.


**The Rashi Ketuvim by Rabbi Shraga Silverstein:**
And David said to all the congregation: Bless the L-rd your G-d. And all the congregation blessed the L-rd, the G-d of their fathers. And they knelt and prostrated themselves to the L-rd and to the king.


###### I Chronicles 29:21

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/I%20Chronicles/r/29:21](https://torahapp.org/share/book/I%20Chronicles/r/29:21)


וַיִּזְבְּח֣וּ לַיהֹוָ֣ה׀זְ֠בָחִ֠ים וַיַּעֲל֨וּ עֹל֜וֹת לַיהֹוָ֗ה לְֽמׇחֳרַת֮ הַיּ֣וֹם הַהוּא֒ פָּרִ֨ים אֶ֜לֶף אֵלִ֥ים אֶ֛לֶף כְּבָשִׂ֥ים אֶ֖לֶף וְנִסְכֵּיהֶ֑ם וּזְבָחִ֥ים לָרֹ֖ב לְכׇל־יִשְׂרָאֵֽל׃


**The Koren Jerusalem Bible:**
And they sacrificed sacrifices to the Lord, and offered burnt offerings to the Lord, on the morrow after that day, a thousand bullocks, a thousand rams, a thousand lambs, with their drink offerings, and sacrifices in abundance for all Yisra᾽el:


**The Rashi Ketuvim by Rabbi Shraga Silverstein:**
And they sacrificed peace-offerings to the L-rd, and they offered up burnt-offerings to the L-rd on the morrow of that day: a thousand bulls, a thousand rams, a thousand lambs, and their libations. And peace-offerings in abundance (were offered) by all of Israel.


###### I Chronicles 29:22

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/I%20Chronicles/r/29:22](https://torahapp.org/share/book/I%20Chronicles/r/29:22)


וַיֹּאכְל֨וּ וַיִּשְׁתּ֜וּ לִפְנֵ֧י יְהֹוָ֛ה בַּיּ֥וֹם הַה֖וּא בְּשִׂמְחָ֣ה גְדוֹלָ֑ה וַיַּמְלִ֤יכוּ שֵׁנִית֙ לִשְׁלֹמֹ֣ה בֶן־דָּוִ֔יד וַיִּמְשְׁח֧וּ לַיהֹוָ֛ה לְנָגִ֖יד וּלְצָד֥וֹק לְכֹהֵֽן׃


**The Koren Jerusalem Bible:**
and they did eat and drink before the Lord on that day with great gladness. And they made Shelomo the son of David king the second time, and anointed him to the Lord to be the chief governor, and Żadoq to be priest.


**The Rashi Ketuvim by Rabbi Shraga Silverstein:**
And they ate and they drank before the L-rd on that day with great joy. And they crowned Solomon the son of David as king a second time [viz. I Kings 1:39], and they anointed him to the L-rd as leader, and (they anointed) Tzaddok as [high-] priest.

**The Rashi Ketuvim by Rabbi Shraga Silverstein:**

**The Rashi Ketuvim by Rabbi Shraga Silverstein:**

**The Rashi Ketuvim by Rabbi Shraga Silverstein:**
And the events of (the reign of) King David, the earlier and the later, are recorded in the words of Samuel the seer and in the words of Nathan the prophet and in the words of Gad the seer,

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/I%20Chronicles/r/29:30](https://torahapp.org/share/book/I%20Chronicles/r/29:30)


עִ֥ם כׇּל־מַלְכוּת֖וֹ וּגְבוּרָת֑וֹ וְהָעִתִּ֗ים אֲשֶׁ֨ר עָבְר֤וּ עָלָיו֙ וְעַל־יִשְׂרָאֵ֔ל וְעַ֖ל כׇּל־מַמְלְכ֥וֹת הָאֲרָצֽוֹת׃{פ}

with all his reign and his might, and the times that went over him, and over Yisra᾽el, and over all the kingdoms of the countries.


**The Rashi Ketuvim by Rabbi Shraga Silverstein:**
with (an account of) all his kingdom and his might and of the times that passed over him and over Israel and over all the kingdoms of the lands.
